# LooneyBin — Player Wiki

The complete field guide to **LooneyBin** (Arma 3 Altis Life). Every player-facing system on the island, with step-by-step how-tos, exact prices, payouts, requirements, and timers — pulled straight from the live server code (34 systems documented).

**Using this wiki:** browse the Contents below, or search (Ctrl+F) for what you want to do.

**For the Ben Bones bot:** this file is the ground-truth knowledge base. Answer player questions from these sections only; if it's not here, say you're not sure rather than guessing.

## Getting Started & Civilian Basics

Welcome to the LoonyBin. This section covers everything a brand-new civilian needs: how you spawn, how the onboarding works, the difference between the cash in your pocket and the money in your bank, your phone, and the licenses that unlock the rest of the island. Everything here is confirmed from the live mission.

---

### First login: The Intake

**What it is:** A one-time cinematic onboarding that every brand-new account plays instead of the normal spawn menu. You wake up committed inside the Loony Bin (the asylum), learn the controls, sit through a short interview, and get released into town.

**How it flows:**
1. A loading/welcome screen appears. Click **Play** when it finishes to enter the world.
2. Instead of a spawn menu, a cinematic flies over the asylum with the title "THE LOONY BIN – PATIENT INTAKE, DAY 1." You can press any key to skip the fly-in.
3. You wake up on a cot in a padded ward. Follow the on-screen prompts: **take a few steps (W A S D)**, then **look around (move the mouse)**.
4. **Use the scroll wheel on the Doctor** (the man in the ward) and pick the cyan **Talk** option.
5. The Doctor asks **3 interview questions**. Pick any answer to each — your answers secretly set your "starting path" (worker / criminal / do-gooder) that flavors later hints. There are no wrong answers.
6. The screen fades with "THREE WEEKS LATER" and you wake up in **Athira**, released on parole, with a stipend deposited to your bank.

**Reward:** A release stipend of **$2,500** is deposited straight to your **bank account** when you finish the interview.

**Skip option:** A scroll-wheel action **"Skip the intake (I've been here before)"** is available the entire time — useful for alternate accounts. Skipping pays **no stipend**.

**Gotchas / tips:**
- The Intake only runs for genuinely new accounts. Existing/veteran players never see it — they go straight to the normal spawn menu.
- If you disconnect in the middle of the Intake, it simply runs again from the start next login (you haven't "used it up").
- You are invulnerable during the Intake, so you can't die in the ward.

---

### Parole Papers (your first quest chain)

**What it is:** A fixed **6-step tutorial contract** that starts right after the Intake releases you into Athira. It walks you through the core money loop and pays escalating rewards. It shows up as a task on your objective HUD plus a map marker for each step, and it resumes from where you left off if you relog.

**The 6 steps and their rewards (paid to your bank as you complete each):**

| # | Task | Where | Reward |
|---|------|-------|--------|
| 1 | Buy a **Driver's License** | Athira DMV | **$2,500** |
| 2 | Buy **any vehicle** (a quadbike is fine) | Athira car dealer | **$2,500** |
| 3 | Pick **15 peaches** (scroll-wheel in the field) | Peach orchard NW of Athira (~1.3 km) | **$5,000** |
| 4 | Sell at least **10 peaches** | Athira market NPC | **$7,500** |
| 5 | **Learn a talent** — open the phone (Y) → Talents and spend any point | anywhere | **$5,000** |
| 6 | **Report to the Parole Officer** near Athira square | Athira square | **$15,000** |

**Total from Parole Papers: $37,500.** Combined with the $2,500 Intake stipend and your $2,500 starting bank, a fresh civilian who finishes onboarding walks away with roughly **$42,500**, almost all in the bank.

**Tips:**
- Each step's map marker shows exactly where to go — check your map (M).
- Rewards only pay once per step; you can't farm them by replaying.
- If you get lost, the Parole Officer notes that side chat is full of players who help — just ask.

---

### The Mentor Program

If you're a new player, an experienced player can **offer to mentor you** (a scroll action they get on you while you still count as "new blood"). Once linked, when **you finish your Parole Papers**, your mentor is paid **$1,000,000**. You lose nothing — it's a bonus paid to them for helping you. Only one mentor per new player.

---

### The message tutorial

Separately from the Intake, brand-new players (under ~3 hours played) get a series of pop-up **info messages** that explain the rules and systems, spaced roughly 2 minutes apart. If you're between 1 and 3 hours in, the game asks if you'd like to run it. It covers: Discord link, the RDM and VDM rules, useful keybinds, the quest/talent systems, whitelisted factions, housing, gangs & rebel life, the bounty hunter license, and a money-making overview. You can always revisit tutorials later from the **Tutorials tab in your phone (Y)**.

**Useful chat commands mentioned in onboarding:**
- `!stats` — see your leaderboard standing in-game.
- `!loginbonus` — claim your daily consecutive-login bonus when the game tells you one is available.

---

### Choosing your role: Civilian, Police (MaxSec), or Medic (TT)

**Everyone starts as a Civilian.** That's the open, no-application faction and is what this section is about.

The other two factions are **whitelisted** — you must apply and be approved before you can play them:
- **MaxSec ("cop")** — law enforcement.
- **TT (Trauma Team / medics)** — revive downed players and support roles.

You **apply to become an officer or medic via the community forums/Discord** (linked on the Escape menu and in-game). Once you're whitelisted, you can select that faction's slots at the lobby; until then those slots aren't available to you. There is also a teased **"4th Faction (Coming Soon)"** that is not playable yet — picking one of those slots just shows a teaser and bounces you back.

For a new player: **stay a civilian.** Cops and medics are covered in their own wiki sections.

---

### Spawning & the spawn menu

After onboarding (and every time you die or reconnect while dead/at a fresh session), you get the **Spawn Selection** menu with a map and a list of spawn points. Pick one and click to spawn.

**Civilian spawn towns** (the number in brackets is how many players are currently near that town):
- **Kavala**
- **Athira**
- **Pyrgos**
- **Sofia**
- **Therisa**

**Extra civilian spawn points that appear when you qualify:**
- **Your own house** — any house you own that isn't in an active event zone or tax-locked.
- **Gang Building** — your gang's HQ, if your gang owns one (any member can spawn here even if the owner is offline).
- **Turf spawns** (Rodopoli, Zaros, Panagia) — if your gang controls that turf and it isn't contested.
- **Gang Fortress / Gang Boat** — during the relevant events, for the controlling gang.
- **Rebel Outpost 1 / 2** — open to anyone during an active Gang Fortress event so you can join the fight.
- **Governor Office** — only if you are the current Governor.

**Gotchas:**
- House and gang spawns are hidden while a turf war, cartel/warzone, fortress, or jewelry-heist event is active near them.
- If you click Spawn and get "Error. Please wait a bit or try a different spawn point," just pick another spawn — the location wasn't ready.

**Redeploy:** From the spawn menu you can **redeploy to a different spawn while alive**, but:
- It's limited to **once every 5 minutes**.
- You must **stand still and unharmed** during a ~15-second countdown. Taking damage, entering a vehicle, or moving more than ~10 m **cancels** the redeploy.

---

### Cash on hand vs. your Bank account

This is the single most important thing for a new player to understand. You have **two separate pools of money**:

- **Cash on hand** — the physical money in your pockets. **This is what gets stolen if you're robbed and dropped/lost when you die.** You start with **$0** cash.
- **Bank account** — money kept safely at the bank. **It is not lost on death and cannot be robbed off your body.** A new civilian starts with **$2,500** in the bank (plus onboarding rewards on top).

**Rule of thumb: keep your money in the bank. Only carry the cash you need right now.**

**New Drachma** is a third, rebel-only currency (shown as "ND") used at rebel shops — the server's exact digital coin. You won't have any as a new player.

#### ATMs

**Where:** Walk up to any physical **ATM** (the wall/standing bank machines in towns) and use the scroll wheel within ~3 m to pick the green **ATM** option.

**What it does:** Move money between your **cash on hand** and your **bank account** (deposit / withdraw), and transfer money to other players' bank accounts. If you're in a gang, the same ATM also offers a **Gang Account** option.

#### The Debit Card (spend your bank money anywhere)

**What it is:** A virtual item that lets you **pay for things directly from your bank account without standing at an ATM**. When you buy something and don't have enough cash on hand, the game automatically pulls from your bank **if you're carrying a Debit Card**.

**Cost:** about **$267** as a virtual item (bought at a general store/market) *(verify exact shop)*.

**How paying works in practice** (e.g., buying a license or shop item):
1. If you have enough **cash on hand**, it's taken from cash first ("You paid $X from cash on hand").
2. If not, and you **own a Debit Card**, it's taken from your **bank** ("You paid $X from your bank account using a debit card").
3. If you have neither enough cash nor a Debit Card, the purchase fails.

**Tip:** Buy a Debit Card early. It saves constant ATM trips, since most of your money should live in the bank.

---

### Paychecks

You automatically receive a **paycheck deposited to your bank** at a fixed interval just for being online and alive.

- **Interval:** every **5 minutes**.
- **Civilian paycheck:** **$350** each time.
- **Important:** if you are **dead** when a paycheck fires, you **miss** it ("You have missed a paycheck because you were dead").
- A system-chat message tells you how many minutes until your next paycheck and confirms each deposit.

---

### The Phone (press **Y**)

**Where/how:** Press **Y** at any time (while alive and with free hands) to open your smartphone. You **can't** open it while your hands are restrained.

The phone is your hub for almost every menu. Core apps a civilian will use:

- **Money** — shows your **Bank**, **Cash on hand**, **New Drachma**, the current **Governor tax rate**, and your **Prestige**; also lets you **hand cash on hand** to a nearby player.
- **Message** — send text messages to other players (also how you legally *initiate* roleplay).
- **Phone** — call another player.
- **Inventory** — view your **virtual inventory** (also opened directly with **T**).
- **Sync** — push your current character data to the server (a manual save).
- **Licenses** — view the licenses you currently own.
- **Market** — check live sell prices for sellable items.
- **Talents** — spend talent points.
- **Quests** — view available quests.
- **Keychain** — view and trade your vehicle keys.
- **Group** — create or join a temporary group.
- **My Gang** — create, view, or manage a gang.
- **Settings** — game and system preferences (including your earplug volume).
- **Wallpaper** — change your phone's wallpaper.
- **Achieve / Titles** — achievements and unlockable name titles.
- **Casino** — play blackjack.
- **Browser** — browse the in-game "LoonyNet."
- **Summon Vehicle** — recall your starred vehicle; it drives or flies itself to you.

(Some apps only appear when relevant — e.g., **Housing** shows up once you own a house, **Infamy/Honor** once you hold a rebel or bounty license, and cop/medic apps only on those factions.)

**Favorite apps:** You can pin **up to 4 apps as favorites** so they sit front-and-center on your phone. Set them from the favorites screen — you'll be prompted to "Select up to 4 Apps to favorite."

---

### Licenses & the DMV

**What they are:** Licenses are permanent permits that unlock activities — driving, flying, owning a home, carrying legal guns, and processing raw materials into sellable goods.

**Where to buy most of them:** the **DMV** (also called the Driver's License / civil-services NPC) in town. Athira's DMV is the one the Parole quest sends you to. Scroll-wheel the DMV NPC and pick the license you want; it's paid from cash or, with a Debit Card, from your bank.

**DMV license list and exact prices:**

| License | Cost | What it unlocks |
|---|---|---|
| Driver's License | **$100** | Drive land vehicles |
| Boating License | **$150** | Operate boats |
| Diving License | **$200** | Scuba diving |
| Firearm License | **$250** | Buy/carry legal firearms |
| Copper Processing | **$600** | Process copper ore |
| Sand Processing | **$700** | Process sand |
| Salt Processing | **$900** | Process salt |
| Iron Processing | **$950** | Process iron ore |
| Home Owner License | **$3,500** | Buy houses (also sold at Home Improvement stores) |
| Diamond Processing | **$3,500** | Process uncut diamonds |
| Pilot License | **$4,000** | Fly helicopters/planes |
| Truck License | **$4,000** | Drive large trucks |

**Licenses bought elsewhere (not at the DMV):**
- **Marijuana Processing Contract** — **$800** (at the weed processing location)
- **Oil Drilling and Processing** — **$1,000** (at the oil site)
- **Rebel License** — **$12,500** (rebel outpost) — unlocks rebel weapons/gear and illegal activity
- **Bounty Hunter License** — **$12,500** (bounty office) — lets you hunt wanted players for money

**Important license gotchas:**
- **Rebel and Bounty Hunter licenses are mutually exclusive.** Buying one **automatically removes** the other. Choose your path.
- If **MaxSec revokes your Bounty Hunter license**, you **cannot rebuy it until the next server restart**.
- A firearm-heavy weapon like the MX rifle also requires the **Bounty Hunter License plus the Master Bounty Hunter talent** — without both, the game strips the weapon from you.
- The Licenses phone app only **displays** what you own; you must visit the DMV/relevant NPC to actually **buy**.

---

### Essential keybinds & starter tips

- **Y** — open your phone.
- **T** — open the virtual inventory (look at a vehicle/crate and press T to access its storage).
- **U** — lock/unlock vehicles and doors you have keys to.
- **M** — map (with a route/search HUD; every civilian is auto-given a GPS so this works).
- **H** — holster your weapon; press **1** or **2** to bring it back out.
- **Tab** — raise your hands (comply when told to by cops, robbers, or bounty hunters).
- **Double Left-Ctrl** — lower/raise your weapon so you're not pointing at people.
- **Caps Lock** — voice chat (make sure you're in the right channel or nobody hears you).
- **Scroll wheel + Space** — the main way you interact with ATMs, NPCs, gathering spots, players, and medical actions.
- **Custom Action 12** — insert earplugs (set the lowered volume first in phone → Settings).
- **Escape** — find the server links and Discord.

**General new-player advice:**
- Do the **Parole Papers** chain first — it's ~$42,500 of easy starting money and teaches the core loop.
- **Bank your money** and carry a **Debit Card**; only what's in your pocket can be robbed or lost on death.
- Legal money runs (apples, peaches, hydrogen, mining, processing ore) are safe. Illegal drug runs pay far more but are dangerous and cops will chase you.
- Your **worn gear and inventory save automatically** roughly every 4 minutes and on changes, so an unexpected disconnect loses at most a few minutes of progress.

---

## The Phone (apps, calls, texts, transfers)

Your phone is your pocket computer: it holds all your menu "apps," lets you text and voice-call other players, hand cash and items to people standing next to you, browse the island's in-game internet (LoonyNet), summon a starred vehicle to your position, and place bets. Everything below is done from the phone unless noted.

### Opening the phone & the home screen

- **Press `Y`** to open the phone **home screen** (the app grid). You must be **alive**, and you **cannot** open it while your **hands are restrained** (you'll get "You cannot access your phone because your hands are restrained").
- The home screen fades in a grid of app icons. The **bottom row** is always the four core apps: **Sync**, **Money**, **Message**, **Inventory**. The rest of the grid is filled with apps you're allowed to use (some are civ-only, cop-only, or Trauma Team-only — see the app list below).
- Tapping an app icon opens that app.

### Favorite apps (pin up to 4)

- The **Wallpaper**/favorites screen lets you **pin up to 4 apps** as favorites so they sit at the top of your phone. You'll see the prompt "Select up to 4 Apps to favorite!"
- Tap an app to favorite it. If you already have 4, you'll get "You cannot favorite more than 4 phone Applications at once!" — remove one first.
- Your favorites are saved to your profile and persist between sessions.

### The full app list

Which apps you see depends on your side and unlocks. The apps that exist:

- **Sync** — pushes your current character data to the server (manual save).
- **Money** — view bank / cash / New Drachma / governor tax rate / prestige, and **give cash** to a nearby player (see Transfers).
- **Message** — send **text messages** (see Texting).
- **Inventory** — view your virtual inventory and **give items** to a nearby player (see Transfers).
- **Phone** — **voice-call** another player (see Calls).
- **Browser** — open the **LoonyNet** web browser (see Browser).
- **Summon Vehicle** — civilian; recall your starred Garage vehicle to your position. *(Covered under the transport/vehicle wiki.)*
- **Casino** — play blackjack. *(Covered under the casino wiki.)*
- **Group** — civ; join or create a temporary squad/group.
- **My Gang** — civ; create/view/manage your gang.
- **Perks** — civ, gang members; manage gang perks.
- **Talents** — everyone; assign standard talent points.
- **Infamy** — civ with the Rebel license; assign infamy points.
- **Captures** — civ/cop; live contents of capture containers.
- **Keychain** — everyone; view and trade your vehicle keys.
- **Licenses** — everyone; view the licenses you currently hold (see below).
- **Settings** — everyone; game/system preferences including phone ringer and ignore-calls (see Settings).
- **Wallpaper** — everyone; change your phone wallpaper.
- **Wanted** — cop; index of criminals and warrants.
- **Gangs Online** — cop; roster of civ gangs currently online.
- **Bounty** — civ with Bounty license + the bounty talent; request a new bounty.
- **Honor** — civ bounty hunters; assign bounty-hunter specialty points.
- **Prestige** — cop (rank above Officer); assign cop prestige points.
- **TT Prestige** — Trauma Team (rank 3+); assign TT prestige points.
- **Achieve** — everyone; view achievements.
- **Titles** — everyone; view/unlock titles.
- **Market** — civ/cop; current sell prices of sellable items.
- **Housing** — civ house owners; manage house taxes.
- **Quests** — everyone; view available quests.

> Tip: the **Licenses** app just *lists* the licenses you already own for your side; it is not where you buy them. You buy licenses at the relevant town shops/NPCs.

### Texting / Messaging

**Open it:** the **Message** app, or the shortcut **press `5`** to jump straight to texts.

**How to text a player:**
1. Open Message. The list shows every **living player** on the server (the headless "HC1" bot is hidden).
2. Select a name, type your message in the box, and hit the send button.
3. There are also fixed one-tap buttons to text **The Police**, **Emergency Medical**, and **The Admins**, plus a **Message History** button to review past texts.

**Limits & rules:**
- Player, Police, Medic, and Admin texts are capped at **200 characters**.
- Your text **cannot contain HTML-style tags** (`<br/>`, `<t`, `<img`, `<a`) — those are blocked and the send is rejected.
- If you are a **prison inmate** (arrested and on the west/police side of the jail), you **cannot text the police**. You'll get "Prison inmates are not allowed to message officers of the law!"
- The old **taxi request** text button is **disabled** — use **Summon Vehicle** instead.

**Ben Bones Official (the AI you can text):** the contact **"Ben Bones Official"** is pinned at the top of your text list in gold. Texting it doesn't message a player — it DMs the island's AI conspiracy host. You'll see "You texted Ben Bones Official. He's in the bunker — give him a minute to reply," and a reply comes back to your phone shortly after.

### Voice calls

**Open it:** the **Phone** app on the home screen.

**How to call someone:**
1. Open Phone. The status line shows your current state: **Idle / Ringing / Active Call / Call Declined**.
2. Select a living player from the list and tap **Call**.
3. It rings for up to **30 seconds** while a dial tone plays. If they don't pick up in time, the call auto-cancels ("Your phone call wasn't answered in time").
4. When connected you'll see **"Phone link established!"** and a live voice channel opens. Tap **Hang Up** (confirm Yes) to end it; you'll get "Your phone call has ended."

**Receiving a call:** you'll see **"[Name] is calling your phone! Press 8 to answer or 9 to decline."**
- **Press `8` to answer**, **press `9` to decline**.
- If you ignore it, after ~30s it's marked missed.

**Rules & gotchas:**
- **Prison inmates cannot call the police** ("Prison inmates are not allowed to call officers of the law!").
- You **can't call a player who is at/near the police spawn** (within ~1000 m of west respawn) — they show as "currently not available."
- If too many calls are active server-wide you'll get "Phone lines are currently at maximum capacity, try again later!"
- You can silence the ringer or block all incoming calls in **Settings** (see below).

### Remote detonators (special call-list "contacts")

If you have **primed a remote explosive on a vehicle**, a special contact **"@Remote Bomb Detonator"** appears at the top of your **Phone (call)** list. Selecting it and pressing **Call** **detonates the charge**. Likewise, if you've set a **remote engine kill switch**, **"@Remote Engine Kill Switch Detonator"** appears — calling it kills that vehicle's engine. These only show up while you actually have a device armed.

### Transfers — giving cash

**Open it:** the **Money** app.

**How to give cash:**
1. Open Money. It shows your **bank**, **cash on hand**, **New Drachma** (civ), governor tax rate, and prestige.
2. The player list shows people **within ~10 m** of you (or your vehicle's crew). Select one.
3. Type the amount and tap **Give Money**.

**Rules & exact limits:**
- Money is given from your **cash on hand**, not your bank.
- **Maximum $999,999 per transfer** ("You can't give more than $999,999 at a time.").
- You must be **within 20 m** of the person or you'll get "The selected player is too far away!"
- **Blocked if you recently robbed a store** ("You recently robbed a store! You can't give money away just yet.").
- **Blocked while a gang-bank withdrawal is settling** ("You can't give money while having an active gang bank withdrawal. Try again in a couple seconds.").
- **Blocked toward someone you just arrested** — you can't hand a suspect back the money you earned arresting them.

### Transfers — giving items

**Open it:** the **Inventory** app, then use its give function (this runs the give-item flow).

**How to give an item:**
1. Open Inventory. Select the **item** and the **recipient**, and enter a **quantity**.
2. Tap give. If you enter more than you own (or more than the item's max), it's clamped/rejected.

**Rules & gotchas:**
- You must have a **clear line of sight** — you can't pass items through a wall ("It would be hard to pass things through the wall, don't you think?").
- You **can't give items while processing** materials.
- You **can't give while your hands are restrained**.
- Enter a **positive whole number**; zero or garbage is rejected.

### The Browser (LoonyNet)

**Open it:** the **Browser** app. It's a full retro web browser, **purely cosmetic/informational plus a few interactive stores** — you type an address in the top bar or tap a bookmark. Use the **Back** button to step back through history. The home page is **LoonGle** (loonynet.start), a Web-1.0 portal with clickable bookmarks.

The sites you can visit:

- **looner.com** — the island microblog (see Looner).
- **databattle.com** — Ben Bones' conspiracy news (see DataBattle).
- **loonybets.com** — the sportsbook (see LoonyBets).
- **loonstreets.com** — the leaderboards (see Loonstreets).
- **3dprintfreedom.com** — an intentional **404 stub** ("under construction"), nothing there yet.
- Anything else → a "This site can't be reached / DNS_PROBE_FINISHED_NO_LOONYNET" error page.

### Looner (the microblog)

On **looner.com** a **compose bar** appears at the bottom of the page. It's a **server-wide public feed** — everyone sees every post.

- **Post:** type your message (**max 180 characters** — a live counter shows what's left) and send. Your post appears in the feed tagged with your name and a timestamp (now / 12m / 3h / 2d).
- **Boost a post:** tap the **heart / "boost"** link under any loon, or type **`like <number>`** in the compose bar to boost that post by its `#id`.
- Some posts show a gold **verified checkmark** (cosmetic).

### LoonyBets (the sportsbook)

On **loonybets.com** you can bet real money on **active island heists**. It's **parimutuel**: all winners split the losing side's pool minus a **10% house rake**.

- **The book only opens when a heist alarm is live.** Markets cover: **Bank, Jewelry, Fed Reserve, Evidence, Shipping, Loony Bin**. When nothing's running you'll see "WINDOW SHUT."
- **Place a bet:** tap **YES** or **NO** on a market, then enter your stake.
  - **Minimum stake: $5,000. Maximum stake: $50,000.**
  - Stakes are paid from your **bank** (you need the funds there).
- You can also bet from the URL bar by typing: **`bet <market> <yes/no> <amount>`**.
- Each market **settles on its event's outcome**, or pays **NO** if it's still open at window close. Live odds (e.g. "pays 2.40x") update as people bet.

### Loonstreets (leaderboards)

On **loonstreets.com** — the island scoreboard, **refreshed every 5 minutes**:
- **Fortress Crown:** which gang currently holds the Gang Fortress.
- **Turf:** which gang controls each turf zone.
- **Top Crews:** gangs ranked by experience, with each gang's XP and banked cash (gold/silver/bronze for the top 3).

### DataBattle (news / lore)

On **databattle.com** — Ben Bones' satirical conspiracy tabloid. The front page also shows any **live BREAKING headlines** generated by real server events. The left rail has articles (The Great Reset, CLANKR Is Watching, Stimmy Checks, Red Gull Exposed, the Malden relocation, Lizard Police, Bug Invasion, etc.). It's flavor/lore — no gameplay actions — but the "BREAKING" ticker can tip you off to things actually happening on the map.

### Phone settings (ringer & ignore calls)

**Open it:** the **Settings** app.

- **Phone Ringer** — toggle the dial-tone/ring sound on or off.
- **Ignore incoming calls** — when on, you won't be rung or prompted for incoming calls at all.
- These (and your wallpaper, view-distance, and other preferences) are saved to your profile between sessions.

### Quick reference — keys & numbers

- **`Y`** — open phone home screen (must be alive & un-restrained).
- **`5`** — jump straight to the Message/text app.
- **`8` / `9`** — answer / decline an incoming call.
- **`0`** — open the online gang/group roster.
- **Call ring timeout:** 30 seconds.
- **Text length cap:** 200 characters (no HTML tags).
- **Looner post cap:** 180 characters.
- **Give cash:** max **$999,999** per transfer, target within **20 m**, from cash on hand.
- **LoonyBets stake:** **$5,000 min – $50,000 max**, from bank, 10% house rake.
- **Favorite apps:** up to **4** pinned.

---

## Legal Money: Gathering & Processing

Honest work on LooneyBin follows one pattern: **gather a raw resource, process it into something worth more, then sell it to the right trader.** No cops, no jail time — just a pickaxe, an axe, or a boat and a bit of patience. This section covers every legal gather-and-sell loop in the game with the exact tools, locations, and prices.

### How the loop works (read this first)

- **Gather → Process → Sell.** Most resources are worth little raw. You mine, cut, catch, or pick them, take them to a **processor NPC** (or a campfire/distillery), then sell the finished goods at the trader that buys them.
- **The market is dynamic.** Every price below is the *typical* value from the price list. The live market drifts prices **up** when a good is under-supplied and **down** when everyone is flooding it — so if a good stops paying well, switch products or wait for the price to recover.
- **Weight matters.** Everything you carry has a weight, and you have a carry cap. When your inventory is full, gathering stops with a "you can't hold more" message. Bring a vehicle with a trunk for big hauls.
- **Grid references** below are read straight off your in-game map (e.g. "grid ~081/144" = easting 081, northing 144). Traders and resource zones also show as icons on your map.
- **You must be a civilian** for nearly all of this. Cops and Trauma Team are locked out of most processing and civilian selling.

### Tools you'll need (and where to buy them)

Buy these once and keep them in your Y-inventory (they aren't consumed):

- **Pickaxe** — **$50**. Required to mine quarries and to drill unprocessed oil. Sold at the **Market**, **Commodities Trader**, **Oil Trader**, and **Jewelry Store**.
- **Wood Axe** — **$75**. Required to chop trees for timber. Sold at the **Market**, **Commodities Trader**, and **Hunting Item Shop**.
- **Skinning Knife** — **$150**. Required to skin dead animals for raw meat. Sold at the **Market**, **Rebel Market**, and **Hunting Item Shop**.
- **Sickle** — **$5,000**. Guarantees a bonus unit when harvesting grain (barley/corn/rye). Sold only at the **Hunting Item Shop**.
- **Metal Detector** — **$100**. Digs bonus loot at the quarries (see below). Sold at the **Market**.
- **Campfire Kit** — **$500**. Place and light it to cook raw meat and oysters. Sold at the **Market**, **Gas Station**, and **Hunting Item Shop**.
- **Yeast** — **$20**, and **Water Bottle** — **$7**. Ingredients for distilling spirits. Yeast sold at the **Market**, **Wong's Food & Liquor**, and **Hunting Item Shop**.
- A **boat** (or amphibious vehicle) for net fishing, and **Tents / camping gear** if you want portable storage while out gathering.

### Processing licenses (buy at the DMV)

Several processors are meant to require a one-time license, sold at the **DMV** (Department of Motor Vehicles NPC in town). Prices:

- **Copper Processing — $600**
- **Sand Processing — $700**
- **Salt Processing — $900**
- **Iron Processing — $950**
- **Diamond Processing — $3,500**
- **Oil Drilling & Processing — $1,000** (also buyable right at the oil processor)

> You need the relevant **processing license** for each material (mineral / salt / oil / weed / wax). Processing without it is not intended — if the game ever lets you skip a license, that's a bug, so buy the license anyway.

### Where to sell — market directory

Traders only buy certain goods. Take each product to the right one:

- **Market** (general store, in every major city — Kavala ~036/131, Athira ~140/188, Pyrgos ~168/126, Sofia ~258/214, and one more south-central ~107/122): buys **raw & cooked meat, apples, peaches, wild berries, all timber, phosphorus, hydrogen sulfate, and fish.**
- **Fish Market** (Kavala ~034/129): buys **all fish** (salema, ornate, mackerel, mullet, tuna, catshark).
- **Commodities Trader** (Kavala ~034/132, Athira ~140/186, Pyrgos ~169/127): buys **iron ingots, copper ingots, cut diamonds, glass, rubber, processed oil, raw timber, all planks, and gold nuggets.**
- **Salt Dealer / Trader** (Sofia, ~258/214): buys **refined salt.**
- **Glass Dealer**: buys **glass.** (marked on map)
- **Jewelry Store**: buys **uncut & cut diamonds, flawless diamonds, rubies, pearls, gold doubloons, silver pieces, gold nuggets, and treasure.** (marked on map)
- **Oil Trader** (Kavala ~040/126): buys **processed oil and rubber.**
- **Wong's Food & Liquor** (Kavala ~036/125, Pyrgos ~167/123): buys **all spirits (scotch, corn & rye whiskey, moonshine, white lightning), turtle meat, turtle soup, dog meat, and cooked/fried oysters.**
- **Hunting Item Shop** (far-north hunting ground ~143/219): a *buy-only* tool shop (knives, sickle, axe, campfire, tents) — it does not buy your goods.

Prices fluctuate, so if a trader's offer looks low, check another city or come back later.

### Mining the quarries (iron, copper, diamond, sand, rock)

- **What:** Swing a pickaxe at a mineral quarry to pull ores and gems out of the rock.
- **Where:** Six quarries spread across Altis — grid **~081/144** (central-west), **~115/156** (central), **~197/124** (southeast), **~161/182** (near Athira), **~044/126** (near Kavala), and **~200/189** (east). Look for the mine-cart/rock props.
- **Requirement:** A **Pickaxe** ($50). Stand at the quarry and scroll **"Mine Quarry."**
- **What you get (per swing, ~4 seconds):** Mostly **Rock**, with chances at **Copper Ore**, **Iron Ore**, **Sand**, **Uncut Diamond**, and rarer hits of **Flawless Diamond** (~6% a swing after a drop-rate buff), **Gold Nugget** (~0.2%), or a **Treasure Map** (~0.05%).
- **The two rich quarries** at grid ~081/144 and ~200/189 pay **double** — every strike yields 2 of an ore instead of 1. Mine those if you can.
- **Then process** at a **Mineral Processor** NPC (near the quarries — e.g. grid ~159/174 by Athira, ~127/164, ~183/155, ~053/145):
  - **Iron Ore → Iron Ingot** (sells ~**$290–315** at Commodities)
  - **Copper Ore → Copper Ingot** (sells ~**$85–99** at Commodities)
  - **Uncut Diamond → Cut Diamond** (sells ~**$480–612** at Jewelry/Commodities)
  - **Sand → Glass** (sells ~**$200–250** at Glass Dealer/Commodities)
  - **Rock → Sand** ("Crush Rock") so you can turn worthless rock into glass.
- **Flawless Diamonds** and **Gold Nuggets** are sold **raw** — don't process them. Flawless Diamond sells up to ~**$2,850** at the Jewelry Store; a **Gold Nugget** is a jackpot at up to ~**$58,375** (Jewelry/Commodities).
- **Tip:** Diamonds are the best steady earner here. Bring a car so you can fill your trunk and bulk-process (see below).

### Metal detecting (quarry bonus)

- **What:** With a **Metal Detector** ($100) in your inventory, scroll **"Start Metal Detector"** at any quarry and **walk around** — you must keep moving (the detector only works while you're moving) or it cancels.
- **Reward (every ~10 seconds, 8s with a mining talent):** random buried loot — **scrap metal, lockpicks, rubber, silver pieces, gold doubloons, rubies, pearls, RedGull**, and rare big finds (**Treasure Map, Gold Bar, Gold Nugget, Flawless Diamond**, ~0.05% each). The two rich quarries dig up 2 at a time.
- **Tip:** Great passive income while you're already at a quarry — run laps with the detector between pickaxe swings.

### Salt

- **What:** Scrape salt off salt flats.
- **Where:** The eastern salt flats around grid **~234/174**, or anywhere the ground is dead/salt terrain. Scroll **"Gather Salt."** No tool required.
- **Process:** Take **Salt** to a **Salt Processor** (east, ~213/165) → **"Process Salt" → Refined Salt.** Needs the Salt Processing license (see above).
- **Sell:** **Refined Salt** at the **Salt Dealer** (Sofia) for ~**$180–256** each.

### Oil (and rubber)

- **What:** Drill crude oil from oil fields.
- **Where:** Oil fields at grid **~121/148** (central), **~082/150** (west), and **~199/179** (east). Scroll **"Gather Oil."**
- **Requirement — important:** To pull **Unprocessed Oil** you need a **Pickaxe *and* a fuel truck or fuel van parked within ~15m**. Without both, the same spot only gives you **Hydrogen Sulfate** instead.
- **Process:** Take **Unprocessed Oil** to an **Oil Processor** (~182/155) → **"Process Oil" → Processed Oil**, and every batch **also drops 1–7 units of Rubber for free** as a byproduct.
- **Sell:** **Processed Oil** ~**$210–484** and **Rubber** ~**$950–1,500** at the **Oil Trader** or **Commodities Trader**. Rubber is the real money here.
- **Bonus:** Oil processing is **not taxed** by any cartel, unlike drugs and spirits — you keep 100%.

### Phosphorus & Hydrogen Sulfate

- **What:** Two chemical raw materials gathered without any special process step.
- **Phosphorus:** Scroll **"Gather Phosphorus"** at the rocky deposit zones (grids **~187/182**, **~171/114**, **~118/204**). Sells raw at the **Market** for ~**$115–234**.
- **Hydrogen Sulfate:** Gathered at oil fields when you *don't* meet the oil-drilling requirement (no pickaxe or no fuel truck). Sells at the **Market** for ~**$115–234**.
- Both double as ingredients for certain drug recipes, but you can just sell them straight for clean cash.

### Lumber & timber

- **What:** Chop trees for wood, mill it into planks.
- **Where:** Anywhere **outside city limits** — you can't chop within ~1,000m of Kavala or ~500m of the other spawn towns. Face a live tree (within ~4m) with a **Wood Axe** and scroll **"Chop Tree."**
- **Yield:** A taller tree drops more — up to a big pile per tree. The wood type depends on the species:
  - Pine trees → **Pine Timber**
  - Olive trees → **Olive Timber**
  - Hardwood/palm/oak → **Hardwood Timber**
- **Process:** Take timber to a **Timber Mill** (north-central ~086/185 or northeast ~215/194) and pick **Mill Pine / Olive / Hardwood Timber → planks.**
  - **Pine Plank** ~**$112–171**, **Olive Plank** ~**$168–342**, **Hardwood Plank** ~**$280–570** (all sold at Commodities).
  - **Heads up:** milling **olive and hardwood** loses ~15% of the units (a built-in yield cut). Pine has no cut.
- **Sell:** Raw timber sells at the **Market/Commodities** too (Pine ~$50–114, Olive ~$75–171, Hardwood ~$120–285), but milling into planks pays much more. **Hardwood is the top earner.**

### Fruit, berries & grain

- **Apples & Peaches:** Scroll **"Pick Apples" / "Pick Peaches"** at the orchards (apples near Kavala ~042/131 and Sofia ~251/215; peaches near Kavala). Sell at the **Market** for ~**$5–6** each — pocket change, but fine for a brand-new player.
- **Wild Berries:** Face a berry bush and scroll **"Pick Berries"** — gives **1–3 berries** per bush. Sell at the **Market** for ~**$9–12** each.
- **Grain (Barley, Corn, Rye):** Face the right wild plant and scroll **"Pick Barley / Corn / Rye."** These aren't sold directly — they're the ingredients for **distilling spirits** (below). A **Sickle** guarantees a bonus unit per harvest.
- **Tip:** The **Bumper Crop** talent adds +1 to every field harvest (apples, peaches, berries, grain).

### Fishing

- **Net fishing (best):** Get into a **boat** (or amphibious vehicle), sit near-still over water, and scroll **"Drop Fishing Net."** After ~5 seconds it scoops up **every fish within 30m** (extends to **45m** or **60m** with fishing talents) — a whole net of fish at once.
- **Hand catching:** In shallow water near a visible fish, scroll **"Catch Fish"** to grab them one at a time.
- **The catch & values (sold at the Fish Market or Market):** Salema ~**$40**, Ornate ~**$42**, Mackerel ~**$70**, Mullet ~**$110**, Catshark ~**$130**, **Tuna ~$250** (the prize).
- **Tip:** Net fishing from a boat is one of the most relaxing, low-risk earners in the game — a full net of mixed fish adds up fast.

### Hunting, skinning & cooking meat

- **What:** Kill wild animals, skin them for raw meat, then cook the meat to roughly double its value.
- **How:** Shoot an animal (chickens, dogs, goats, sheep, rabbits, snakes), then with a **Skinning Knife** face the carcass and scroll **"Skin Animal"** — one raw meat per animal. Animals roam the countryside; the **Hunting Ground** (far north ~143/219) is a reliable spot and has the Hunting Item Shop.
- **Cook it:** Place & light a **Campfire Kit** (or use a lit barrel), then scroll **"Cook Raw Meat"** — it cooks **all** your raw meat at once.
- **Raw → Cooked values (sold at the Market):**
  - Chicken **$300 → $700**
  - Rabbit **$180 → $200**
  - Snake **$185 → $200**
  - Sheep **$300 → $1,200**
  - Goat **$400 → $1,300**
  - Dog meat **$1,200 → $2,100** (sold at **Wong's**, not the Market)
- **Always cook before selling** — cooked meat is worth far more. Goat and sheep are the money makers.

### Turtles

- **What:** By the water you may find turtles. With them dead, scroll **"take turtle meat."**
- **Sell:** **Turtle Meat** at **Wong's Food & Liquor** for up to ~**$2,422** — one of the best single-item gathers. Wong's also buys **Turtle Soup** if you have it (verify how soup is crafted; turtle meat itself is the reliable earner).

### Oysters

- **What:** Harvest oyster beds, then cook them up the food chain.
- **Where:** Oyster beds in shallow coastal water — near Pyrgos (~152/123) and west of Kavala (~020/121). Scroll **"Gather"** in the bed.
- **Bonus finds:** While gathering you can pull a **Pearl** (~1-in-15, sells ~$280–570 at the Jewelry Store) or rarely a **Treasure Map** (~1-in-2000).
- **Process:** At a lit **campfire** (or the oyster processor), **Cook Oysters** then **Fry Oysters**:
  - Fresh Oysters **$250 → Cooked $400 → Fried $700**
- **Sell:** Cooked & fried oysters at **Wong's**. Always cook/fry before selling.

### Distilling spirits & barrel aging

The most involved — and one of the most profitable — legal chains.

- **Distill** at a **Distillery** (the Heat Pump, north-central ~092/208). Recipes (scroll the distillery):
  - **Scotch** = Water + Yeast + Barley → New Make Scotch
  - **Corn Whiskey** = Water + Yeast + Barley + Corn → White Dog Corn Whiskey
  - **Rye Whiskey** = Water + Yeast + Barley + Rye → White Dog Rye Whiskey
  - **Rum** = Water + Yeast + Sugar → New Make Rum
  - (Each recipe also has a **Salt Water** version — collect Salt Water by scrolling **"Collect Water"** while standing in the sea — so you don't have to buy Water Bottles.)
- **Cartel cut:** Distilling pays a **15% cut to the Wongs Triad** cartel (5% on top-tier products) unless your gang controls that cartel — factor that into your margins.
- **Age it for real money.** New-make spirit is nearly worthless. Buy the **Aging Barrel** upgrade for a house you own, then scroll the barrel to **"Lay Down Spirits."** It auto-ages **one stage every 6 hours**, up to stage 2 (the "aged/15-Year" tier). Come back and **collect**. Barrel capacity scales with the house's storage cap.
- **Top-tier aging:** Take **15-Year Scotch** back to the distillery and use **"Age Scotch (Aging Chemicals)"** to push it to **25-Year Scotch**, the most valuable bottle.
- **White Lightning:** Distill Corn Hash → Moonshine → then **"Process White Lightning"** at the distillery for the purified top grade.
- **Sell all spirits at Wong's Food & Liquor.** Rough top values: **15-Year Scotch ~$900–1,282**, **25-Year Scotch ~$1,429–2,035**, aged **Corn/Rye Whiskey ~$600–1,014**, **Moonshine ~$600** and **White Lightning ~$1,300**.
- **Tip:** The chain rewards patience — grow/gather grain, distill in bulk, and let barrels age while you do something else. Aged bottles are worth many times the new-make.

### Bulk-processing from a vehicle trunk

Don't process one unit at a time. At most processors you'll see green **"Process Vehicle: ..."** options (Iron, Copper, Diamond, Salt, Oil, and more).

- Park an **owned vehicle within 25m** of the processor with the raw materials **in its trunk**, then scroll the green option.
- It processes the **entire trunk** in one batch and drops the finished goods **back into the trunk**. Finished units are banked into the trunk every 5% of progress, so if you get interrupted you keep everything already made — only the still-raw remainder stays raw.
- Same per-unit cartel tax as hand-processing, so it's not a tax dodge — just a massive time saver. **This is the standard way to run mining, oil, and salt at scale.**

### LoonyCoin mining rigs (passive income)

A newer, hands-off earner:

- The **8 PC components** (CPU, GPU, PSU, Storage, Motherboard, RAM, Cooler, Case) each drop as a grabbable **hardware case at Federal Reserve heist events** — so collecting the set means being near that (illegal) action, but the mining itself is passive and clean.
- Scroll a dropped case to **"Grab Component."** With all 8, scroll **"Assemble Mining Rig."**
- Scroll a **house you own** to **"Install Mining Rig"** (one rig per house). It then mines **LoonyCoin around the clock, even while you're offline.**
- Come back and **"Collect LoonyCoin"** — it accrues **2 coins per hour**, capped at **96 coins** (about 48 hours of stockpiling, so it's not a log-off-forever printer).
- **Sell LoonyCoin at the Blackmarket** for ~**$6,000–7,200 each.** A capped-out rig is a very tidy passive payout.
- **Note:** installing a rig is a little conspicuous — a nosy cop nearby might notice.

### Talents that boost this

If you invest side/skill talents, several speed up gathering and processing:

- **Assembly Line:** all processing runs **25% faster.**
- **Bumper Crop:** **+1 unit** on every field harvest (apples, peaches, berries, grain).
- **Mining talents:** faster pickaxe swings, better iron/diamond odds, and one that **doubles copper** (and removes junk rock drops).
- **Fishing net range:** extends the net's catch radius to 45m or 60m.
- **Oil:** a talent that adds +1 rubber per oil batch.

Stack these once you've settled into a favorite loop and your per-hour income climbs noticeably.

---

## Drugs: Grow, Cook, Sell

Running drugs is the classic civilian money-maker: **gather a raw crop → process (cook) it into a finished product → sell it to a dealer.** Almost every drug follows that same three-step loop, and most steps carry risk — the police can search you, seize the goods, and jail you, and processing hard drugs is taxed by whichever gang owns the Drug Cartel.

### How the drug loop works (read this first)

1. **Gather** the raw material. Most raw drugs come from hidden **illegal fields** (you scroll-wheel "Gather X" when you're standing on the field). Weed can also be **home-grown** in a house greenhouse.
2. **Process** it at the matching **processor** — either a fixed NPC (Cocaine/Heroin/Meth/Marijuana/PCP/Spirit processors) or a **mobile lab vehicle** (the Crank RV and the Moonshine Distillery). Processing runs a progress bar; stay next to the processor/vehicle until it finishes or you get nothing.
3. **Sell** the finished product. Illegal drugs go to a **Drug Dealer**; spirits (moonshine, scotch, whiskey) go to **Wong's Food & Liquor**.

**Important money note:** the Drug Dealer pays a **live, floating price** (dynamic market). The dollar figures below are the base sell prices. Farmable drugs (weed, coke, heroin, meth) float **up** when the map is under-supplied, and gang/infamy perks add up to roughly **+14%** on top. The exact price is always shown live in the shop window — trust that number over any figure here.

### Do I need a license?

- **Gathering and cooking most drugs needs NO license** — cocaine, heroin, meth, crank, LSD, PCP, moonshine, and basic weed are all unlicensed.
- **Marijuana Processing Contract — $800** (bought at any Marijuana processor). You only need it to turn Indica Bud into **Golden Wax**. Making regular Reggie Cannabis needs no license.
- **Medicinal Cannabis** can only be processed when the sitting **Governor has legalized it** (a Governor toggle) — otherwise the option is greyed out at the Medicinal processor.
- A **Rebel license** isn't required for drugs, but many of the drug speed/yield/price perks only apply if you own one (see Talents below).

---

### Weed — the wild field route

**One-line:** Gather wild cannabis, process it into sellable Reggie Cannabis.

- **Where to gather:** the **Cannabis field** (west-central Altis, around grid **6490 E / 14543 N**, plus a second weed field). Stand in the field and scroll **"Gather Cannabis"** — you pull **Reggie Stems** (~1 per 4-second tick).
- **Where to process:** any **Marijuana Processor** NPC (several around the map — e.g. near **4175 E / 11658 N** in the south-west, and others central/east). Scroll **"Process Marijuana"** to turn Reggie Stems → **Reggie Cannabis**.
- **Where to sell:** any **Drug Dealer** → **Reggie Cannabis ≈ $420 each** (floats up to ~$500).

**Golden Wax (premium):** if you have Indica Bud, use **"Process Indica Bud into Golden Wax"** at the Marijuana processor (needs the **$800 Marijuana Contract**). Golden Wax sells for **≈ $230** (up to ~$400). Sativa Bud can NOT be turned into wax — it only sells as a bud.

**Medicinal Cannabis:** processed at the Medicinal processor (south-west, ~**3769 E / 11343 N**) only while it's Governor-legalized; sells **≈ $380**. It's also the only weed the LoonyBin Hospital will buy.

### Weed — home grow (house greenhouse)

**One-line:** Buy seeds, plant them in your house greenhouse, and they grow even while you're offline.

- **Requirement:** a house with the **Greenhouse upgrade — $45,000** (bought from your House Menu → "Add Greenhouse"). You must **own the house or hold keys** to it.
- **Buy seeds** at any **Drug Dealer**: **Sativa Seed $100** / **Indica Seed $100** each (the dealer's only buyable items).
- **Plant:** stand near your greenhouse house and scroll **"Plant Sativa Seeds"** or **"Plant Indica Seeds."** Each planting uses up your seeds and fills open plant slots.
  - **Capacity = your house's virtual-storage capacity ÷ 10 plants.** Upgrade virtual storage to grow more at once.
- **Grow times (they tick even while you're logged off):**
  - **Sativa → matures in 2 hours**
  - **Indica → matures in 3 hours**
- **Check progress:** scroll **"Check Greenhouse"** — shows each batch's stage (Seed → Seedling → Juvenile → Mature) and time left.
- **Harvest:** scroll **"Harvest Greenhouse."** It fills your free carry weight (bud weighs 1), Sativa first then Indica. You get **Sativa Bud** and **Indica Bud** directly (no drying needed).
- **Sell buds** at any Drug Dealer: **Sativa Bud / Indica Bud ≈ $120 each** (floats up to ~$200). Turn Indica Bud into Golden Wax first for more (see above).

**Tip:** the greenhouse is shared by everyone with keys to the house, and a gardening service can even tend it while you're away — always **Check Greenhouse** when you log in to see what landed.

### Weed — smoking it (paraphernalia)

Bud and wax can also be **used**, not just sold. Buy pipes/papers at the **Medicinal Dispensary** (Glass Bong, Glass Rig, Pre-Roll Joint, Dab Vape, RAW Papers) or RAW Papers at the Hospital. Roll a **Rolled Joint** from RAW Papers + flower, or smoke from a **Bong** (flower) / **Glass Rig** (Golden Wax) / **Vape**. Smoking briefly **reduces your weapon sway** (12% for 1–6 minutes depending on the method) and acts as a mild painkiller. Golden Wax via the rig gives the longest sway reduction.

---

### Cocaine

**One-line:** Gather coca, refine it, brick it, sell it.

- **Gather:** the **Cocaine field** (central, ~**11812 E / 16223 N**). Scroll **"Gather Cocaine"** → **Unprocessed Cocaine**.
- **Process:** at the **Cocaine Processor** (north, ~**14581 E / 20760 N**):
  1. **"Process Cocaine"** → **Cocaine** (≈ $600, up to ~$1,018)
  2. **"Form Cocaine Bricks (Purify)"** → **Cocaine Brick** (≈ $1,000, up to ~$1,618) — always brick it, bricks are worth the most.
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

### Heroin (Opium)

**One-line:** Gather opium, cook it into heroin, sell it.

- **Gather:** the **Opium field** (east, ~**20903 E / 17459 N**). Scroll **"Gather Opium"** → **Opium** (also sells raw for ~$312 if you can't process).
- **Process:** at the **Heroin Processor** (north-east, ~**23253 E / 21820 N**) → **"Process Heroin"** → **Heroin** (≈ $550, up to ~$819).
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

### Meth

**One-line:** Mix a cocktail from ephedra + chemicals, cook it, purify it blue.

- **Gather three ingredients:**
  - **Ephedra plant** — the **Ephedra field** (south-central, ~**17527 E / 10849 N**), scroll **"Gather Ephedra."**
  - **Phosphorus** — any **rock/quarry** field (scroll "Gather Phosphorus").
  - **Hydrogen Sulfate** — any **oil field** (scroll "Gather Hydrogen Sulfate").
- **Process:** at the **Meth Processor** (east, ~**22158 E / 14422 N**):
  1. **"Mix Meth cocktail"** (Ephedra + Phosphorus + Hydrogen Sulfate) → **Meth Cocktail** (sells ~$375)
  2. **"Cook Meth"** → **Crystal Meth** (≈ $1,800, up to ~$3,277)
  3. **"Purify Meth (Blue)"** → **Heisenberg Meth** (≈ $2,700, up to ~$3,880) — the top-tier meth.
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

### Crank (mobile RV lab)

**One-line:** A "Breaking Bad" mobile cook — same chemicals as meth, but done in a moving van out in the wilderness.

- **Vehicle:** a **Box Van fitted with the meth-lab skin** (the Crank RV). You cook inside/next to it, not at a fixed NPC.
- **Set up:** drive somewhere remote and scroll **"Get the lab ready."** The lab **warms up (~30s)** and starts smoking (everyone can see the smoke). **You cannot set up a lab:**
  - within **1,000m of a city** (Kavala/Athira/Pyrgos/Sofia),
  - within **300m of a police HQ or a delivery point**,
  - within **400m of any house**, near the Gang Fortress, arms/skull islands, the courthouse, spawn, admin island, or an oil rig,
  - or within **20m of any building / industrial shed**.
- **Cook (ingredients are the same as the meth cocktail):**
  1. **"Mix Crank cocktail"** (Ephedra + Phosphorus + Hydrogen Sulfate) → **Crank Cocktail** (sells ~$235)
  2. **"Cook Crank"** → **Crank** (≈ $1,000, up to ~$1,780)
- **Break down:** scroll **"Break down the lab"** when done.
- **Gotcha:** **moving the van while the lab is running blows the engine / shuts the lab down.** Park it, cook, then break down before you drive off.
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

### LSD (Dark Fungi Den)

**One-line:** Dig glowing fungi in an underground den, extract LSD.

- **Gather:** find the **Fungi Den elevator** (a workshop entrance in the north-west, ~**7858 E / 18040 N**). Scroll **"Enter Fungi Den"** to ride the **Elevator 3000** down ~250m. Once inside, scroll **"Dig for Dark Fungi"** at the dig site → **Dark Fungi**. You **must take the elevator down** — you can't dig from the surface.
- **Process** (at the fungi processor near the den): **"Extract LSD from Moist Fungi"** → **LSD Pill** (≈ $550, up to ~$2,000), then **"Form LSD Sheets (Purify)"** → **Purified LSD Sheet** (≈ $900, up to ~$2,000).
- Scroll **"Exit Fungi Den"** to elevator back up.
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

### PCP

**One-line:** Mix henbane + chemicals into powder, press pills, purify sheets.

- **Gather:** **Henbane** at the **Henbane field** (north-central, ~**9206 E / 17397 N**) + **Hydrogen Sulfate** from an oil field.
- **Process:** at the **PCP Processor** (far north, ~**11809 E / 22700 N**):
  1. **"Mix PCP Powder"** (Henbane + Hydrogen Sulfate) → **PCP Powder**
  2. **"Process PCP Pills"** → **PCP Pill** (≈ $1,000, up to ~$2,000)
  3. **"Form PCP Sheets (Purify)"** → **Purified PCP Sheet** (≈ $1,350, up to ~$2,000)
- **Sell:** any Drug Dealer.

---

### Moonshine (mobile distillery)

**One-line:** Homebrew corn liquor in a mobile still out in the sticks — no license, sells at Wong's.

- **Vehicle:** a **Fuel Van fitted with the moonshine skin** (the Mobile Distillery). Same placement rules as the Crank RV (away from cities, police, houses, buildings, sheds — see Crank above).
- **Ingredients:** **Water** (collect from the sea — scroll "Collect Water" while standing in shallow water), **Yeast** ($20 at the Market or Wong's), and **Corn** (gather at corn fields).
- **Set up:** scroll **"Get the distillery ready"** (warms up ~30s and smokes). Then:
  1. **"Make Supplies into Corn Hash"** (Water + Yeast + Corn) → **Moonshine Hash**
  2. **"Distill Hash into Homebrew Moonshine"** → **Homebrew Moonshine** (sells **≈ $790** at Wong's)
- **White Lightning (upgrade):** take Moonshine to the **Spirit Distillery** (the heat-pump NPC in the north, ~**9159 E / 20790 N**) and scroll **"Process White Lightning"** → **White Lightning** (sells **≈ $1,100** at Wong's).
- **Gotcha:** as with the crank RV, **moving the van mid-cook shuts it down.**
- **Sell:** **Wong's Food & Liquor** (central).

### Scotch, Whiskey & Rum (distillery + house aging barrel)

**One-line:** Distill "new-make" spirit, then age it for hours in a house Aging Barrel to multiply its value.

- **Step 1 — distill new-make** at the **Spirit Distillery** (heat-pump NPC, north ~**9159 E / 20790 N**). All recipes use **Water + Yeast** (salt water also works) plus a grain:
  - **Scotch:** + **Barley** → **New Make Scotch**
  - **Corn Whiskey:** + **Barley + Corn** → **White Dog Corn Whiskey**
  - **Rye Whiskey:** + **Barley + Rye** → **White Dog Rye Whiskey**
  - (Grains — barley, corn, rye — are gathered at the grain fields; a **Sickle** doubles grain yield.)
- **Step 2 — age it** in a house **Aging Barrel** upgrade (**$35,000**, from your House Menu → "Add Aging Barrel"):
  - Scroll **"Lay Down Spirits to Age"** at the house — it lays down **every new-make bottle** you're carrying.
  - **Capacity = house virtual-storage capacity ÷ 30 units** (each spirit bottle weighs 3).
  - Spirits auto-age **one stage every 6 hours**, stage 0 → 1 → 2 (e.g. New Make Scotch → Scotch → **15 Year Scotch**). Aging happens while you're offline.
  - Scroll **"Check Aging Barrel"** for stages/timers, **"Collect Aging Barrel"** to pull finished bottles.
- **25 Year Scotch (top tier):** take **15 Year Scotch** back to the Spirit Distillery → **"Age Scotch (Aging Chemicals)"** → **25 Year Scotch**.
- **Sell everything at Wong's Food & Liquor.** Approx sell prices (they rise with age):
  - **Scotch:** New Make ~$35 → Scotch ~$375 → 15 Year ~$700 → 25 Year ~$1,200
  - **Corn Whiskey:** ~$180 → $426 → $639 → $987
  - **Rye Whiskey:** ~$212 → $479 → $693 → $1,013
- **Rum is not currently implemented.** You may see rum items or recipes referenced, but the rum line isn't finished (its recipe needs Sugar, which isn't sold anywhere) — ignore rum for now.

---

### The Drug Cartel cut (processing tax)

When you **process** hard drugs, the gang that owns the **Drug Cartel** skims part of your yield:

- **Standard hard-drug processing** (cocaine, heroin, meth, weed, LSD, PCP, etc.): the cartel takes **15%** of the units you'd have made (you keep **85%**).
- **Purified/second-stage tiers** (Cocaine Brick, Heisenberg Meth, Sativa/Indica Bud drying, 25-Year Scotch, PCP Sheets): only a **5%** cut (you keep **95%**).
- **If your gang owns the relevant cartel, you pay 0% and keep the whole batch.**
- Spirits (scotch/whiskey) are taxed the same way by the **Wongs Triad** cartel; **moonshine and oil are untaxed.**

This is a tax on the **amount produced when processing**, not on the sale — you never lose money at the dealer, just some units at the processor.

---

### Where to sell — the Drug Dealer

**Drug Dealers** are scattered around the map (several NPCs, e.g. near the central market and the far corners of the island). They are **civilian-only and sell-only** — the only thing you can **buy** there is **Sativa/Indica seeds ($100)**. They buy every finished illegal drug: Reggie Cannabis, Medicinal Cannabis, Sativa/Indica Bud, Golden Wax, Cocaine, Cocaine Brick, Heroin, Crystal Meth, Heisenberg Meth, Meth Cocktail, Crank, Crank Cocktail, LSD Pills & Sheets, and PCP Pills & Sheets.

**Spirits go to Wong's Food & Liquor** (moonshine, white lightning, scotch, corn & rye whiskey), not the Drug Dealer.

**Selling boosts (stack on the live price):**
- Gang talents at the dealer: **+5%** and **+2%** sell bonuses.
- **Infamy talent 26 + Rebel license:** **+7%** at the Drug Dealer, Wong's and Rebel market.
- Low-supply dynamic market floats farmable drugs even higher.

---

### Drug Runner (the plane smuggling job)

**One-line:** A high-risk flying courier mission that pays out based on how far you fly.

- **Where:** talk to a **Drug Runner NPC** at one of **5 island airfields** and pick **"Get Plane Drug Runner Job"** (civilian only).
- **Cost/risk:** you must put a **$15,000 deposit** from your **bank** down. You're loaned a plane and drugs.
- **How it runs:**
  1. Fly the supplied plane to the **target airfield** (get within **50m**), scroll **"Load Drugs on Plane."**
  2. Fly to the next airfield and scroll **"Deliver to Smuggler."**
  3. Each leg has a timer based on distance (~**0.3 minutes per km**, minimum **3 minutes** per leg).
- **Informant risk:** normally a **30% chance** the police get tipped off that a drug plane will land at a given airfield. Talents reduce this to **20%** (talent 35) or **10%** (talent 36).
- **Reward on success:** **cash = total distance flown × 0.8** (e.g. long routes pay tens of thousands), **+5%** if you have Infamy talent 24 + a Rebel license. Your **$15,000 deposit is returned to your bank**, plus **+10 side EXP** and **+15 Infamy**.
- **Failure** (you die, the plane is destroyed, or you run out of time): the smugglers **keep your $15,000 deposit**.
- **Abandon** ("Abandon Job!"): the smugglers keep **$3,000** and refund **$12,000**.
- **Gotcha:** the loaded plane is flagged as drug-running — landing at an alerted airfield with cops waiting is how most runs die.

---

### Talents & bonuses that speed up drugs

Grab these on the civilian/infamy talent trees to earn more per run (most illegal-drug perks also require a **Rebel license**):

- **Processing yield:** full drug-processing return is **baked in for every civilian** — no talent tax anymore. (Timber still loses 15%.)
- **Faster processing:** Assembly Line (**−25%** on all processing) and a general drug-process speed perk with a Rebel license; weed processing is already **~5× faster** than other drugs by default.
- **Faster gathering:** per-drug infamy talents shave time off gathering cannabis, opium, cocaine, ephedra and henbane. **Bumper Crop** adds **+1 unit** to every field harvest; **Rockhound** adds +1 to fungi digs.
- **Gang gather bonus:** gang talents give a chance at **+1 unit** on drug gathers; controlling **Rodopoli turf** can double coca/opium pulls.
- **Better sell prices & informant reduction:** see the sell-boost and Drug Runner sections above.
- **Cheaper house upgrades:** the Homesteader "Landlord" talent cuts storage-upgrade costs **−35%** (helps expand greenhouse/barrel capacity).

### Gotchas & tips

- **Always brick/purify before selling** — Cocaine Brick, Heisenberg Meth, White Lightning, Golden Wax and aged spirits are worth far more than their base stage.
- **Watch your weight** — buds weigh 1 and spirits weigh 3; harvesting/collecting only fills your free carry weight, so make room first.
- **Cook labs away from everything** — cities, police, houses, buildings and sheds all block mobile lab/distillery setup, and the smoke is visible to the whole server.
- **Never move a running RV/distillery** — it shuts down (and can blow the engine).
- **Processing = wanted heat** — cooking hard drugs raises your **Infamy** (rebel rep): roughly **3× per unit** for hard drugs (capped ~45 per batch), but only **0.2× per unit** for home-grown weed (capped 15). Carrying drugs is illegal — cops can search and seize.
- **The greenhouse and aging barrel keep working while you're offline** — plant/lay-down before you log off and collect next session.

---

## Shops & Markets & Player Exchange

Everything you buy and sell on LooneyBin happens at one of three places: **shop NPCs** (walk up, scroll-wheel, trade), the **Market app** on your phone (a read-only price board), or the **LoonyBin Grand Exchange** (a player-run marketplace where other players set the prices). This section covers all of them, with the real prices from the game.

### Money & how paying works

You carry money in two places:
- **Cash** — physical money on you. Dropped when you die/get robbed.
- **Bank** — safe in your account. You start with **$3,000** in the bank as a civilian (cops start with $7,000, Trauma Team $6,500).

At most legal shops you can pay with **cash OR your bank**, but to spend bank money in a shop you must have a **Debit Card** in your inventory. If you have no debit card, you can only spend cash.

**Rebel Market, Turf Market, Rebel Armory, and Gang Gats are CASH-ONLY** — the bank/debit card is not accepted there, so bring physical cash.

An item row turns **bright** (fully lit) when you can afford it and **dim** when you can't.

### Buying and selling at trader shops (the virtual shop menu)

Most trading is done at a **trader NPC** (drug dealer, market vendor, jeweler, etc.). Walk up and use the scroll-wheel action to open the shop. The window lists item **Name**, **Price**, and how many you currently carry (**Inv Amount**).

**To buy:** click the item, type a quantity into the box, press **Buy**.
**To sell:** click the item, type a quantity (or **double-click** the row to auto-fill the full amount you're carrying), press **Sell**.

Rules and limits:
- You must be **within ~10 m**, **on foot** (not in a vehicle), and have **line of sight** — you can't buy through a wall.
- You can't shop while **restrained** or with your **hands up**.
- **You can buy at most 100 of an item per click.** Selling has no per-click cap (you can sell your whole stack).
- Your **carry weight** limits how much you can buy at once (max carry weight is **96**).

### General Store & Gas Station

These are your everyday civilian shops, found in essentially every town.
- The **General Store** (`<Town> General Store`, e.g. "Pyrgos General Store") and **Gas Station** (`<Town> Gas Station`) sell basic gear: **Toolkit $50, First Aid Kit $75, Binocular $100, GPS/Map/Compass/Watch $10 each**, night-vision goggles **$300**, plus flashlights and chemlights.
- The same buildings also have a **virtual food/supplies shop** (the "`<Town> Market`" / "Gas Station Market") selling and buying consumables: **Water $7, Apple $6, RedGull $150, Beer $10, Fuel canisters, kebabs, camping gear, spray cans**, etc. The town Market also **buys your farmed food and raw materials** (rabbit, chicken, timber, apples, berries, etc.).
- **Delivery / DP Supplies** shops support the delivery-driver job.

### The trader shops (where to sell what)

Each resource has a dedicated buyer. Sell your goods where they're wanted:

| Shop (NPC name) | What it does |
|---|---|
| **Drug Dealer** | **Buys all your processed drugs** (cocaine, heroin, meth, crank, PCP, marijuana, etc.). **Sells only cannabis seeds** — you buy your first seeds here, everything else is sell-only. |
| **Wong's Food & Liquor** | Buys processed food & liquor (moonshine/White Lightning, scotch, whiskey, turtle soup, oyster fry) and some exotic goods (weapon-grade uranium, human meat, Element 115). Sells **Yeast**. |
| **Jewelry Store** | Buys/sells cut & flawless diamonds, rubies, pearls, doubloons, silver, treasure, gold nuggets. Sells a **Pickaxe**. |
| **Commodities Trader** | Iron/copper ingots, glass, rubber, processed oil, timber & planks, gold nuggets. |
| **Oil Trader** | Processed oil, rubber, fuel, pickaxes. |
| **Salt Dealer** | Refined salt. |
| **Glass Dealer** | Glass. |
| **`<Town> Fish Market** | Buys/sells your catch (salema, ornate, mackerel, mullet, tuna, catshark). |
| **Black Market** | Sells oil; **buys stolen/illegal goods**: human organs, foreskins, LoonyCoin. |
| **African Warlord** | Buys stolen jewelry-heist loot (Hope diamond, Rolex, exotic jewels, gold bags, etc.). |
| **Rebel Market / Turf Market** | Buys/sells rebel gear: lockpicks, blindfolds, zipties, bolt cutters, thermite, demo charges, drills, ATM skimmers, jewelry-heist tools, spike/nitro, bag walls, and **Vehicle Ammo ($3,500)**. Also **buys Gold Bars** (see below). CASH ONLY. |
| **Homegoods Store** | Furniture for your house (beds, chairs, TVs, lamps, PCs, etc.). |
| **LoonyBin Hospital** | Adrenaline shots, defibs, blood bags, first aid kits, and a **House Repair Kit ($50,000)** to fix your own destroyed house. |
| **Medicinal Dispensary** | Cannabis smoking supplies (bongs, rigs, vapes, papers). |

**Reference prices** (buy → sell, before taxes/market swings):

- **Consumables:** Water $7→$5 · Apple $6→$5 · RedGull $150→$75 · Beer $10→$7 · Lockpick $25→$10 · Blindfold $1,000→$500 · Bolt Cutter $1,000→$500 · Ziptie $20→$15 · Spray Can $500→$200.
- **Drugs (sell only, typical values — these float with the market, see below):** Cocaine ~$600–$1,000 · Cocaine Brick ~$1,000–$1,600 · Heroin ~$550–$800 · Crank ~$1,000–$1,700 · Crystal Meth ~$1,800–$3,300 · Heisenberg (Blue) Meth ~$2,700–$3,900 · Purified PCP ~$1,350–$2,000 · Weapon-grade Uranium ~$575–$1,000 · White Lightning (moonshine) ~$1,100.
- **Gems:** Flawless Diamond ~$1,400 · Cut Diamond ~$480 · Ruby ~$315 · Pearl ~$280.
- **Metals/materials:** Iron Ingot $290 · Copper Ingot $95 · Glass $230 · Rubber ~$1,050 · Refined Salt $180 · Processed Oil ~$340.
- **Gold:** **Gold Bar sells for ~$50,000** — but **only at a Rebel Outpost** (you must be within 200 m of one). If your gang owns the **Zaros turf**, gold bars sell **+10%**. Gold Nugget sells ~$30,000 (Jewelry/Commodities).

> Numbers shown as ranges are goods tracked by the **dynamic market** (see next). Static goods (most consumables) always sell for their listed price.

### The dynamic market (prices that move)

Many farmed and processed goods have **live, floating prices** driven by supply and demand:
- **Every time players sell a good, its price drops**; the more that's dumped, the cheaper it gets.
- If a good is **left alone, its price recovers and climbs** back up (a scarcity premium).
- Prices swing between roughly **60% and 130%** of the item's ceiling. **Tip:** sell into a "fresh" market for the top price; if everyone's been dumping cocaine, wait or sell something else.
- **Anti-flip rule:** any item you can *buy* can never be *sold* for more than its buy price — no free-money loops.
- **Drug Bust event:** occasionally (a Casino wheel prize) every illegal drug sells **+12% server-wide** for a 5-minute window — great time to offload.

### Market app (phone price board)

Open your **phone → Market** app (available to civilians and police, not Trauma Team) to see a live **price board** of what things sell for right now. It lists sell prices for the **Drug Dealer, Wong's, Rebel, Town Market, Oil, Fish Market, Jewelry, Salt, Commodities, and Black Market**. It's **read-only** — you still have to physically visit a shop to trade. Use it to decide where to haul your goods before you drive across the map.

### Sales tax, cartel cuts & the wealth surcharge

Some of what you pay/earn is skimmed off the top:
- **Governor sales tax:** legal shops (town market, gas, jewelry, oil, salt, commodities, fish, hospital, etc.) add a **tax set by the elected Governor** to your purchases. This money funds the government.
- **Wealth surcharge:** on top of the Governor's rate, **rich players pay extra tax** based on bank balance — **+2%** over $750k, **+4%** over $1.25M, **+8%** over $2.5M, **+10%** over $5M, **+12%** over $7.5M, **+15%** over $10M, **+20%** over $20M. Keeping cash out of your bank reduces this.
- **Arms Dealer cut:** buying at the **Rebel** shop/armory costs an extra **8%** that goes to the gang that owns the Arms cartel — unless **your gang owns it**, in which case you skip the cut *and* get a **15% discount** on all rebel purchases.
- **Wong's cut:** selling at **Wong's** takes a **10%** cut for the gang that owns the Wong cartel (raw/unprocessed spirits are exempt).
- **Turf tax:** selling drugs at a **Drug Dealer inside a turf zone** takes a **20%** cut for the turf owner.
- **Gang Fortress discount:** while your gang holds the **Gang Fortress**, buying inside its zone is **15% cheaper** (shop items and vehicles).

### Merchant talents (sell for more)

If you've spent talent points on the **Merchant** line, your sell prices go up:
- **Fence** — **+10%** on *all* sales.
- **Kingpin** — **+25%** at drug shops (Drug Dealer, Wong's).
- **Wholesaler** — **+20%** at non-drug shops.

Rebel-license infamy perks and gang perks add further small discounts/bonuses at rebel and drug shops.

### Weapon shops

Weapon shops are gated by **license, side, and sometimes talents**:

- **Billy Joe's Firearms (Gun Store)** — civilian. Needs a **Firearm License** ($250). Sells legal pistols, PDWs, a few SMGs, and the legal SPAR-16. Prices e.g. pistols $1,000–$2,500, PDW $3,500.
- **Rebel Armory ("Your Jihadi Shop")** — civilian, needs **Rebel License** ($12,500) + **Rebel Training**. Full illegal catalog: rifles, snipers, LMGs ($5k–$50k), sawn-off/shotguns, RPG-7, and grenades. **CASH ONLY**. An **8% Arms cut** applies (see taxes).
- **Gang Gats (Turf Armory)** — sells a mid-tier rebel catalog inside turf zones. CASH ONLY.
- **Hunting Ground Supplies** — hunting-themed shop (shotguns, hunting rifles, camping gear).
- **Bounty Hunter Shop** — needs a **Bounty Hunter License** ($12,500). The upgraded **Skiptracer** tier needs the **Master Bounty Hunter** talent and Honor perks for its illegal high-end rifles.
- **Police / MaxTac / SRT armories** and the **TT Equipment Shop** exist for on-duty cops and Trauma Team and are gated by rank/whitelist.

Buying a weapon: open the shop, pick the gun (ammo shows in the side panel), press Buy. You can't buy a second primary/secondary/handgun without a confirmation that it **replaces** your current one. Tracer/LMG-box ammo is blocked at civilian shops.

### Clothing stores

Clothing shops let you **preview outfits on a rotating camera** before buying:
- **Bruce's Outback Outfits** — civilian clothing. Uniforms are cheap (**$25–$50** each). You also get **Rebel clothing**, **diving**, **go-kart**, and **barber (faces)** stores, plus **Cop** and **Medic** clothing for those factions.
- Filter by **Uniforms / Headgear / Glasses / Vests / Backpacks**, or type in the **search box** to find a piece by name.
- Select a piece to preview it; the total price updates. Press **Purchase** to buy the whole outfit. **Cancel/Escape restores your original clothes** at no cost.
- You must be **on foot, within ~7 m**, with line of sight. A bought outfit is saved and survives relogs.

### Vehicle shop (and buy-time upgrades)

At a car/boat/air/dealer, open the shop, pick a vehicle from the list, and **configure it before buying**:

**Buy-time modifications** (added to the base price):
- **Car Alarm** — $5,000
- **Trunk storage** — +50 for $5,000 · +75 for $7,500 · +100 for $10,000
- **Handling** — Suspension $3,500 · Brakes+Suspension $7,500
- **Nitrous** — $10,000
- **GPS Tracker** — $10,000
- **Hardened Locks** — Basic $10,000 · Medium $20,000 · Advanced $30,000
- **Doors** (LSVs only) — $35,000
- **Autopilot Module** (ground vehicles only) — $30,000 (free with the *Autopilot Certified* Motorist talent)

Notes:
- The **Governor sales tax + wealth surcharge** apply to the total. Buying inside your own **Gang Fortress** is **15% off**.
- **Vehicle Discount** talent knocks **10%** off vehicle purchases; the rebel-car infamy perk adds another 10% on rebel cars.
- You can hold up to **45 vehicles in your garage**. If it's full you must sell/store one before buying another.
- Bought vehicles spawn on the dealer's pads; a blocked pad will stop the spawn, so keep them clear.

### The LoonyBin Grand Exchange (player marketplace)

The **Grand Exchange** is a player-to-player auction house — **you** set prices and buy from **other players**. Find the **Exchange trader NPC** (a man in shorts) — there are around five of them across the map's **major towns/trade hubs** (verify exact towns). Scroll-wheel him for these options (civilian only):

- **LoonyBin Grand Exchange** — browse & buy listings.
- **LoonyBin Exchange Storage** — collect items you bought, retracted, or that expired.
- **LoonyBin Exchange – List Virtual Items** — list resources from your car trunk.
- **LoonyBin Exchange – List Physical Items** — list weapons/gear from your car trunk.
- **(List Vehicle)** — scroll on a vehicle you own within 5 m to list the car itself.

**Listing an item:**
1. **Park your owned vehicle within 35 m** of the Exchange NPC (or the vehicle itself within 5 m for a car listing). The listing pulls from the **trunk** — the entire stack of the chosen item is listed.
2. Pick the item, then **enter your asking price per unit**.
3. Pay a **listing fee of 5% of the total listing value** (price × quantity), taken from your **bank**.
4. **Price/quantity limits:** virtual items **$25–$500,000 per unit, up to 750 units** per listing; physical items and vehicles **$25–$999,999**. Vehicle listing fee is 5% of the asking price.
5. You may have up to **20 virtual + 20 physical + 20 vehicle** live listings at once.

**Buying a listing:** open the Grand Exchange, browse or **Check Availability** to search for a specific item, select a listing, and **Purchase** (you can enter a quantity for stacked listings). **You pay from your bank.** You can't buy your own listing.
- **Purchased virtual/physical items go to your Exchange Storage** — you must return to an Exchange NPC and use **Exchange Storage → Retrieve** to withdraw them. They do **not** appear in your inventory automatically.
- **Purchased vehicles go straight to your garage** (you need a free garage slot; DLC vehicles prompt a confirmation).

**Getting paid / expiry:**
- When your listing sells, **you're paid the full asking price into your bank** — instantly if online, or added to your bank while offline (you'll see "Exchange sale: … sold for $…!").
- **Listings last 7 days.** If yours doesn't sell in time, the goods go to your **Exchange Storage** (vehicles go back to your **garage**) — nothing is lost.
- You can **Retract** your own listing early to pull it back into Exchange Storage.
- **Exchange Storage holds up to 5 virtual + 5 physical stored listings.** If storage is full, you can't buy/retract more until you clear it (retrieve items or re-list them).

**Gotchas:**
- The Exchange is **disabled for ~3 minutes before a server restart** — finish your trades early.
- There's a short cooldown (a couple of seconds) between exchange actions.
- Listing pulls from a vehicle you **own** that isn't already in use; make sure the item is actually in that vehicle's trunk.

### Quick license reference (for shop access)

Costs to unlock the shops that need a license (bought from the license/DMV points):
- **Firearm License** — $250 (Gun Store)
- **Rebel License** — $12,500 (Rebel Armory/Market)
- **Bounty Hunter License** — $12,500 (Bounty/Skiptracer shops)
- **Home Owner License** — $3,500 · **Driver's License** — $100 · **Pilot License** — $4,000 · **Truck License** — $4,000 · **Boating License** — $150
- **Processing licenses:** Copper $600 · Iron $950 · Diamond $3,500 · Marijuana $800 · Oil $1,000 · Salt $900 · Sand $700

### Gotchas & tips

- **Buying at a Drug Dealer while you're wanted (bounty over $500) can ping nearby police** ("informant spotted a suspect at a drug dealer"). Sell clean when possible.
- **Selling drugs leaves a heat/"drug trace"** on you for a while after the sale.
- The **Drug Dealer buys, it does not sell** anything except cannabis seeds — bring product, not cash.
- **Gold bars only sell at a Rebel Outpost.** Trying to sell them anywhere else gives "You cannot sell this here!"
- Keep a **Debit Card** on you to spend bank money at shops; rebel/turf shops still need **cash**.
- Rich? Expect **higher sales tax** on purchases — the wealth surcharge scales with your bank balance.
