- The **Market phone app** is the fastest way to check where a good sells best before you drive.

---

## Vehicles: Buy, Garage, Chop, Keys, Recovery

Everything you own with wheels, rotors, or a hull lives in one of your garages. You buy vehicles from dealer NPCs, store and re-pull them at garages, mod them, trade keys, chop stolen ones for cash, and (if one blows up) recover the wreck. This section walks through all of it with the exact prices and rules from the server.

> Money note: **cash** = the money on your person (can be robbed/lost on death), **bank** = your ATM balance. Different actions draw from and pay to different pockets — it's called out each time. Numbers below are base prices; taxes, discounts, and gang perks adjust the final total.

### Quick reference

| Thing | Where | Cost |
|---|---|---|
| Buy a vehicle | Dealer NPC (Car/Heli/Boat/Truck/Kart/RV Shop) | Vehicle price + governor tax |
| Store a vehicle | Any matching garage NPC | Free |
| Pull from garage | Garage NPC (or your house) | Pull fee (free from your own house/property) |
| Sell from garage | Garage menu → Sell | Refunds to **bank** |
| Repair/refuel in garage | Garage menu → Repair | Litres needed × $6 + $150, from **bank** |
| Chop a vehicle | Chop Shop (Kavala / Athira) | Pays **cash** |
| Claim a vehicle | Chop Shop | Pays the claim price from **bank** |
| Recover a wreck | Scroll a wreck with a Wreckage Recovery Kit | Consumes 1 kit ($1,000) |
| Roadside refuel | Near a gas pump | $6 per litre, from **bank** |
| Garage vehicle cap | Per garage | **45 vehicles** |

---

### Buying a vehicle

**Where:** Dealer NPCs stand at motor pools and garages in towns across Altis (Kavala, Athira, Pyrgos, Sofia, and more). Look at the NPC and scroll to open the shop. Depending on the dealer you'll see one or more of:

- **Car Shop** ("LoonyBin Autos") — standard ground vehicles
- **Helicopter Shop** ("LoonyBin Air")
- **Boat Shop** ("LoonyBin Boat Marina")
- **Truck Shop** ("LoonyBin Truck")
- **Go-Kart Shop** ("LoonyBin Karts")
- **RV Shop** ("LoonyBin RV") — box trucks/RVs, found at the Chop Shop NPCs
- **Bounty Hunter Car/Helicopter Shop** — requires a Bounty Hunter license
- **Rebel Car Shop / New Drachma Car Shop** — at rebel outposts (requires a Rebel license); New Drachma vehicles are paid for in ND instead of cash
- **Turf Car Shop** — appears when your gang owns a turf zone
- **Gang Fortress vehicle shops** — appear when your gang holds the Gang Fortress
- **TT Motorpool / TT Air / TT Boat Shop** — Trauma Team only (rank-gated)
- **MaxTac Garage / SRT Vehicle Shop / Cop Car & Helicopter Shop** — police only (rank/role-gated)
- **Firefighter Garage** — active firefighters only

**How to buy:**
1. Scroll the dealer and open the shop.
2. Pick a vehicle from the list (the panel shows its stats).
3. Pick a **paint color/skin** from the color dropdown.
4. (Optional) tick the **alarm** checkbox (+$500) and set any buy-time **mods** in the Configure grid (see below). The Total readout updates live.
5. Click **Purchase**. The vehicle spawns on the dealer's pad with a **full tank of fuel**.

**Requirements & costs:**
- Most civilian shops need no license. **Rebel** shops need a Rebel license; **Bounty Hunter** shops need a Bounty Hunter license.
- **Governor tax** is added to cash purchases (a percentage set by the current Governor, 0–15%). The tax amount is shown in your purchase confirmation and is paid into the government/gang fund. Rebel and New Drachma shops are **not** taxed.
- **Gang Fortress discount:** buying while your gang owns and stands inside the Gang Fortress zone gives **15% off**.
- **Lifetime-quest discounts:** completing certain lifetime quests unlocks small standing discounts (roughly 2–5% off specific vehicle classes — e.g. sport hatchbacks, aircraft, armored trucks). These stack automatically.
- **MaxTac and Firefighter** duty vehicles cost **$0**.

**Garage cap:** each garage holds a maximum of **45 vehicles**. If you're at the cap, sell or scrap something before buying more. (MaxTac, SRT, and Firefighter duty vehicles do **not** count against your garage.)

**Fleet limits (pulled at once):**
- **MaxTac** vehicles: unlimited pulls.
- **Firefighter** and **SRT** vehicles: max **3** of that type out at a time.
- The **Madrid/Marid APC** (armored): only **1** can be out at a time, server-wide.

---

### Vehicle mods & upgrades

You can add mods **at purchase** (the Configure grid in the shop) or later at a garage. Prices are identical either way and are **flat** — they are **not** taxed or discounted.

| Mod | Options & price |
|---|---|
| **Car Alarm** (buy checkbox) | +$500 — alerts you if someone tampers with the vehicle |
| **Trunk Storage** | +50 storage $5,000 · +75 storage $7,500 · +100 storage $10,000 |
| **Handling** | Suspension Mod $3,500 · Brakes + Suspension $7,500 |
| **Horn** | ~49 horn sounds, $5,000 each (Stock horn is free) |
| **Nitrous** | $10,000 — adds a nitro boost (see below) |
| **Tracker** | $10,000 — lets you track the vehicle on your map |
| **Hardened Locks** | Basic $10,000 · Medium $20,000 · Advanced $30,000 — resist lockpicking |
| **Doors** | $35,000 — **LSV/Prowler/Qilin-type vehicles only** |
| **Autopilot Module** | $30,000 — **ground vehicles only**; lets the vehicle self-drive |

Notes and gotchas:
- **Autopilot** is free at the dealer if you have the Motorist "Autopilot Certified" talent.
- **Doors** only appear as an option on LSV-type vehicles; **Autopilot** only on ground vehicles. The option is greyed out otherwise.
- Duty vehicles (MaxTac/SRT/Firefighter) are temporary and cannot be modded.
- Horns are triggered with your horn keybind once installed.

---

### Your personal garage

Garage NPCs offer **Vehicle Garage**, **Helicopter Garage**, **Boat Garage** (matching the vehicle domain), plus a **Store vehicle in Garage** action. Some dealers combine shop + garage in one NPC.

#### Storing a vehicle
Drive/park your vehicle near the garage NPC, get out, and scroll **"Store vehicle in Garage."**
- The vehicle must be **yours** (or your gang's) and reasonably close (within ~40m, generous enough for big airframes like the Blackfish).
- **You can't store while in or near combat** — if you or a nearby player has been aggressive in the last 60 seconds, the garage clerk "cowers in fright." Wait a couple minutes.
- **Illegal cargo** (drugs/contraband) is stripped when you store; **legal cargo is kept** (civilians). Police vehicles must be fully emptied first.
- **Duty vehicles (MaxTac/SRT/Firefighter) cannot be stored** — they'd be lost. Just abandon them.

#### Pulling a vehicle out
Scroll the garage → **Vehicle/Helicopter/Boat Garage** → select the vehicle → **Get/Pull**.
- **Pull fee:** each vehicle has a pull fee, paid from your money. Examples: Hatchback **$150**, most sport cars **$235**, Offroad **$250**, SUV **$200**, many trucks **$700–$1,750**, helicopters **$750–$2,500**. Karts and quads are free.
- **Pulling from your own house/property garage is FREE** (no pull fee).
- You **cannot pull a vehicle that is already spawned** in the world ("This vehicle is already in use").
- The spawn pad must be **clear** — if a car/heli/boat is blocking it you'll get "There is a vehicle currently blocking the spawn point."
- Police note: an undercover-only role restriction applies to marked cop vehicles (they can only be pulled in the undercover role).

#### Selling a vehicle back
Garage menu → select → **Sell Vehicle** → confirm.
- Refund equals the vehicle's **sell-back value**, paid to your **bank**.
- Examples of sell-back: Hatchback **$500**, Offroad **$1,500**, sport hatchback **$2,250**, SUV **$1,500**.
- The vehicle **must be stored** (not out in the world) to sell it.

#### Repair & refuel at the garage
Garage menu → select → **Repair/Refuel**.
- Cost = **(litres needed to fill) × $6 + $150 flat**, paid from your **bank**.
- You can only use this if the tank is **below ~90%** or the vehicle is **above 75% damaged** — a nearly-full, barely-scratched vehicle is refused.

---

### Gang garage

If you're in a gang, garage NPCs also show a **Gang Garage** option (ground/air/boat variants). Gang vehicles are pooled and shared by the gang.

- **Rank gate:** only gang **ranks 1–3** can pull, store, send, or destroy gang vehicles. Ranks 4–5 are blocked.
- **Send a personal vehicle to the gang garage:** from the personal garage menu, this costs **35% of the vehicle's sell-back value** (paid from your **bank**) and is **irreversible** — the vehicle becomes gang property.
- Storing, pulling, repair/refuel, and the 45-vehicle cap work the same as personal garages.
- Any online gang member with a key can drive a pulled gang vehicle.

---

### Keys & locks (Keychain)

Every vehicle you buy is keyed to you. Keys let others drive/unlock it.

- **Lock/unlock:** look at the vehicle and press **U** to lock or unlock it (the buy message reminds you of this).
- **Keychain app:** open your phone → **Keychain** (also available to everyone). It lists your vehicles with their distance and, for idle vehicles, a **despawn countdown** tooltip.
- **Give keys:** in the Keychain, select a vehicle and a nearby player (they must be in your group, your gang, in the same vehicle, or within ~12m on foot) → **Give Keys**. They can now unlock and drive it.
- **Drop key:** removes a vehicle from your keychain (you give up your copy of the key). If you do this while standing at the impound lot as the vehicle's original owner, it scraps the vehicle entirely. *(verify: exact impound-lot behavior)*
- **Open All Doors:** in the Keychain, opens doors/ramps on vehicles that have them (e.g. Ifrit, Blackfish).
- **Hardened Locks** mod makes your vehicle resist lockpicking (thieves need more time/tools).
- **Stealing:** a thief with a **Lockpick** can "Pick Vehicle Lock" on a locked vehicle that isn't theirs (works on cars/air/ships; slower against hardened locks). Once open they can drive it away — and take it to a Chop Shop.

---

### Chop Shop (scrapping vehicles for cash)

**Where:** two Chop Shops — one in **Kavala** and one in **Athira** (scroll the shady NPC → **Chop Shop**). The shop lists every eligible vehicle within **25m**.

**What it does:** permanently destroys a vehicle and pays **cash** — this is how you cash out **stolen** vehicles (you don't need to own it).

**How to chop:**
1. Park/leave the target vehicle within 25m of the Chop Shop NPC.
2. Scroll the NPC → **Chop Shop**, select the vehicle (its price is shown), and confirm.
3. Stand still by the vehicle for the ~**30-second** chopping progress. Don't move more than ~2m from where you started or more than ~22m from the vehicle, and stay out of vehicles / off the ground.

**Payout:**
- Base payout = the vehicle's **sell value** (same figure as garage sell-back), indexed by the vehicle's faction. Examples: Hatchback **$500**, Offroad **$1,500**, Ifrit **~$70,000**, Strider **~$27,500**.
- **Sought-after vehicles** pay a **+10% bonus** (shown in the confirmation message).
- **Outlaw gang perks** stack on top: **Chop Artist +20%**, **Master Thief +15%** (if the chopper has them).
- Payout goes to your **cash on hand**.

**What can be chopped:** the vehicle must be registered ("DMV-registered") and be a civilian, police, or gang vehicle. **Cannot chop:** MaxTac vehicles, TT firefighting vehicles, airdropped vehicles, or the jet.

**Gotcha — chop trace:** chopping leaves a temporary trace on you that police can act on. It clears once you get rid of the chop cash (spend/deposit it), or automatically after ~200 seconds once your cash hits $0. Launder the money quickly.

---

### Claiming a vehicle

At the Chop Shop you can also **Claim** a civilian vehicle instead of chopping it — this transfers ownership to **you** (it goes into your garage) rather than destroying it.

- Select the vehicle → **Claim** → confirm. It costs the listed **claim price** (paid from your **bank**), then runs a ~**60-second** progress (same movement rules as chopping).
- **Free claim:** if your gang owns the **Zaros** turf, claiming is **$0**.
- Claiming **transfers the keys to you** and revokes everyone else's keys.
- **Can only claim:** civilian-side vehicles. **Cannot claim:** MaxTac, TT firefighting, airdropped vehicles, the jet, or a vehicle that's already yours.

---

### Wreckage Recovery Kit (getting a blown-up vehicle back)

**Important:** this server has **no vehicle insurance** — when your owned vehicle is destroyed, its garage record is **removed**. If you do nothing, that vehicle is **gone forever**. A **Wreckage Recovery Kit** is the way to get it back.

- **Buy it:** **$1,000** each at the **Gas Station Market** and the general **Market** (item: "Wreckage Recovery Kit"). It weighs the same as a First Aid Kit and is consumed on use.
- **Use it:** with a kit in your inventory, walk up to the **wreck** and scroll the green **"Recover Wreckage"** action.
- Stand by the wreck for the ~**10-second** progress; stay within ~8m and on foot.
- On success, the wreck is returned to its **owner's garage** (personal or gang), repaired and with an empty trunk, and **1 kit is consumed**.

**Requirements & gotchas:**
- The wreck must have an **ownership record** — i.e. it was a garage/dealer vehicle. Random/unowned wrecks can't be recovered (you're warned *before* the kit is used, so you never waste one).
- It returns the vehicle to **whoever owned it**, not necessarily to you — so you can recover a friend's or gang's downed vehicle for them.
- Works on cars, aircraft, and boats.

---

### Slingload & cargo (IgiLoad)

Bigger transport vehicles can carry other vehicles/cargo as a load (the IgiLoad system), so you can haul a car in a box truck or sling a vehicle under a transport heli.

- **Supported carriers include:** Offroads, box/transport Vans, HEMTT/Zamak/Tempest trucks, transport helicopters (Mohawk-class), the Blackfish, and boats.
- **How:** look at a supported carrier and scroll for **"Load cargo on …"** (some carriers offer left/right loading), then **"Unload cargo from …"** to drop it. Helicopters and the Blackfish also offer **"Unload cargo with parachute"** / **"Unload ALL cargo with parachute"** to air-drop the load.
- Carriers have limited cargo slots (small offroad least, HEMTT/Blackfish most).
- Pilots/drivers can toggle **"Enable/Disable loading for Co-Pilot"** and **"Enable/Disable loading from outside."**
- Loaded cargo adds mass — a heavily loaded aircraft handles accordingly.

---

### On the road: fuel, repair, unflip, push

- **Refuel at a gas pump:** stand next to a fuel pump (out of the vehicle, engine off) and use the refuel action. Costs **$6 per litre** from your **bank**; fills to full. You can also carry a **Full Fuel Can** (~$75) to top up anywhere.
- **Repair Vehicle:** scroll a vehicle within 5m while on foot → **Repair Vehicle**. You need a **Toolkit** in your inventory. (Also available from the Vehicle Interaction Menu.)
- **Unflip Vehicle:** scroll a flipped/stuck vehicle → **Unflip Vehicle**.
- **Push Vehicle:** scroll an aircraft or boat you own → **Push** to nudge it. Boats also have **"Push Boat"** in the interaction menu.
- **Vehicle Interaction Menu:** offers Repair, Unflip/Push, and (for karts) "Get In Kart." Police see extra options here — Registration check, Search Vehicle, Pull Out Players, and **Impound/Seize** (a cop can impound or seize your vehicle, removing it from the world).
- **Activate Nitro:** if the vehicle has the Nitrous mod, scroll **"Activate Nitro"** while driving for a speed boost.

---

### Pay 'n' Spray (custom skins)

A **Pay 'n' Spray** NPC (near the Pyrgos commodities area) sells **permanent vehicle skins**. Buy a skin once and you can then **apply it at any garage** to matching vehicles. Skin prices vary by skin. *(verify: exact per-skin prices)*

*(Note: the handheld "Spray Can" item is for tagging graffiti on the ground/walls, not for repainting vehicles — pick your vehicle color/skin at purchase or via Pay 'n' Spray.)*

---

### Tips & gotchas

- **No insurance:** a destroyed owned vehicle is deleted from your garage. Keep a couple of **Wreckage Recovery Kits** ($1,000) on hand if you're driving something expensive.
- **New vehicles spawn full of fuel.** Don't pay to refuel a brand-new purchase.
- **Pulling from your own house is free** — no pull fee at property garages.
- **Chop = cash, Sell = bank.** Chopping also works on cars you don't own; selling only works on your own stored vehicles.
- **Chop trace:** don't sit on freshly-chopped cash near police — spend or bank it.
- **45-vehicle cap per garage.** Sell or scrap to make room.
- **Combat blocks storing** — you can't store a vehicle if you or someone nearby has been aggressive in the last minute.
- **Gang vehicles need rank 1–3** to pull/store/send/destroy.
- **Duty vehicles (MaxTac/SRT/Firefighter) can't be stored, chopped, or claimed** — they're disposable.
- **Lock your vehicle (U key)** so thieves can't just drive off. Add **Hardened Locks** for extra protection, and a **Tracker** so you can find it on the map if it's stolen.

---

## Housing: Buy, Store, Upgrade, Grow

Owning property is the backbone of the civilian economy: a house is your locked storage locker, your drug farm, your distillery, your crypto-mine, and your spawn point. Everything below is done at the building itself through the green **House Menu** scroll-wheel option (walk up to any house and scroll), except paying tax, which is done from your phone.

---

### Getting Started: The Home Owner's License

You cannot buy, open, or even see the House Menu on an owned building without a **Home Owner's License**. Buy it from the License section of a civilian shop before you go house-hunting. You must also be a **civilian** (cops and Trauma Team cannot own homes).

---

### Buying a House

**Where:** Walk up to any *unowned* building, aim at it, and scroll to **House Menu** (green text). You must be within **10m**. If the building is for sale, you get a purchase prompt.

**Requirements:**
- Home Owner's License
- Enough money on-hand **or** in your bank (the game pays from cash first, then ATM/bank)
- You may own at most **5 buildings total**
- You may own at most **2 industrial sheds (warehouses)**

**Buyable buildings and their base price / sell-back price:**

| Building | Buy | Sell to Bank |
|---|---|---|
| Slum shack (wooden) | $20,000 | $15,000 |
| Stone Shed / small Addon | $40,000 | $30,000 |
| Garage (damaged) | $50,000 | $30,000 |
| Garage | $60,000 | $35,000 |
| Small house / shop | $70,000 | $52,500 |
| Stone House (small) | $120,000 | $90,000 |
| Big house | $150,000 | $112,500 |
| Industrial Shed (warehouse) | $200,000 | $150,000 |

On a fresh purchase you also pay a **government sales tax** on top of the price. The rate is set by the current Governor and ranges from **0% to 15%** — the confirmation box shows the exact tax before you commit.

**Step by step:**
1. Get your Home Owner's License and enough money.
2. Find an empty building and scroll **House Menu** within 10m.
3. Read the price + tax in the pop-up, click **Purchase**.
4. The "Altis Realtor Group" processes the sale and the house becomes yours.

**Where you CANNOT buy:**
- Within **500m** of any cartel zone (Arms, Drug, Oil, Wong's)
- Within **150m** of the Meth Lab
- Within **40m** of the Spirit Distillery
- Any building that is too damaged (over 15% damage) — wait for a server restart to repair it

**Cooldowns / gotchas:**
- You cannot rapid-fire purchases: there's roughly a **60-second** cooldown between buys.
- A recently-sold house is locked for a short while ("still being processed") before it can change hands again.
- Buying from another player's market listing (see Selling) runs through the same menu; the price is whatever they listed.

---

### Buying From the Player Market

Other players can list their houses for sale. Listed houses show up as map markers. Walk to one and use **House Menu** to buy it directly from the owner (ownership transfers to you). Sellers set the price anywhere from **$1,000** to **$4,999,999**.

---

### The House Menu (your control panel)

Once you own a building, **House Menu** opens a full management panel showing the house's storage capacity, installed upgrades, max keys, market-listing status and total value. From here you (the owner) can:

- **View Storage** — open the house's inventory (also available to keyholders)
- **Lock / Unlock All doors**
- **Turn Lights On/Off** and pick a light color (Regular, Red, Blue, Aqua, Green, Orange, Yellow, Pink)
- **Upgrade Virtual / Physical storage**
- **Manage Keys / View Key Holders**
- **Add Aging Barrel / Greenhouse / Lead Container**
- **List for Sale / Remove Listing / Sell to Bank**
- **Rename** the house (type a name in the field, click the rename button)
- **Malden Makeover** — re-skin eligible houses to a different color/look (cosmetic)
- **Set / Change / Remove Gang House** (if you're a gang leader)
- **Homegoods Store** — buy furniture (see below)

You must be within **10m** to interact (extended to **25m** with the Open House talent — see Talents). **Garages** are special: instead of storage/lights they give **Access Garage** and **Store Vehicle** options, and they cannot take a barrel, greenhouse or lead container.

---

### Storage: Virtual vs Physical

Every house has two separate lockers, opened via **View Storage**:

- **Virtual storage** — holds virtual/"Y-inventory" items (drugs, crops, crafting materials, food, gold, etc.). Measured in **virtual capacity (vcap)**.
- **Physical storage** — holds physical gear: weapons, magazines, uniforms, vests, backpacks and physical items. Measured in **physical capacity**.

Both start small at purchase and can be upgraded. You (and anyone you give an inventory key to) can deposit and withdraw. You can also move loot straight between a nearby vehicle trunk and the house storage.

**Overdue tax locks you out:** if a house's tax is more than 14 days overdue, its storage is sealed ("house tax has to be paid to gain access again") until you pay.

---

### Storage Upgrades

**Where:** House Menu → **Upgrade Virtual Storage** / **Upgrade Physical Storage** (owner only).

Each house type has a fixed ladder of capacity tiers. You buy them in order; the menu charges the next tier's price and tells you the new capacity. Sample ladders (base you get at purchase → each paid upgrade):

| Building | Virtual tiers (cap @ cost) | Physical tiers (cap @ cost) |
|---|---|---|
| Small house | 500 → 1000 ($10k) → 2000 ($20k) | 200 → 325 ($15k) → 500 ($35k) |
| Big house | 500 → 1500 ($15k) → 3000 ($30k) | 200 → 650 ($35k) → 900 ($55k) |
| Stone House | 500 → 1500 ($15k) → 3000 ($30k) | 200 → 500 ($30k) → 750 ($60k) |
| Stone Shed / Addon | 500 → 700 ($7k) → 1000 ($10k) | 200 → 250 ($10k) → 300 ($20k) |
| Garage | 175 → 250 ($5k) → 325 ($7.5k) | 125 → 175 ($8k) → 250 ($15k) |
| Industrial Shed | 500 → 1500 ($15k) → 3000 ($30k) → 5000 ($50k) | 200 → 700 ($50k) → 900 ($100k) → 1260 ($150k) |

The **Industrial Shed is the biggest stash on the server** (up to 5,000 virtual / 1,260 physical). There's roughly a **10-second** cooldown between upgrade actions. Cost comes from cash first, then bank.

> Tip: virtual capacity also sets your farm sizes — greenhouse plant cap = vcap ÷ 10, aging-barrel cap = vcap ÷ 30. Bigger virtual storage = bigger grow op.

---

### House Keys & Keyholders

**Where:** House Menu → **Manage Keys** (owner only).

You can hand out spare keys to other players so they can use your storage and/or farms. Each keyholder gets one of three access levels:
- **Both** — inventory access *and* can lock/unlock doors
- **Inv** — inventory (storage/farm) access only
- **Keys** — door lock/unlock only

**How to give a key:** open Manage Keys, select a nearby player (must be within ~15–20m), choose their access level from the dropdown, click Add.

**Spare-key slots cost money to cut.** A new house starts with limited key capacity; buy more slots (max **5 keys** total per house) at escalating prices:

| Key # | 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cost | $10,000 | $25,000 | $50,000 | $75,000 | $100,000 |

**Notes:**
- Keyholders **cannot** spawn at or sell your building — only the owner can.
- **Revoke access** by removing a single keyholder, or **Change the Locks** to instantly void *all* existing keys at once.
- Greenhouse and aging barrel are usable by anyone with an inventory key, not just the owner.

---

### House Tax (upkeep)

Every owned building accrues property tax over real time. Ignore it too long and you get locked out of the house.

**Where to pay:** open your phone → **Housing** app (only appears when you're a civilian who owns at least one house). This opens the House Taxes menu, where you can **Pay Selected** or **Pay All**. Tax is always paid from your **bank**, and only when the server isn't mid-restart.

**How it's calculated:** `days since last paid × upkeep rate × reduction multiplier`.
- **Upkeep rate per house type:** small house 300, stone house 500, big house 675, industrial shed 1375, garage / stone shed / addon / slum 275, damaged garage 275.
- **Reduction multiplier (0.25–2.0):** the *more you play*, the cheaper your tax (playtime is tracked while you're online, +5 min per 5 min). Owning *more houses* pushes the multiplier up. Active players with one house can drop as low as **0.25×**; someone who owns 5 houses and rarely logs in can pay up to **2.0×**.
- **Overdue penalty:** a house is flagged **Overdue** after **14 days** unpaid. If it's overdue *and* more than 32 days behind, a **+20%** surcharge is added.
- You can pay a house once it's at least **1 day** since the last payment.

**Lockout:** an overdue house (>14 days) blocks storage access until you pay. You'll also get a warning message on login and a phone reminder when houses need attention.

> There is a Homesteader talent that cuts all property tax by 40% — see Talents.

---

### Aging Barrel — Age Spirits at Home

**What it is:** a house upgrade that turns your property into a distillery cellar; new-make spirits mature into valuable aged liquor in real time, even while you're offline.

**Install:** House Menu → **Add Aging Barrel**. Cost **$35,000**. Not available on garages.

**How to use** (owner or inventory-keyholder, standing within ~12m of the house):
1. Distill **new-make (stage-0) spirits** at a distillery first — New Make Scotch, White Dog Corn, White Dog Rye, or New Make Rum.
2. Scroll **Lay Down Spirits to Age** at the house — it lays down every new-make bottle you're carrying, up to capacity.
3. Come back later and scroll **Collect Aging Barrel** to pull out the aged bottles. Scroll **Check Aging Barrel** any time to see stages and timers.

**Aging rules:**
- Each stage takes **6 hours**; it takes **12 hours total** to fully age. Stages: 0 = New Make → 1 = the named spirit (Scotch / Corn / Rye / Rum) → 2 = the top tier ("15 Year" / Aged / Reserve).
- Aging continues while you're logged off. You can collect at any time and you get whatever stage it's currently at.
- **Capacity = virtual storage ÷ 30 units.** Each bottle weighs 3, so bring free inventory weight to collect (you pull `floor(free weight ÷ 3)` bottles at a time).
- The final "25 Year / Reserve" step is done separately at a processor with aging chemicals — the barrel only takes you to stage 2.

---

### Greenhouse — Grow Weed at Home

**What it is:** a house upgrade that lets you grow cannabis on-site; plants mature in real time, even offline.

**Install:** House Menu → **Add Greenhouse**. Cost **$45,000**. Not available on garages.

**How to use** (owner or inventory-keyholder, within ~12m):
1. Buy **Sativa seeds** and/or **Indica seeds** from any **Drug Dealer** — **$100 each** (seeds are the *only* thing drug dealers sell; everything else there is sell-only).
2. Scroll **Plant Sativa Seeds** or **Plant Indica Seeds** at the house — it plants as many seeds as you're carrying, up to capacity.
3. Wait for them to mature (they grow while you're offline), then scroll **Harvest Greenhouse**. Scroll **Check Greenhouse** any time for growth stages and how many are ready.

**Grow rules:**
- **Sativa** matures in **2 hours** and yields **Weed**.
- **Indica** matures in **3 hours** and yields **High-Potency Weed** (worth more).
- **Capacity = virtual storage ÷ 10 plants.** A maxed small house (2,000 vcap) fits 200 plants; a maxed industrial shed (5,000 vcap) fits 500.
- Harvest fills your free carry weight (each bud weighs 1), Sativa first then Indica — clear inventory room before harvesting.

---

### Lead Container — Store Weapon-Grade Uranium

**What it is:** a specialist house upgrade that lets you safely stockpile far more **Uranium (Weapon Grade)** at your property. Install via House Menu → **Add Lead Container**. Cost **$250,000**. Not available on garages. This is the priciest storage upgrade on a home and is aimed at high-end uranium runners.

---

### Furniture & the Homegoods Store

**What it is:** decorate and kit out your property with placeable furniture that persists across restarts.

**Where to buy:** House Menu → **Homegoods Store**. This opens a virtual shop with ~65 items — beds, chairs, tables, sofas, TVs, fridges, PCs and gaming setups, satellite dishes, lamps/floodlights/glow lights, rugs, plants, kitchen units, a firepit, a (prop) CCTV camera, deer stands, crates and more. Prices range from a few hundred dollars (chairs, sleeping bags ~$250–450) up to about **$25,000** for a Wooden Double Bed (Sofa ~$9,000, Flat Screen TV ~$18,000, prop Security Camera ~$4,500).

**How to place:** buy the piece (it goes into your Y inventory), then place it. Furniture can only be placed **within 20m of a house you own**, and there's a hard cap of **20 furniture pieces per house**.

**On placed furniture** (scroll-wheel, at your own house):
- **Move** — pick it up and re-place it somewhere else
- **Delete** — returns it to your Y inventory (composition pieces like helipad lamps can only be **Scrapped**, no refund)
- Beds add **Lay Down** / **Get Up** so you can lie on them

Some furniture (lamps, floodlights, glow lights, firepit) actually emits light.

---

### Shed Helipad — Rooftop Air Access

**What it is:** a construction upgrade that builds a working helipad on the roof of an industrial shed, so your air vehicles become pullable and storable right at the shed.

**Install:** on an industrial shed you own, open House Menu → **Build Helipad** button. Cost **$1,000,000**. Sheds only, one per shed, **permanent — no refund** (it's construction, not furniture).

**Notes:**
- Once built, the House Menu is reachable from up on the deck, and storage/vehicle reach is measured flat so standing on the raised pad still counts.
- The pad stands whenever you (or your gang, for a gang shed) are online.

---

### Mining Rig — Passive LoonyCoin Income

**What it is:** install a crypto-mining rig in a house you own and it mines **LoonyCoin around the clock**, even while you're logged off.

**How to get a rig:**
1. Collect the **8 crypto components** (CPU, GPU, PSU, Storage, Motherboard, RAM, Cooler, Case). One component drops in a hardware case at **each Federal Reserve event** — grab it via **Grab Component** (within 5m) before it despawns (~10 min).
2. When you have all 8, scroll **Assemble Mining Rig** to build one rig.
3. At a house you own, scroll **Install Mining Rig** (within 6m) — one rig per house.

**Collecting:** scroll **Collect LoonyCoin** at the house (owner or keyholder). Then sell the LoonyCoin — it's **sell-only at the Blackmarket** (base around **$6,000 each**; the dynamic market can shift the price).

**Rig rules:**
- Mines **2 LoonyCoin per hour**.
- Accrual is **capped at 96 coins (48 hours)** — it's not a log-off-forever printer, so collect regularly.
- The rig is loud enough that a nosy cop might notice.

---

### Selling, Listing, Renaming & Cosmetics

- **Sell to Bank** — instant sale for the fixed sell-back price in the table above. You can't sell the exact same house twice in a row (logged as an exploit attempt).
- **List for Sale / Remove Listing** — put your house on the player market for **$1,000–$4,999,999**; it appears as a map marker for other players to buy. Enter **0** to de-list.
- **Rename** — give your house a custom name.
- **Lights** — toggle interior lights and choose from 8 colors.
- **Malden Makeover** — re-skin eligible house types to a different color variant (cosmetic only; owner only).
- **Lock / Unlock All doors** — secure the building.

---

### Homesteader Talents (money-savers)

Spend civilian talent points on the **Homesteader** tree to make property ownership much cheaper:

| Talent | Effect |
|---|---|
| **Homesteader** | Property tax on all your homes reduced by **40%** (unlocks the tree) |
| **First-Time Buyer** | **20% off** house/shed purchases (confirmed in code for player-listed market buys) |
| **Landlord** | All storage upgrades (virtual and physical) cost **35% less** |
| **Locksmith** | House keys cost **50% less** to cut |
| **Open House** | Access house/shed storage from up to **25m** away instead of 10m — load the truck from the driveway |

---

### Quick Reference

| Thing | Cost / Value |
|---|---|
| Aging Barrel upgrade | $35,000 |
| Greenhouse upgrade | $45,000 |
| Lead Container upgrade | $250,000 |
| Shed Helipad | $1,000,000 (permanent) |
| Weed seeds (Drug Dealer) | $100 each |
| Spare keys (1→5) | $10k / $25k / $50k / $75k / $100k |
| Max houses owned | 5 (max 2 industrial sheds) |
| Max keys per house | 5 |
| Furniture cap per house | 20 pieces |
| Greenhouse plant cap | virtual storage ÷ 10 |
| Aging barrel unit cap | virtual storage ÷ 30 |
| Sativa mature / Indica mature | 2h / 3h |
| Spirit aging | 6h per stage, 12h to fully age |
| Mining rig rate / cap | 2 LoonyCoin/hr, cap 96 (48h) |
| Tax overdue threshold | 14 days (+20% if >32 days behind) |
| Storage reach | 10m (25m with Open House talent) |

---

## Gangs & loonstreets

A gang is a persistent, named crew you own with other civilians: it gives you a shared spawn point (your gang house), a shared bank, a shared vehicle garage, gang-wide perks, formal rivalries ("wars"), and a spot on the public leaderboard. Everything below is civilian-only — cops and Trauma Team cannot create or join gangs.

### What you get for being in a gang
- A shared **gang bank** account, separate from your personal bank.
- A **gang house** you can all spawn at and store gear in.
- A **gang garage** that holds up to **45** vehicles the whole crew can pull.
- **Gang perks** that discount purchases and boost payouts for every member.
- One shared group, so gang side-chat shows your gang name and members get a marker over their heads.
- A public scoreboard on **loonstreets.com** (see the last section).

---

### Creating a gang

**Where:** Open your phone (default **Y**) and tap the **My Gang** app.

**Requirements:**
- You must be a **civilian**.
- You must **not** already be in a gang (leave or disband your current one first).
- You must **not** be in **passive mode** (turn it off in your phone settings first).
- You need at least **$25,000 in your bank account** (not cash on hand). The $25,000 is charged from your bank.

**Steps:**
1. Phone → **My Gang**. If you're not in a gang, you'll be asked "Create one for $25,000?" — confirm.
2. Type a gang name and hit **Create**.
3. Name rules: **3–25 characters**, letters and numbers only (no symbols, and names that look like script commands are rejected). The name must be **unique** — if another gang already uses it, you'll be told to pick another.
4. Once created you become the **gang leader (rank 1)**, a gang group is formed, and $25,000 leaves your bank.

**Tip:** Names are checked for uniqueness on the server, so if creation seems to "hang," you may have picked a taken name — try a different one.

---

### Ranks & permissions

Ranks run from **1 (Leader) at the top down to 5 (Recruit) at the bottom** — a **lower number means more power**. New members join at **rank 5**.

- **Rank 1 — Leader.** Full control: disband, transfer leadership/ownership, promote/demote, invite, kick, set the gang tag, declare/end wars, and full bank access (deposit, withdraw, transfer). The leader is also the in-game group leader.
- **Rank 2 — Officer.** Can promote, demote, kick (anyone ranked below them), invite, set the gang tag, use the gang bank including **withdraw** and **transfer**, and access gang-house storage.
- **Rank 3 — Recruiter.** Can **invite** new members and **kick recruits (rank 5) only**. Can open the gang bank to **deposit/check balance** (but not withdraw or transfer), and can **store and pull gang vehicles**.
- **Rank 4 — Member.** A standard member. Cannot invite, cannot use the gang bank at all, and **cannot pull gang vehicles**.
- **Rank 5 — Recruit.** The default rank on joining; same access level as rank 4.

**Key gates to remember:**
- **Gang bank** can only be opened by ranks **1–3**. Only ranks **1–2** can withdraw or transfer funds.
- **Gang vehicles** can only be stored/pulled/repaired by ranks **1–3**.
- **War** can only be declared/accepted/surrendered by ranks **1–3**.
- **Gang perks** can only be edited by ranks **1–2**.
- **Gang tag** can only be set by ranks **1–2**.

---

### Inviting, joining, and leaving

**Inviting (ranks 1–3):**
1. Phone → **My Gang** → **Invite**.
2. Pick a player from the list (only alive civilians who aren't already in a gang appear).
3. They get an accept/decline popup. If they accept, they join at **rank 5**.

Notes: You can't invite someone who's already in a gang. The invited player must not be in passive mode when they accept.

**Joining:** When invited, accept the popup. You'll be pulled into the gang group automatically **unless you're already in a temporary group** — in that case, leave your current group first so the game can place you in the gang group.

**Leaving:** Phone → **My Gang** → **Leave** (and confirm). You **cannot leave while you are the acting leader** — you must appoint a new leader or disband first.

**Kicking:** Phone → **My Gang**, select a member, hit **Kick**. You can only kick members ranked **below** you, and a **rank 3 recruiter can only kick recruits (rank 5)**.

**Disbanding (leader only):** Phone → **My Gang** → **Disband** (and confirm). This wipes the gang from the database, breaks up the group, and releases the gang house. There is no undo.

---

### Promoting, demoting, and handing over the gang

**Promote / Demote:** Phone → **My Gang**, select a member, use **Promote** or **Demote**. You can only change ranks **below your own**, and you can't move someone to a rank equal to or above you. You cannot change your own rank.

**Transfer Leadership (leader only):** Hands the leader role to a chosen member; you drop to officer (rank 2). This also **moves the gang house across all servers** to the new leader. You keep membership but lose control unless it's handed back.

**Transfer Ownership (leader only):** A separate leader-only handover option in the menu.

---

### Gang tag

**Who:** Ranks **1–2**. **Where:** Phone → **My Gang** → **Set Tag**.

Enter a tag of **up to 4 characters**. It appears **over your gang members' heads** and in **kill-feed messages**. The tags **"MSEC"** and **"TT"** are not allowed (those are reserved for police and Trauma Team).

---

### The gang bank

A shared account, separate from personal money. There is **no practical cap on how much the gang bank can hold** — deposits are unlimited.

**Where / how to open it:** Walk up to any **ATM**, look at it (within ~3 m), and use the yellow **"Gang Account"** scroll-wheel action. Only ranks **1–3** can open it. There's a short cooldown between opens.

**Deposit (ranks 1–3):**
- Type an amount and deposit, or use **Deposit All** to dump all your cash-on-hand in.
- Minimum: any amount above $0. Maximum per transaction: **$9,999,999**.

**Withdraw (ranks 1–2 only):**
- Minimum **$100**, maximum **$9,999,999** per transaction.
- There's a deliberate **few-second delay** on withdrawals to prevent exploits — this is normal, wait for it to finish.

**Transfer to a member (ranks 1–2 only):**
- Send gang funds straight to a specific gang member's **bank**.
- Minimum **$1,000**, maximum **$9,999,999** per transaction. You confirm the recipient and amount before it sends.
- The recipient gets it whether they're online or offline.

**History:** The gang bank keeps a log of deposits, withdrawals, and transfers that you can review from the bank menu.

**Cooldowns/gotchas:** Wait a few seconds between bank actions — spamming deposit/withdraw is rate-limited and will tell you to slow down. If you just robbed a store, you can't use ATMs (and therefore the gang bank) for 5 minutes.

---

### Gang houses (your HQ)

Your gang house is a house or industrial shed that a member already owns, converted into a shared HQ. It becomes a **spawn point** for the whole gang and gives access to shared storage. On the map it shows as a **green "Gang Building" marker**.

**Setting one (leader only):**
- You must **own** the building you want to convert, and your gang must **not already have a gang house**.
- Look at your owned house/shed and use the **convert-to-gang-building** action, then confirm.
- Once set, all online members get the green marker and can spawn there.

**Using it:** Members spawn at the gang house from the spawn-select screen and can access its storage and vehicle functions.

**Changing / removing it (leader only):** You can switch the gang house to a different owned building, or it's removed automatically if the leader sells the house. When leadership is transferred, the gang house moves with the new leader **across all servers**.

**Gotcha:** If you log in far away from the HQ, the green **map marker** shows immediately, but the building itself (for spawning/storage/vehicle pulls) only fully wires up once you're close enough for it to stream in. Get near the HQ if spawn/storage options don't appear right away.

---

### Gang vehicles (the Gang Garage)

A shared garage that holds up to **45 vehicles** the whole crew (ranks 1–3) can pull.

**Storing a vehicle (ranks 1–3):** At a garage building, house, or shed (within ~20 m), use the **"Gang Garage"** scroll action, pick the vehicle, and confirm.
- Cost to send a personal vehicle into the gang garage: **35% of that vehicle's base price**, paid from your **bank**.
- This is **irreversible** — the vehicle becomes gang property.
- If the garage is already at **45** vehicles, you can't add more.

**Pulling a vehicle (ranks 1–3):**
- Open the Gang Garage, select the vehicle, and pull it.
- You pay the vehicle's normal **pull fee** (varies by vehicle; free when pulling at your own property spawn).
- There's a built-in random delay of roughly a few seconds while it spawns, and the spawn point must be clear of other vehicles.
- Ranks **4–5 cannot pull gang vehicles** at all.

**Repair & refuel (in the Gang Garage):**
- Cost = **(liters of fuel needed × $6) + $150**, paid from your bank.
- Only available if the vehicle is below **90% fuel** or above **75% damage** — you can't top off an already-full, undamaged vehicle.

---

### Gang wars

A war is a formal, mutual rivalry between two gangs. It's mainly a **tracking-and-recognition** system, not a rules change — you can already fight other civilians without one.

**Declaring war (ranks 1–3):**
1. Phone → **My Gang** → **Wars**.
2. Select an enemy gang from the list (only gangs with online members appear) and hit **Challenge**.
3. A war challenge is sent to all online members of that gang.
4. You can only send a challenge **once every 5 minutes**.

**Accepting:** The challenged gang's ranks 1–3 open the same Wars menu and hit **Accept** on the challenging gang. The war then goes live for both sides.

**Ending:** Either side (ranks 1–3) can select the warring gang and **Surrender** to end it.

**What war actually does:**
- Enemy gang members get a red **gang-war icon over their heads** so you can spot them.
- Kills between the gangs show **"killed by enemy gang member"** in the kill feed.
- War kills count toward gang stats/**war rating** and a kill quest.
- In the Wars menu, rivals are labeled **[WARRING]** (red) or **[CHALLENGED]** while a challenge is pending.

---

### Gang XP & perks

Your gang earns **experience** from gang activity, and that XP unlocks **gang-wide perks** every member benefits from.

**Earning gang XP:**
- **Capturing a cartel** grants **+5 gang XP** each time.
- Gains to your **war rating** (from gang-war combat) also add to gang XP.

**Spending it (ranks 1–2 only):** Phone → **Perks** app. You get one perk point per XP milestone, up to a maximum of **9 points** (every perk). XP thresholds for each point:

| Points unlocked | Gang XP needed |
|---|---|
| 1 | 36 |
| 2 | 97 |
| 3 | 190 |
| 4 | 320 |
| 5 | 490 |
| 6 | 690 |
| 7 | 920 |
| 8 | 1,180 |
| 9 | 1,500 |

**The perks** (three branches of three; each higher tier needs the one before it):

*Rebel-shopping branch*
- **Gang Groupon** — all members get **10% off** at rebel markets.
- **Hagglers** — all members get **5% off** rebel clothing and equipment.
- **Offer He Can't Refuse** — all members get **5% off** rebel weapons.

*Selling branch*
- **Fence** — all members get **+5%** on sale prices at Wong's and drug dealers.
- **Kingpin** — an **additional +2%** on drug-dealer sales.
- **Bootlegger** — an **additional +2%** on Wong's sales.

*Utility branch*
- **Flyboys** — all members can spawn **air vehicles at turf regions your gang owns**.
- **Drug Pushers** — all members gather **cocaine, heroin, and cannabis faster**.
- **Distributors** — all members gather **ephedra/henbane faster**.

Perks apply to the **whole gang** the moment they're learned. Only ranks 1–2 can add or remove them.

---

### Loonstreets.com — the leaderboard

**Where:** Phone → **Browser** app → type or tap **loonstreets.com** (tagline: "the streets keep score").

Loonstreets is the island's public gang scoreboard. It shows three things:

- **Fortress Crown** — which gang currently holds the **Gang Fortress**, or "Unclaimed" if nobody does.
- **Turf** — every turf region and which gang holds it (or "unclaimed").
- **Top Crews** — the **top 8 gangs ranked by experience**, each listed with its **XP** and its **gang bank balance**. The #1 crew is highlighted (gold), #2 silver, #3 bronze.

**Refresh:** The board updates **every 5 minutes**, so recent captures and deposits take a few minutes to appear.

---

### Seeing who's online (the roster)

Press the **0 key** at any time (civilian or cop) to open the **online roster**: every gang that has members online — sorted by most members first — plus every active group/squad, each showing member names. Press **0** again to close it. Cops also have a **"Gangs Online"** phone app that opens the same roster.

---

### Quick reference / gotchas

- **Create a gang:** $25,000 from your **bank**, civilian, not in passive mode, name 3–25 letters/numbers.
- **Ranks:** 1 = Leader (most power) → 5 = Recruit (least). New joiners start at 5.
- **Gang bank:** open at an ATM ("Gang Account"), ranks 1–3 only; withdraw/transfer ranks 1–2 only. Deposit ≤ $9,999,999; withdraw $100–$9,999,999; transfer $1,000–$9,999,999. Unlimited holding.
- **Gang garage:** cap **45** vehicles; storing costs **35%** of base price and is permanent; repair = (fuel liters × $6) + $150. Ranks 1–3 only.
- **War:** ranks 1–3; one challenge per **5 minutes**; either side can surrender.
- **Perks:** unlocked by gang XP (cartel captures = +5 each), spent by ranks 1–2, max 9, benefit everyone.
- You can't leave as the acting **leader** — transfer leadership or disband first.
- If your gang house marker shows but you can't spawn/store there, get physically closer to the building so it loads in.

---

## Gang Fortress, Turf, Cartels

The three big gang-vs-gang territory systems on LooneyBin. All three reward your **gang** (not you personally) with income, perks, and bragging rights — and all three require you to be **in a gang**. Create or join one from your phone's **My Gang** app first.

Quick reference for what each system needs:

| System | Must be in a gang | Rebel license | Weapon out | On foot |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Cartels (Arms/Drug/Oil/Wong) | Yes | **Yes** | Yes (see blacklist) | Yes |
| Turf (Rodopoli/Zaros/Panagia) | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |
| Gang Fortress | Yes | No | Yes | Yes |

Bounty hunters **cannot** capture cartels. You can check live cartel/rubber status any time from your phone's **Captures** app.

---

### Cartels

**What it is:** Four permanent capturable zones your gang can own for ongoing income and perks.

**Where:** Look for the labeled markers on your map:
- **Arms Dealer**
- **Drug Cartel** (sits at a low coastal church near the waterline)
- **Oil Cartel** (out on the **Sea Fortress**, over water)
- **Wong Triad**

(Exact towns for each marker: verify.)

**Requirements to capture:**
- Be in a gang, hold the **Rebel license**, and be **on foot** (out of any vehicle).
- Have a **primary weapon that isn't blacklisted**. Blacklisted for capping: **SMG-02, PDW2000**, and holding only a binocular/rangefinder counts as "no weapon." Rifles, the SDAR, shotguns (including the sawed-off), and LMGs all count. The **Oil Cartel waives the weapon rule** entirely.
- You must **not** be a bounty hunter, restrained, or downed.

**How to capture (step by step):**
1. Walk into the cartel zone on foot — capture starts **automatically**; a progress bar appears.
2. Stay inside the zone: **50 m** for Arms/Drug/Wong, **85 m** for the Oil Cartel.
3. A fresh, uncontested capture takes **roughly 10 minutes** to reach 100%.
4. If a rival gang member is standing in the zone, it goes **"Contested"** and your progress stalls.

**Taking a cartel someone else owns:** you first have to grind the current owner's progress back down to 0, *then* build your own up — so re-capturing an owned cartel takes longer than capturing a neutral one. Progress only moves while **only your gang** is present in the radius.

**On a successful full capture your gang gets:**
- **+5 gang XP** (toward gang perks) and a **+1 cartel-captured** stat for everyone in your gang nearby.
- A **30-minute cooldown** before that cartel can be flipped again.
- The previous owner's online members get a warning ping the moment a rival starts capping, so expect a fight.

**Why own a cartel — the income:**
- **Arms Dealer:** your whole gang gets a **15% discount on all rebel purchases** (rebel weapons, rebel virtual items, and rebel clothing). On top of that, everyone else who processes there pays your gang (see below).
- **Processing tax:** when a player whose gang does **not** own the cartel processes drugs/materials at Arms, Drug, or Wong cartel, they keep only **85%** of the output (they keep **95%** for top-tier products like cannabis brick, coke brick, blue meth, aged scotch, and PCP). The cash value of that **15%/5% cut goes into the owning gang's bank** (capped per payout to prevent abuse). If your gang owns it, you process there **tax-free**.
- **Wong Triad:** selling at the **Wong's Food & Liquor** shop pays the Wong owner a **10% cut** on the sale (unless your gang owns it). A few alcohol items are exempt.
- **Oil Cartel:** takes **no processing tax** — it produces **Rubber** instead (below).

#### Oil Cartel Rubber

- The Oil Cartel steadily **produces 1 rubber per minute, up to a 100-rubber cap**. It produces even when uncapped or contested.
- **Only the gang that has FULLY captured the Oil Cartel can retrieve the rubber.**
- To collect: scroll-wheel **"Get Cartel Rubber"** on the oil flag.
- Rules while grabbing: stand **within 8 m** of the flag, **no other player within 10 m**, and you **can't grab within 5 minutes of a server restart**.
- Each grab pulls as much rubber as fits in your inventory, so bring free carry weight.

---

### Turf

**What it is:** Three rotating gang-turf zones. Hold one (or two) for economy perks.

**The turf areas (Altis):** **Rodopoli**, **Zaros**, **Panagia** — each is a ~750 m zone marked on your map.

**How the rotation works:**
- **One turf at a time** goes "active" and its map circle turns **red**. A new turf pops roughly **every ~33 minutes** and stays active for about **15 minutes** (a 900-second window).
- While a turf is **red/active it's a KOS zone — RDM rules do NOT apply** inside it. When the circle is yellow again, it's safe.
- **Recap-on-red:** the instant a turf goes active it's wiped to neutral, so **even the current owner has to re-capture it** every cycle. Defend to keep it.

**How to capture (step by step):**
1. Wait for the turf you want to go **red**.
2. Go to that turf's flag with a **weapon out**, in a gang, on foot.
3. Scroll-wheel **"Begin Capture"** within **5 m** of the flag.
4. Hold the capture (~**60 seconds**): don't move more than **4 m**, don't sprint, and don't get downed, restrained, or enter a vehicle, or it fails.
5. The **last gang to complete a capture before the window closes wins** the turf.

**Limits and gotchas:**
- A single gang can hold a **maximum of 2 turfs** at once.
- **No more than 5 of your gang members within 750 m** during a capture, or the capture is **voided** (the game will tell you the turf failed due to too many nearby gang members). Bring a small squad.
- You **cannot capture a turf while your gang owns the Gang Fortress**.
- Successful capture also grants a small amount of **gang XP** to nearby members and counts toward the "capture a turf" quest.

**Turf perks (while your gang holds it):**
- **Rodopoli** — gather **cocaine and heroin at up to double yield** (1–2 per pull instead of 1).
- **Zaros** — **gas-station robberies pay uncapped** (they're normally capped at **$5,000**), and **chop-shop claims are free ($0)** instead of paying the auction price.
- **Panagia** — **reduced material cost at the black market**.
- **Holding 2 turfs at once** also gives your gang **reduced weapon prices**.

**Turf tax income:** the **Drug Dealer (heroin) shop that sits inside a turf zone** pays the turf's owning gang a **20% cut** on drug sales made there (unless your own gang owns that turf, in which case there's no cut).

---

### Gang Fortress

**What it is:** A scheduled **king-of-the-hill** fight for control of the Gang Fortress. The gang that racks up the most points wins and **owns the fortress until the next event**.

**Schedule:** Auto-starts **every Wednesday and Sunday at 5:00 PM Central Time** and runs for **1 hour**.

**Where:** The **"Gang Fortress"** marker on your map. When the event starts you'll get a server-wide pop-up ("The Gang Fortress can now be captured for the next hour!") and a red 125 m capture circle appears. **Two temporary safezone Rebel Outposts** (each with a weapon NPC and a vehicle NPC) spawn about **1 km** from the fortress — inside a 50 m no-damage / no-fire bubble, so you can gear up safely near the fight. These outposts are open to everyone during the event.

**How to capture the flag (step by step):**
1. During the event, get to the **fortress flag** with a **weapon out**, in a gang, on foot.
2. Use the **"Capture Fortress Flag"** action and hold it (~**30 seconds**): stay **within 8 m** of the flag and don't get downed, restrained, or hop in a vehicle.
3. On success, **ownership of the flag flips to your gang** and a server broadcast announces it.

**Scoring (how you win):**
- While your gang **holds the flag**, you earn **5 points every 5 seconds per living gang member** inside the **125 m zone** (1 member = 5/tick, 4 members = 20/tick, etc.). **More defenders = faster scoring**, so commit people to hold it.
- A rival gang can **re-capture the flag** to steal ownership and start scoring for themselves — expect the flag to change hands repeatedly.
- A **live leaderboard HUD** shows the top 3 gangs plus your own gang's score.
- At the end of the hour, the **highest-scoring gang wins** and keeps the fortress.

**What owning the Gang Fortress gets you:** The fortress is a permanent rebel-style base with its own NPCs — a **rebel weapon shop**, a **sell-only drug dealer** (he buys drugs at a premium but won't sell to you), a **Wong's food court**, **rebel + New Drachma car shops**, **vehicle / helicopter / gang garages**, and **Rebel Training for $12,500**. While your gang owns the fortress, you get a **15% discount** at the fortress-area shops (weapons, vehicles, virtual items, loadouts). Ownership **persists through server restarts** until the next event. (Exact drug sell-premium rate at the fortress dealer: verify.)

**Reminder:** you can't capture regular turf while you hold the fortress.

---

### Tips & Common Mistakes

- **Cartels won't cap if you bring the wrong gun** — leave the SMG-02 and PDW2000 at home; anything else (rifle, SDAR, shotgun, LMG) works. The Oil Cartel doesn't care what you carry.
- **Get out of your vehicle** to cap a cartel — capturing is on-foot only.
- **Contested = no progress.** If a cartel or turf is stuck, there's a rival in the radius. Clear them out.
- **Only the gang that fully owns the Oil Cartel can grab rubber**, and only from right at the flag with nobody else nearby. Bring inventory space and don't try in the last 5 minutes before a restart.
- **Keep your turf capture squad small** (5 or fewer within 750 m) or the whole capture is thrown out.
- **Cartel captures ping the old owner** — every cap is likely to draw a fight, so bring backup.
- **Fortress and turf don't mix** — winning the fortress locks you out of turf capping while you hold it.
- Watch the **Captures** phone app to see who owns each cartel, current capture %, cooldowns, and how much rubber is waiting at the Oil Cartel.

---

## Federal Events & Heists

The biggest paydays on the island are the Federal Events: the Federal Reserve, the Bank of Altis, the Jewelry Emporium, the MaxSec Evidence Lockup, the Altis Shipping convoy, and the Loony Bin breach. They are crew jobs — loud, marked on everyone's map, and they drag the whole police force down on top of you. This page covers how to start each one, what you need, and what you walk away with.

### Before You Start (rules that apply to every heist)

- **You must be a civilian (rebel).** These jobs are all civ-side. Most also require an actual **primary weapon slung on your back** — try to start one empty-handed and you'll be told "You'll need a bigger gun to pull this off..."
- **Only ONE Federal Event can run at a time.** If the Reserve, Bank, Evidence Lockup, convoy, Loony Bin, Jewelry Emporium, or a prison break is already active, you cannot start another — you'll get "There is already another Federal Event happening."
- **Police requirement.** Each job needs a minimum number of officers who have been on duty a while (this stops people flashing to cop just to unlock a heist). Current minimums: **Federal Reserve — 1**, **Bank — 4**, **Jewelry Emporium — 6**, **Evidence Lockup — 2**, **Altis Shipping convoy — 4 / 6 / 9** (low/med/high), **Loony Bin — 1**. Admins can waive the police requirement during special events or low population.
- **The 15-minute "security is high" lockout.** After any **Bank** or **Federal Reserve** job wraps up, a 15-minute island-wide cooldown blocks the start of *every* heist. You'll see "Security is high due to a recent robbery."
- **Everyone nearby gets wanted.** Standing near an active heist tags you with a bounty even if you never touched a tool (see each section for the amount).

**Where to buy your tools:** any **Rebel Outpost / Rebel Market** stocks the full kit (the Turf Market carries the drill and bolt cutter too). Approximate prices — they float a little with the market:

| Tool | Used for | Price (approx) |
|---|---|---|
| Diamond Tip Drill | Reserve domes/safes, Bank vault, Jewelry door | ~$1,500 |
| Bolt Cutter | Reserve perimeter gate (consumed per cut) | ~$1,000 |
| Demo Charge | Reserve safes (talent), Evidence dome, Loony Bin wall | ~$1,500 |
| Thermite | Loony Bin front door | ~$1,700 |
| Trojan Virus USB | Loony Bin mainframe laptops | ~$1,250 |
| Plasma Cutter | Jewelry Emporium safe | ~$1,000 |
| Crowbar | Jewelry Emporium display cases | ~$375 |

**Where to sell the loot** (details in each section):
- **Gold Bars** → Rebel Market / Turf Market, roughly **$50,000 each** (up to ~$78,000 when demand is high; +10% if your gang holds Zaros turf). You can also break a gold bar into gold nuggets at the Black Market.
- **Mental Patient Remains** → Rebel Market, about **$80,000 each**.
- **Dirty Money** → don't sell it; break the bands in your Y-menu to convert it **1:1 into clean cash** (you have to wait for the dye packs to die — see the Bank section).
- **Bags of Jewelry** → the **African Warlord** fence.
- **Seized contraband** from the Evidence Lockup → whatever's in the truck, sold at the matching dealers.

---

### Federal Reserve

*The island's gold vault — seven armored domes full of gold bars.*

**Where:** the **Federal Reserve** marker in north-central Altis (near **Telos**). Look for the fenced compound of white domes with inner vault buildings.

**You need:** a **Bolt Cutter** for the perimeter gate, a **Diamond Tip Drill** for the domes and inner safes. With the **Safe-Cracker** talent you can also carry **Demo Charges** to blow the inner safes fast. At least **1 police officer** online. Not available during another Federal Event, during the 15-minute security lockout, or during the Reserve's own **60-minute** post-robbery lockdown.

**How to do it:**
1. **Bolt Cut Gate** — walk up to the perimeter bar gate (within ~5 m) and cut it (~30 seconds). Each cut **uses up one Bolt Cutter**. Bring spares if there are multiple gates.
2. **Break Into Dome (Side Door)** — stand at a dome's electrical panel by the small side door (you have to be right on the panel, within ~4 m) and drill it (~105 seconds, drill required). This unlocks that dome's vault doors and **spawns the gold**: a briefcase holding **1–5 gold bars** appears in each inner vault building around that dome.
3. **Open the inner vault buildings** — at an inner building's door, either **Crack Safe** (drill, ~25 seconds) or, with the Safe-Cracker talent, **Plant Demo Charge** (demo charge, ~10 seconds + a bang).
4. **Grab the gold** and repeat across all **seven domes** — a full Reserve can yield up to roughly **70 gold bars**.
5. **Sell** the gold at the Rebel or Turf Market.

**The heat:** about 10 seconds after your first dome breach the alarm trips (cutting a gate can also set it off early). Police are dispatched, and with a crew present **MaxTac** deploys. Everyone the alarm catches near the Reserve is wanted for **Federal Reserve Robbery (a $10,000 bounty)**.

**Cooldowns:** ~105 s per dome; a **60-minute** lockdown after the job ends, plus the 15-minute security lockout.

**Tips:**
- The **Heister** talent lets you open a gate fully in a single cut, cuts faster, and gives a chance to avoid tripping the gate alarm.
- The drill fails if you move, take a hit that downs/cuffs you, or step off the panel. Have a buddy watch your back.

---

### Bank of Altis

*Drill the main bank vault for a pile of Dirty Money.*

**Where:** the **Bank of Altis** marker in south-central Altis, at the vault door.

**You need:** a **Diamond Tip Drill**. At least **4 police officers** online. Not available during another Federal Event, the security lockout, or the Bank's own **20-minute** re-rob window.

**How to do it:**
1. **Begin Drilling Vault** — stand within ~4 m of the vault door and start the drill. A drill prop appears and the alarm goes off immediately; civilians within 90 m are wanted for **Bank Robbery ($10,000 bounty)**. With two or more robbers, **MaxTac** is dispatched.
2. **Hold the bank for the full 15 minutes.** At least one living, un-cuffed robber has to stay within **60 m** the whole time — abandon it and the drill dies. A red draining bar shows your countdown.
3. On success the vault door opens for **3 minutes** and **Dirty Money briefcases** drop on the vault floor. The total payout is **$50,000 + $50,000 for every officer online**, split across several ownerless briefcases so the whole crew can each grab one.
4. Grab the Dirty Money and get out.
5. **Launder it:** in your Y-menu, use/break the Dirty Money to convert it **1:1 into clean cash** — but the dye packs stay active for a few minutes, so you have to **wait until roughly 6–7 minutes after the robbery ends** ("The dye packs are still active…") before it'll convert.

**The counter:** a cop standing at the vault can **Disable Drill** to end the robbery early.

**Cooldown:** 20-minute re-rob window, plus the 15-minute security lockout.

---

### Jewelry Emporium

*Smash-and-grab a display store full of jewels.*

**Where:** the **Jewelry Emporium** building in eastern Altis. The break-in point is the back door.

**You need:** a **Diamond Tip Drill** (to start), a **Crowbar** (display cases), and a **Plasma Cutter** (the safe). This is the most cop-heavy job: **6 police officers** online. Not available during another Federal Event, the security lockout, or the Emporium's **30-minute** cooldown.

**How to do it:**
1. **Start Drilling Door** — at the back door, drill in (drill required, ~10 seconds). The alarm blares, cops are called, and everyone within 90 m is wanted for **Jewelry Emporium Robbery ($7,500 bounty)**.
2. **Break Jewel Case** — pry open each glass display case with your **Crowbar** (~25 seconds each) and grab the loot (a **Bag Of Jewelry** per case).
3. **Torch the safe** — once the drilling is done, run the **Plasma Cutter** on the main safe (~12 seconds), then **Take Safe Loot** for another **Bag Of Jewelry**.
4. **Load Container** at the loading crate to stash your bags, or just carry them out.
5. **Fence the loot** at the **African Warlord** (a fence NPC in east-central Altis (verify town)) — sell your Bags of Jewelry to him.

**Payout:** the Bags of Jewelry are fenced to the African Warlord. Each **Bag of Jewelry** fences for roughly **$150,000** (the sale value is currently bugged, so it may read wrong in-game — $150k is the intended payout). The same fence also buys higher-tier jewelry if you obtain it elsewhere: **Hope Diamond ~$108,000**, **Diamond Cuban Link ~$7,690**, **Blood Diamond ~$6,765**, **Engagement Ring ~$6,420**.

**Window:** the job stays open **20 minutes**, then the cases and safe reset for the next crew. Cooldown 30 minutes.

**Tip:** putting your hands up (surrender) cancels an in-progress drill so you can comply with police.

---

### MaxSec Evidence Lockup

*Blow open the police evidence dome and drive off with everything the cops have seized.*

**Where:** the **MaxSec Evidence Lockup** marker in northeast Altis — a fenced dome compound.

**Worth it only when the cops have loot to lose.** The reward is whatever contraband MaxSec has recently confiscated (seized weapons ride in the getaway truck's cargo; seized virtual goods ride in its trunk). If the pool is empty you still get a floor of **3 Gold Bars**.

**You need:** a proper **primary weapon** (a starter/cartel pistol won't do), and **Demo Charges**. At least **2 police officers** online. Not available during another Federal Event or the Lockup's **30-minute** attack lockout.

**How to do it:**
1. **Mind the defenses.** While the security system is up, the compound is ringed by an **electric fence** that knocks you unconscious if you enter on foot, and an **anti-air system** that shoots down helicopters. Don't go in until it's down.
2. **Hack Defense Systems** — at the security laptop, upload the virus (~90 seconds; needs a real gun). This **alerts the police**, and **5 minutes later** the defenses drop. Defend the laptop in the meantime — a cop can **repair** it (~90 seconds) to bring the defenses back up and kill your attempt.
3. With defenses down, **Plant Demo Charge** on the dome (~90 seconds). A long fuse burns — **15 minutes** normally, or **25 minutes** if another major robbery is also running — then the dome blows open.
4. **Breach Evidence Vault** — plant one more **Demo Charge** on the opened dome (~30 seconds). A **getaway truck** spawns loaded with all the seized contraband, keyed to your whole group.
5. Drive off and sell the haul at the matching dealers.

**The heat:** hacking the system pushes a **redeploy alert** to every officer, and everyone raiding is wanted for **Raiding the Evidence Lockup ($15,000 bounty)**. Cops can **Secure the Lockup** to close the dome, defuse the bomb, and reset the whole event.

**Cooldown:** 30 minutes from the last attack.

---

### Altis Shipping Convoy

*Bribe a dock worker to leak a cargo run, hijack the truck, and sell it to a fence.*

**Where:** the **Altis Shipping** dock worker at the Altis Shipping marker in central Altis.

**You need:** a **primary weapon** and cash for the bribe. Police online scale with the value you pick: **4** (low), **6** (medium), **9** (high). Not available during another Federal Event, the security lockout, or the convoy's **30-minute** cooldown.

**How to do it:**
1. Talk to the dock worker and pick a tier — **Steal Low / Medium / High Value Shipment** — and pay the bribe: **$10,000 / $15,000 / $25,000**.
2. A cargo truck spawns with an AI driver and drives across the island (tracked by an orange marker). MaxSec is told to escort it.
3. **Stop the truck**, deal with the driver, and drive the stolen truck to a **fence**. While the job is live, fences show up on your map, each labeled with a **cut** (25%, 50%, 75%, or 100%).
4. **Deliver Stolen Shipment** at a fence. Your pay is the shipment's full value times that fence's cut:

| Tier | Bribe | Full shipment value |
|---|---|---|
| Low | $10,000 | $80,000 |
| Medium | $15,000 | $140,000 |
| High | $25,000 | $225,000 |

So a High shipment delivered to a **100%** fence pays **$225,000**; the same load to a **25%** fence pays only ~$56,250.

**Talents:** **Hijacker** adds **+25%** to convoy payouts, and **Master Thief** adds a further **+15%** on top.

**Fail states:** if the truck is destroyed, or **45 minutes** pass, the shipment is lost and the job resets. Cooldown 30 minutes.

**Gotcha:** a low-cut fence can pay less than your bribe. Always drive for the highest-cut fence you can reach safely.

---

### Loony Bin Breach

*The server's namesake heist — break into the abandoned mental hospital, crack the network, and rob the patient tomb.*

**Where:** the **Loonybin Mental Hospital** marker in far-east Altis.

**You need:** a **Demo Charge** (breach), **Thermite** (front door), **five Trojan Virus USBs** (one per mainframe laptop), and a **primary weapon**. At least **1 police officer** online. Not available during another Federal Event, the security lockout, or the Bin's **45-minute** re-breach cooldown.

**How to do it:**
1. **Breach Loony Bin Gate** — place a **Demo Charge** on the hospital wall trolley (~25 seconds). This **starts the event**: the back wall blows, the front gate unlocks, the site is marked on the map, and the police are alerted.
2. **Lay Thermite On Front Door** — at the front-door laptop, prime your **Thermite** (~15 seconds). Now **everyone within 500 m is wanted for Loony Bin Robbery ($15,000 bounty)**, and a **5-minute countdown** begins — hold the front door until the thermite burns through.
3. **Hack Loony Bin Laptop** — after the door blows, hack **all five mainframe laptops**, one at a time, with a **USB** in each (~2 minutes per laptop, ~10 minutes total).
4. A **keypad appears on the church tomb door** (marked on the map), the loot spawns inside the still-locked church, and a **4-digit code** is broadcast in chat. Drive to the tomb and **Enter Keycode for Tomb**.
5. The church opens and an **iridescent Tempest getaway truck** spawns nearby with keys for every civilian within 200 m (it has an unlimited trunk). Inside the tomb:
   - **20 Gold Bars** (five briefcases of 4),
   - **3 Mental Patient Remains** (~$80,000 each),
   - a **weapons crate** — SPMGs, GM6 Lynx anti-materiel rifles, MAR-10 marksman rifles and ammo.
   Load it all into the Tempest and escape.
6. A **caretaker buries the remains 10 minutes after the tomb appeared** — but only once every civilian has left or been cuffed within 500 m, so staying on-site keeps the loot and the truck available.

**The counter:** a cop can **reactivate the firewall** at the back-door cop laptop (~85 seconds). If they do it before the thermite blows, or at any later stage, the breach is shut down and the Bin re-seals.

**Cooldowns:** **45-minute** re-breach cooldown, and a 45-minute hard cap on any single run.

**Tips:**
- You can only hack one mainframe laptop at a time, and only after the thermite has blown the front door.
- Only one crew can hold the Bin at once — the whole run must be finished (or lost) before anyone can breach again.

---

## Crime Tools & Robbery

Everything a criminal needs, where to buy it, and exactly how each job works. Almost every tool here is **civilian-only** (police can't rob), and most are sold at the **Rebel Market** found at rebel outposts. Prices below are the shop sticker price; the Rebel Market can add a small arms-cartel tax on top, and gang/fortress perks can discount it.

> Committing these crimes makes you **Wanted** (charges like Armed Robbery, Grand Theft Auto, Kidnapping, Arson, etc.) and builds your **Infamy**. Many jobs also require police to be online.

### Where to buy your tools

| Tool (item name) | Price | Sold at |
|---|---|---|
| Lockpick | $25 | Rebel Market, Turf Market, Skiptracer |
| Ziptie | $20 | Rebel Market, Bounty Hunter Shop, Skiptracer |
| Blindfold | $1,000 | Rebel Market, Turf Market, Skiptracer |
| Bolt Cutter | $1,000 | Rebel Market, Turf Market, Skiptracer |
| Diamond Tip Drill | $1,500 | Rebel Market, Turf Market |
| Demo Charge | $1,500 | Rebel Market |
| Thermite | $1,700 | Rebel Market |
| Trojan Virus USB | $1,250 | Rebel Market |
| Crowbar | $375 | Rebel Market |
| Plasma Cutter | $1,000 | Rebel Market |
| ATM Skimmer | $50,000 | Rebel Market |
| Arson Kit | $75,000 | Rebel Market |
| Remote Engine Kill Switch | $50,000 | Rebel Market |
| Remote Explosive | $215,000 | Rebel Market |
| Ignition Explosive | $200,000 | Rebel Market |
| Speed Bomb | $235,000 | Rebel Market |

A **Rebel License** ($12,500) is the standard buy-in for operating at rebel outposts and for rebel weapons.

Most tools are **used from the Y (inventory) menu** — select the item and press *Use* — or appear as a **scroll-wheel action** when you look at the right target. The exact method is noted per tool below.

---

### Robbing a gas station (store robbery)

**What it is:** Hold up a fuel-station shop for a slow trickle of dirty money.

- **Where:** Walk up to a gas-station **shop building** and scroll-wheel **"Rob Store"** (red text).
- **Requirements:** You must be a **civilian** with a **weapon in your hands** (not binoculars/holstered), and at least **1 police officer must be online**.
- **How:**
  1. Scroll **"Rob Store"** on the store building. An alarm sounds and police are alerted to your rough location.
  2. **Stay within 15 m** of the store. A progress bar fills over about **5 minutes**.
  3. You can leave early for a smaller cut, but you must hold for **at least 1 minute** or you get **nothing**.
- **Payout:** Roughly **$1,500 per minute** held (paid as **dirty money**). Off-turf this is **capped at $5,000**; if your gang **owns the Zaros turf** the cap is removed (~$7,500 at a full 5-minute hold). A certain talent adds **+10-20%**.
- **Cooldowns/limits:** The **same store can't be robbed again for 30 minutes**. You **can't use any ATM for 5 minutes** after robbing. Getting **restrained or knocked down** cancels the payout. A talent can add a random **0-2 minute delay** before the alarm triggers.
- **Tip:** Bank/launder the dirty money — see *Laundering dirty money* below.

---

### Mugging another player ("Rob Person")

**What it is:** Take a subdued player's cash (and optionally their gear).

- **Where:** Scroll-wheel **"Rob Person"** on the target.
- **Requirements:** You must have a **weapon out** (not binoculars). The target must have their **hands up (surrendered)** — or be **restrained**, but only if you have the bounty-hunter robbing talents. Target must be within **~5 m** and standing still. Players in **passive mode cannot be robbed**.
- **What you get:** **All of the victim's on-hand cash.** With bounty-hunter talents you can also force them to **drop their virtual items** and their **pistol**.
- **Gotcha:** Robbing gives you a **wanted charge** (unless the victim has a bounty and you have the honor talents that reduce it — one talent gives a flat +20% chance to avoid the charge). You can't rob someone with an empty bank of cash for cash, but the item/gun drop can still trigger.

---

### Zipties, blindfolds & freeing prisoners

**Ziptie a player (kidnapping):**
- Scroll-wheel **"Restrain"** on a player whose **hands are up** or who is **downed**, within ~5 m. Consumes **1 Ziptie ($20)**.
- **Civilian restraints automatically break after 10 minutes.** Restraining someone who **isn't in your gang and has no bounty** gives you a **Kidnapping** wanted charge.
- Once restrained you can drag/escort them, **Blindfold** them, or **Rob Person**.

**Blindfold ($1,000):** Scroll **"Apply Blindfold"** on a **restrained** player to black out their screen. Scroll **"Remove Blindfold"** to take it back (it returns to your inventory if you have room).

**Freeing a cuffed player:**
- **"Lockpick Handcuffs"** (needs a **Lockpick**) — chance-based (~20% base, higher with talents). On failure the lockpick breaks.
- **"Boltcut Handcuffs"** (needs a **Bolt Cutter**) — **always succeeds.**
- Both only work on players restrained by **police handcuffs**, not on civilian zipties, and the target must not be downed or being escorted. Freeing someone gives you a wanted charge.

---

### Stealing vehicles (lockpicking)

**What it is:** Pick a locked vehicle's lock to add it to your keychain.

- **Where:** Scroll-wheel **"Pick Vehicle Lock"** while looking at a **locked** vehicle within ~4 m, on foot. Needs a **Lockpick** ($25).
- **Success chance:** **20%** for normal cars/boats, **10%** for aircraft, **5%** for military vehicles — plus **+10%** bonuses from various rebel/bounty-hunter talents, certain lifetime quests, and owning the **Gang Fortress**.
- **Gated vehicles:**
  - Heavy vehicles (box trucks, transport trucks, MRAPs, wheeled APC) need a **heavy-vehicle lockpicking talent**.
  - **Helicopters/aircraft** need a **helicopter lockpicking talent**.
  - **Can't be lockpicked at all:** the covered heavy truck (`O_Truck_03`) and medical vehicles.
- **On success:** The vehicle joins your keychain and you gain XP; you'll get a **Grand Theft Auto**-type wanted charge. **On failure** the lockpick breaks and you may still catch a lesser charge. A modified vehicle (upgraded locks) takes longer to pick, and a vehicle with a **car/air alarm** installed will blare an alarm to everyone within 50 m while you work.

---

### Vehicle sabotage bombs

All four are **planted from the Y-menu or scroll-wheel** on a vehicle. The vehicle must be **stopped** (and its engine off for some), and you must be within a few meters.

**Speed Bomb ($235,000):**
- Use the item while looking at a **land vehicle** within **7 m**. You kneel for **~3.5 s** to arm it.
- The bomb goes **live once the vehicle exceeds 70 km/h**, then **detonates the moment its speed drops below 50 km/h** — destroying that vehicle and any vehicles within **10 m**.
- Only **one speed bomb per vehicle.**

**Ignition Bomb ($200,000):**
- You must be **in the driver's seat**, engine **off**, vehicle stopped. Scroll **"Attach Ignition Bomb"** (or use from the Y-menu). ~25 s plant.
