- It **detonates when the next person starts the engine** (a short delay after ignition), destroying the vehicle.

**Remote Engine Kill Switch ($50,000):**
- Scroll **"Insert Remote Engine Disabler"** on a **stopped** vehicle within ~5 m.
- Later, open your **phone** and **call in the detonator** while within **1 km** of the vehicle — this **kills the engine** (disables it, does **not** blow it up). Useful for stopping a getaway.
- You can only have **one active kill switch at a time.**

**Remote Explosive ($215,000):**
- Scroll **"Insert Remote Bomb"** on a **stopped** vehicle within ~5 m.
- **Call the detonator via your phone** within **1 km** to **detonate**, destroying the vehicle and any vehicles within **15 m**.
- Only **one active remote explosive at a time.**

---

### ATM Skimmer

**What it is:** A rig that quietly siphons a cut of every ATM transaction to you.

- **Where:** Look at a **physical ATM machine** (not an NPC banker) within ~3 m and scroll **"Plant ATM Skimmer"**. Consumes **1 ATM Skimmer ($50,000)**.
- **Payout:** You **siphon 10% of every deposit and withdrawal** anyone makes at that ATM (capped at **$999,999 per transaction**), paid straight to your bank. The victim sees a message that a skimmer took 10%.
- **Persistence:** The skimmer **survives server restarts.** Planting a skimmer on an already-rigged ATM **takes it over** (the previous owner is cut off).
- **Getting caught:** A **police officer** can scroll **"Remove ATM Skimmer ($200,000)"** on the ATM — they pocket a **$200,000 reward** and you lose all future skim from it. You keep whatever you already earned.

---

### Arson (burning down houses)

- **Where:** Look at a **house** within **10 m** and scroll **"Set house on fire"**. Consumes **1 Arson Kit ($75,000)**.
- **How:** Stand still through the ~10 s light-up (moving cancels it). The house catches fire and must be put out by the **fire brigade**.
- **Limits:** Only **5 house fires can be burning server-wide at once** — if the cap is hit you can't start another. Some houses are protected and can't be burned. Committing arson gives you a wanted charge.

---

### Jewelry Emporium heist

**What it is:** A multi-stage smash-and-grab at the Jewelry Emporium (marked on the map) using three tools.

- **Requirements:** Civilian, **police online**, no other federal event running, and no robbery in the last 15 minutes. Tools: **Diamond Tip Drill**, **Plasma Cutter**, **Crowbar**.
- **Steps:**
  1. **Diamond Tip Drill** the emporium **back door** (~10 s + a short hold) to trigger the alarm and start the heist.
  2. **Crowbar** open the **glass display cases** (~25 s each) and grab the **jewelry bags** inside.
  3. **Plasma Cutter** the **main safe** door (~12 s), then loot the **heavy jewelry bag** from the safe.
  4. Carry the jewelry bags to the **loading container** and load them in.
- **Payoff:** Sell the looted jewelry (the various jewel items) to the **African Warlord** dealer.
- **Cooldown:** The emporium **can't be breached again for 30 minutes** after a heist, and a recent robbery anywhere raises security for **15 minutes**.

---

### Federal Reserve

**What it is:** Crack the reserve's domes and inner safes for **gold bars**.

- **Where:** At the **Federal Reserve** (within 400 m of the fed marker). Civilian only. Tools: **Diamond Tip Drill** (required), optional **Demo Charge**.
- **Steps:**
  1. **"Break Into Dome (Side Door)"** with a **Diamond Tip Drill** — the drill runs about **105 seconds per dome**. This unlocks that dome's doors. An **alarm fires ~10 s** after the breach.
  2. Inside, on the research buildings, either **"Crack Safe"** with the **Diamond Tip Drill** (~25 s) or **"Plant Demo Charge"** (~10 s) — the demo-charge option just needs a **Demo Charge** (no special talent).
- **Loot:** Each inner safe building drops a **briefcase holding 1-5 gold bars**. Sell **gold bars** at rebel outposts.
- **Cooldowns:** After a completed robbery the domes are locked down for **60 minutes**; a recent robbery raises security for **15 minutes**.

---

### Bank of Altis

**What it is:** Drill the main bank vault for a pile of dirty-money briefcases.

- **Where:** Stand within **4 m of the bank vault door** and scroll **"Begin Drilling Vault"**. Needs a **Diamond Tip Drill**.
- **Requirements:** **4 police officers online** (tenured, not freshly logged-in). Civilian.
- **How:** The drill runs for **15 minutes**. The job holds as long as **any living civilian robber stays within 60 m** of the bank — the original driller can die or leave. **MaxTac may be dispatched.** Police can scroll **"Disable Drill"** to end the robbery.
- **Payout:** **$50,000 + $50,000 per cop online**, paid as **dirty-money briefcases** spread across the vault floor so the whole crew can grab one each. The **vault stays open for 3 minutes** — grab the briefcases fast before it re-locks and they despawn.
- **Cooldown:** The bank **can't be robbed again for 20 minutes.**

---

### Evidence Locker

**What it is:** Blow the police evidence vault for its stored weapons/loot.

- **Where:** At the evidence lockup. Civilian only. Tool: **Demo Charge**.
- **How:** First **hack the electric fence** (evidence-systems step), then scroll to **breach the vault** with a **Demo Charge** — a ~30 s plant, then the charge blows the door. An evidence truck delivers the loot to your group.
- **Gotcha:** You must clear the fence hack first, or the breach won't start.

---

### Loony Bin (Mental Hospital breach)

**What it is:** A heavy holdout heist on the mental hospital using **three different tools** in sequence.

- **Requirements:** Civilian, **police online**, a **primary weapon** in hand, no other federal event running. Tools: **Demo Charge**, **Thermite**, **Trojan Virus USB**.
- **Steps:**
  1. **Demo Charge** to **breach the outer gate** (~25 s plant, then it blows).
  2. **Thermite** on the **front-door laptop** to prime it (~15 s), then **hold out ~5 minutes** while the thermite burns through.
  3. Once it's breached, use a **Trojan Virus USB** to **hack each of the 5 laptop stations** (~2 minutes per laptop). Police can re-activate the firewall to stop you.
  4. Clear the objective, then loot the patient's remains for the reward.
- **Cooldown:** The Loony Bin **can't be breached again for 45 minutes** after a run; a recent robbery raises security for **15 minutes**.

---

### Laundering dirty money

Gas-station and bank robberies pay in **"Dirty Money"** (banded bank notes), not spendable cash.

- To cash it out, **use the Dirty Money item from your Y-menu** to break down the bands into real cash.
- You **can't launder immediately** — the dye packs stay active for about **5 minutes** after a gas rob and roughly **6-7 minutes** after a bank job. Wait out the timer, then break it down.
- Police can't launder dirty money; it must be **seized via HQ/vehicle** if a cop grabs it.

---

### Quick reference — cooldowns & holds

| Job | Time to complete | Re-do cooldown | Cops needed |
|---|---|---|---|
| Gas station | up to 5 min hold | 30 min (same store) | ≥1 |
| Bank of Altis | 15 min drill | 20 min | 4 |
| Federal Reserve dome | ~105 s per dome | 60 min after robbery | (beta: 0) |
| Jewelry Emporium | multi-step | 30 min | ≥1 (verify count) |
| Loony Bin | ~5 min holdout + hacks | 45 min | ≥1 (verify count) |
| ATM Skimmer | instant plant | none (persists) | none |

*(A server-wide "security is high" state also blocks federal events for 15 minutes after any major robbery.)*

---

## Rebel, Combat, Airdrops

This covers everything about going rebel: buying Rebel Training, the rebel weapon/clothing/vehicle shops, the combat reward loop (War Kills, War Rating and New Drachma), how to earn and spend New Drachma, and the server's public weapon airdrops.

### Becoming a Rebel (Rebel Training)

"Rebel" is a civilian license that unlocks illegal, high-caliber weaponry, rebel clothing, rebel vehicles, weapon crafting, and the Infamy talent tree.

- **Where:** any **Rebel Outpost** (marked on your map as "Rebel Outpost" / "Rebel Air Shop"). Walk up to the outpost NPC and scroll-wheel **"Rebel Training ($12,500) (replace bounty hunter)"**.
- **Cost:** **$12,500** (paid from cash on hand).
- **Requirement:** you must be a civilian. That's it — no other license needed.
- **Big gotcha — Rebel and Bounty Hunter are mutually exclusive.** Buying Rebel Training **instantly cancels your Bounty Hunter License** (and vice-versa). You can only be one at a time, and you pay the full $12,500 again to switch back. Bounty Hunter License also costs $12,500.

Once you hold Rebel Training you also get:
- Access to all rebel shops and the **Infamy** phone app (your rebel prestige tree).
- The ability to **craft 7.62mm rifles** (crafting a 7.62 rifle is blocked without a rebel license).
- Infamy talents that add perks like cheaper mag refills, cheaper rebel vehicles, better gathering yields, and more.

### Rebel Outposts — what's there

Rebel Outposts are hidden in the wilderness and shown on the map as **"Rebel Outpost"** markers (a few also have a **"Rebel Air Shop"**). There are several spread around the island — e.g. central near the Warzone (~grid 12133/10519), the far north (~15266/22507), the northwest above Kavala (~3423/17699), and the far south/southeast (~20073/6725). All rebel outposts sell the same stock.

At the outpost NPC (with Rebel Training) you can scroll-wheel:
- **Loadout System** – save/buy full kits (see below)
- **Rebel Weapon Shop** – buy guns for cash
- **Rebel Clothing Shop** / **Civilian Clothing Shop**
- **Rebel Market** – rebel virtual-item shop
- **New Drachma Weapons** / **New Drachma Clothes** – same stock, paid in New Drachma (independent ND price list, seeded at roughly the cash price ÷ 6,000)
- **Refill Magazines** – top off your mags
- **EMT Item Shop**, **Medical Assistance**, **Medical Dispensary** – healing/med items
- **ATM** and **Gang Account** access

The rebel weapon-shop dialog is titled **"Mohammed's Jihadi Shop"** (it reads "Rita's Skiptracer Shop" for bounty hunters instead).

### Rebel Weapon Shop (cash prices)

You need Rebel Training to open it. Every price below is charged in **cash**, or in **New Drachma** via the "New Drachma Weapons" action (ND prices are their own list — about the cash number ÷ 6,000). All rebel weapons are illegal to carry.

Support items (available in most shops):
- ToolKit **$50**, FirstAidKit **$75**, Binocular **$100**, GPS/Map/Compass/Watch **$10** each
- Rangefinder **$1,000**, NV Goggles **$300**, Chemlights **$50**, Smoke grenade (thrown) **$100**, Smoke grenade shell (UGL) **$250**
- Flare Gun **$500**, **Hand Grenade $5,000**

Handguns:
- Zubr (Pistol 01) **$1,250**, Rook-40 **$1,500**, 4-five (ACPC2) **$2,500**, Heavy Pistol (Kimi/.45) **$2,500**

SMGs / PDWs:
- Vermin **$3,500**, PDW2000 **$3,500**, Sting **$4,000**, P90 (compact) **$6,000**, P90 (full) **$7,000**

Shotguns:
- 12Ga Sawed-Off **$5,000**, 12Ga Shotgun **$6,000**

Assault / carbine rifles:
- SDAR **$3,500**, Mk20 / Mk20C **$3,500**, AKS-74U **$3,500**, TRG-21 **$4,000**
- CTAR series **$4,500**, CTAR GL **$5,500**
- Promet (MSBS) **$5,000**, Promet Mark **$6,000**
- Katiba / Katiba Carbine **$5,000**, Katiba GL (smoke) **$6,000**
- AK-12 (all camos) **$5,000**, AKM **$6,000**, AK-12U **$6,500**
- SPAR-16 **$5,000**, SPAR-16S **$7,000**, ARX **$6,000**

Marksman / sniper:
- Rahim (DMR-01) **$5,000**, 7.62 Hunting Rifle **$5,000**, Mk-I EMR (DMR-06) **$6,500**
- Mk18 ABR / EBR **$8,000**, MAR-10 / DMR-03 **$9,000**

Heavy weapons:
- Mk200 LMG **$9,000**, RPK-12 **$20,000**, SPMG (LMG-03) **$30,000**, Zafir **$50,000**
- **RPG-7 launcher $30,000**

### Rebel Clothing

The **Rebel Clothing Shop** (also available as **New Drachma Clothes**) stocks guerilla, CTRG, combat, wetsuit and ghillie uniforms plus rebel vests/backpacks/helmets. Most uniforms are **$300**; survival/CTRG uniforms run **$1,000**, ghillie suits **$1,500**, and a few special uniforms up to **$6,000**. (Rebel-tier vests and packs carry combat gear the civilian clothing shop won't sell you.)

### Rebel Vehicles (cash — "Rebel Car Shop")

Bought at an outpost via **"Rebel Car Shop"** (ground and air). Prices are cash:

Ground:
- Quad Bike **$200**, Hatchback **$750**, Offroad (guerilla) **$3,000**, Offroad Repair **$3,500**, Sport Hatchback **$6,500**
- Prowler (unarmed) **$22,500**, Qilin (unarmed) **$25,000**
- Offroad (LMG) **$45,000**, **Strider $50,000**, Armed Qilin (minigun) **$90,000**, **Armed Offroad (HMG) $100,000**, **Ifrit $120,000**

Air:
- Hummingbird **$45,000**, Hellcat / Hellcat (benches) **$60,000**, Orca **$65,000**
- Taru (bench) **$75,000**, Taru **$90,000**
- **Pawnee (Guns) $1,000,000** and **Orca (Guns) $1,200,000** — note their missiles are stripped on spawn; they keep their guns.

### Loadout System

At the outpost, **"Loadout System"** lets you save complete kits (weapon + attachments + clothing + items) and re-buy them in one click. You can pay with **cash or New Drachma** — the loadout menu has a currency toggle (ND price = the cash total ÷ 6,000). Loadout price is just the sum of every item's shop price, so a saved kit costs the same as buying the pieces individually. Your loadout's total value also feeds the War Rating formula (see below).

### War Kills, War Rating & New Drachma (the combat reward loop)

Killing other players in the right conditions turns kills into **War Rating** (a competitive score) and **New Drachma** (a spendable currency). This is the core rebel gameplay loop.

**A kill counts as a WAR KILL when all of these are true:**
1. Both you (the killer) and the victim are **civilians**, and
2. Any one of:
   - The victim is an **enemy gang member during an active gang war**, OR
   - **Both** players are inside a **restricted area** — the Drug Dealer pen, the Warzone, or within **300m of any cartel flag** (Arms, Drug, Oil, or Wong's) — **and** you're not in the same gang, OR
   - **Both** players are within **400m of an active Gang Fortress** flag.

Cop kills are handled separately (see below). Killing a teammate/groupmate does not count.

**Rewards for a qualifying war kill:**
- **New Drachma: +0.25 ND** (this is the base war-kill payout, credited by the server — you'll see a "+0.25 ND — New Drachma awarded" toast).
- **War Rating:** points based on how your kit value compares to the victim's, plus bonuses (below).
- **+1 War Kill** stat and **+1 to your War Killstreak**.
- You also gain **Infamy** if the kill was near a cartel (requires Rebel Training).
- You become **wanted** for the kill (a charge is added), so expect police heat.

**War Rating point formula** (from loadout values, "cheap kit beats rich kit" pays most):
- Victim's kit **≥ $20,000** and yours **≤ $5,000** → **15 points**
- Both kits **≥ $20,000** → **10 points**
- Both kits between **$5,000–$20,000** → **7 points**
- Everything else → **5 points**

**Bonus conditions** — a bonus war kill pays **0.35 ND** instead of 0.25 and boosts War Rating:
- **Long-range kill** — you were **≥ 500m** from the victim → adds **+10 War Rating**
- **Air kill** — the victim was in a vehicle **≥ 100m up in the sky** → adds **+10 War Rating**
- **Killstreak** — you had a **war killstreak of 3+** → adds **+10 War Rating**
- **Cartel kill** — you or the victim were within 300m of a cartel flag → adds **+3 War Rating** (and the +$500 BM)

You'll get an on-screen brag line ("You killed X from a whopping distance of… you received a New Drachma and war rating bonus"). Max single-kill War Rating is around **28** (10 base + 15 matchup + 3 cartel).

**War Killstreak titles** pop on-screen as your streak climbs: **3 = Triple Threat, 5 = Bloodthirsty, 10 = Merciless, 15 = Ruthless, 20 = Relentless, 25 = Brutal, 30 = Nuclear.** Your killstreak **resets to 0 every time you die.**

**Anti-farm cooldown:** you can only earn War Rating + New Drachma from the **same victim once every 10 minutes**. Re-killing the same person inside that window still makes you wanted but pays **nothing**. Don't farm one guy.

**Dying costs you.** When you're the victim of a war kill you take **+1 War Death** and **lose War Rating** equal to the killer's base matchup value (5–15). War Rating can't drop below 0.

**Cop kills (separate):** killing a MaxSec officer as a civilian records a cop-kill stat and makes you wanted, but only pays **New Drachma if the cop dies inside a restricted area** (Drug pen or Warzone) — that's a flat **+0.25 ND** (wallet-credit toast). Cop kills do not grant War Rating.

War Rating / War Kills feed your seasonal ranking and unlock **titles** like Freedom Fighter (250 cop kills), BluFor Butcher (500), and Pig Farmer (1,000).

### New Drachma — earning & spending

**New Drachma (ND)** is a second currency, separate from cash and bank — an exact digital coin held server-side (4 decimal places, no rounding tricks). Check your balance in the phone **Money** screen (shown as "… ND") — rebel shop titles also show your ND balance.

**How you earn BM:**
- **War kills:** $1,500 each (or $2,000 with a bonus condition).
- **Cop kills inside the Drug pen or Warzone:** $1,500 each.
- **Login/veteran rewards:** BM is handed out on certain login-streak days — **day 22 = 2,500 BM, day 35 = 7,500, day 51 = 10,000, day 71 = 15,000, day 96 = 15,000.**
- Seasonal events (e.g. Christmas presents) can contain BM.

**How you spend ND** — only at Rebel Outposts, via the New Drachma shop actions:
- **New Drachma Weapons** – the full rebel weapon list (including the **RPG-7 for 5 ND**).
- **New Drachma Clothes** – the full rebel clothing list.
- **New Drachma Car Shop** – the heavy/armed rebel vehicles: **Strider 8.3333 ND, Armed Qilin 15 ND, Armed Offroad 16.6667 ND, Ifrit 20 ND, AWC Nyx (turretless tank hull) 33.3333 ND.**

**Important:** BM prices are the **same number** as the cash price — a $5,000 weapon costs 5,000 BM. BM is just a harder-to-get currency you can burn instead of cash on the exact same gear. You can accumulate and spend BM even without Rebel Training in theory, but every place that *spends* it requires Rebel Training, so effectively you need to be a rebel to use it.

### Refill Magazines

At the outpost, **"Refill Magazines"** tops off every partially-used mag you're carrying. You must **stand still for 3 seconds** (moving cancels it).
- **$50** per standard mag that needs refilling.
- **+$300** extra for drum mags, **+$950** extra for LMG box/belt mags.
- With the right rebel Infamy talent, refills are effectively free (about **$1** total).

### Public Rebel Weapon Airdrops

The server periodically drops a free crate of rebel weapons in the wilderness for anyone to grab — a public, free-for-all event.

- **When:** a random check runs roughly **every 45–90 minutes** (never within the first 10 minutes after a restart), and only fires when **4 or more players** are online. Below 4 players it skips and waits for the next window.
- **How it plays out:**
  1. A server-wide message and a "LoonyNet" news alert announce **"a REBEL WEAPONS DROP hits [zone] in 5 minutes,"** and a **red warning marker** appears on the map at the target zone.
  2. Five minutes later a smuggler VTOL flies over and **parachutes a supply crate** in; the marker switches to a **box icon labeled "WEAPON DROP (landed)"** and **green smoke** pops at the crate.
  3. The crate is **unlocked and public — first crew to reach and hold the spot takes the guns.** Expect a fight.
  4. The crate **despawns 20 minutes after it lands.**
- **Where it can land (8 possible zones):** Mountain Pass, Hunting Grounds, Shoreline Ruins, Salt Flats, Land Bridge, Buoy Graveyard, Warzone Island, Power Plant Ridge. The actual crate is **scattered up to 150m** off the named point, so you can't pre-camp the exact pixel — use the green smoke to home in.
- **What's inside (one random weapon set plus a fixed support load):**
  - One of: **3× Katiba** / **2× MX (Black) + 2× Heavy Pistol** / **1× Mk200 LMG + 2× Katiba Carbine** / **2× Mk18 EBR** / **3× AKM**
  - Ammo: **12× 6.5mm caseless, 8× 20-rnd 7.62, 12× 30-rnd 7.62×39, 6× 11-rnd .45 ACP**
  - Gear: **6× FirstAidKit, 2× Arco optics, 2× Holosight optics**

### Tips & Gotchas

- **Rebel vs Bounty Hunter is one-or-the-other.** Don't buy Rebel Training if you were planning to keep hunting bounties — it wipes your BH license and the $12,500 you spent on it.
- **Farming the same player doesn't pay.** The 10-minute-per-victim cooldown means BM/War Rating only counts once per person per 10 min — spread your kills around.
- **A cheap kit killing a rich kit pays the most War Rating (15).** Running a budget loadout into a fight is deliberately rewarded.
- **Stack the bonuses.** Long-range (500m+), air kills (100m+ up), or a 3+ killstreak each add +$500 BM and a chunk of War Rating — and a cartel-zone fight adds even more.
- **Cop kills only pay ND inside the Drug pen or Warzone.** Random highway cop kills give you heat but no New Drachma.
- **Airdrops are contested.** Anyone can loot the crate, cops included — bring friends (the announcement literally says "bring friends, or don't — more for you").
- **New Drachma prices are their own list** (seeded at about the cash price ÷ 6,000, then tuned independently). Spend ND on rifles to save cash, or bank it for the armed vehicles the cash shops won't sell you.

---

## MaxSec / Police

MaxSec (the police faction) is the server's law-enforcement side (BLUFOR/"west"): you patrol, ticket and charge criminals, seize contraband, and throw fugitives in prison. Everything below is MaxSec-only unless noted.

### Joining the force & ranks

MaxSec is **whitelist-only**. If you are not on the cop whitelist, selecting a police slot bounces you back with a "Not Whitelisted" message. Getting whitelisted is handled off-server through the community Discord application process. Once approved you can pick any open `cop_` slot on the slot screen.

There are **7 ranks**, and your rank drives almost everything — shop access, loadout, prestige rate, and which scroll actions you get:

| Rank | Name |
|---|---|
| 1 | Cadet |
| 2 | Patrol Officer |
| 3 | Corporal |
| 4 | Sergeant |
| 5 | Lieutenant |
| 6 | Captain |
| 7 | Chief of Police |

- Every rank wears the same stock MaxSec uniform — rank is shown by your name tag, armory and scroll actions, not by your clothes. (The old per-rank uniform retextures were retired in 2026-08.)
- **Special slots:** some slots are reserved. A dedicated Sergeant+ slot requires rank 4+. "Warrant" slots (fugitive-tracking role) and "cop medic" slots require rank 3+. "SCD/undercover" slots are a separate whitelist (see Street Crime Division below), not a rank gate.
- **Carry weight:** cops carry up to 90.
- **Talents:** you get 20 talent points, plus 2 more for every rank above Patrol Officer (so a Captain has ~28). Talents are bought with **prestige** (see bottom section) and unlock better guns, gear and abilities.

### Your spawn loadout & non-lethal stun

On spawn/redeploy you're kitted out for your rank: the MaxSec uniform, police cap, a sidearm (P07 with suppressor), a rifle/SMG scaled to your rank and talents, smoke, and you always carry First Aid Kits (topped up to 4 when you gear up). Higher-rank talents swap your rifle up to an MX / MXM.

**You start on NON-LETHAL rounds by default** (your job is to down and cuff, not kill). Stun hits **accumulate** and scale with the target's armour — roughly: bare body = 1 hit to down, a basic plate carrier = ~6 hits, a headshot = instant down, a helmeted head = 2 hits. Heavier rigs take more. At **Patrol Officer (rank 2)+** you get scroll-wheel options **"Load Lethal Rounds"** / **"Load Non-Lethal Rounds"** to switch.

### The police station: shops & services

Walk up to the gear NPC at any MaxSec station and scroll-wheel. You'll see:

- **Police Weapons Shop** — your rank armory (see below).
- **Police Equipment Shop** — tools, optics-less gear, NVGs, UAV terminal, etc.
- **Police Uniform Shop** and **Police Items Shop** — clothing and virtual items. The uniform shop stocks stock-Arma police clothing only; there are no custom MaxSec retextures.
- **Loadout System** — save/load full loadouts so you can re-kit in one click.
- **Gear Up!** — re-buy the exact loadout you died with (price = its shop cost; **50% off during a community event**). Stay within 10 m and out of vehicles or it fails.
- **Redeploy Me!** — teleport-redeploy menu.
- **Refill Magazines** — free ammo top-up.
- **ATM**, **Medical Assistance** (free heal), and an **EMT Item Shop** for medical supplies.
- **Report for MaxTac Duty** and **MaxTac Armory** (when MaxTac is active — see MaxTac).
- **Air Training** — buys your Pilot License so you can fly MaxSec helicopters. The scroll action is labelled "$1,500" but you are actually charged the police Pilot License price of **$3,500**, and it needs rank Patrol Officer (2)+.
- At the air HQ: **Service Chopper ($1,000)** repairs/refuels your helicopter.

### Weapons & equipment shop (rank-gated)

Your armory is named after your rank (Cadet Armory → Chief of Police Armory). **Most rifles only appear once you've bought the matching prestige talent** — so a fresh Cadet sees mostly pistols/SMGs until they spend prestige.

Starter/always-available:
- P07 pistol — **$500** (rank 1)
- SMG-05 — **$1,250** (rank 1)
- Flashbang — **$1,000** (rank 2+, needs the flashbang talent)
- Smoke grenades $100, chemlights $50, 40mm smoke rounds $250–$500

Talent-gated rifles (examples, price / rank):
- SPAR-16 — $2,000 (rank 1) · MXC — $2,000 (rank 2) · MX / Promet — $2,500 (rank 2)
- ARX / MXM — $3,000 (rank 3) · SPAR-16S — $5,000 (rank 4)
- Heavy pistol — $750 (rank 5) · SPAR-17 — $4,500 (rank 5) · MK-1 EMR (DMR-03) — $5,000 (rank 5)

Equipment shop staples: Toolkit $50, First Aid Kit $75, GPS/Map/Compass/Watch $10 each, Binocular $100, Rangefinder $1,000, NVGs $300, **UAV Terminal $1,000 (rank 4+)**.

### Police vehicles (car, air, boat)

At the vehicle NPCs, scroll **Car Shop / Helicopter Shop / Boat Shop**, plus **Vehicle/Helicopter/Boat Garage** to pull stored ones and **Store vehicle in Garage**. Prices shown are the police price and each vehicle has a **rank requirement**:

- **Cars:** Quad Bike $150 (r1) · Hatchback $750 · Offroad $500 (r1) · Comms Offroad $3,000 · SUV $3,500 (r2) · Hatchback Sport $5,000 (r2) · Prisoner Transport Van $8,500 (r1) · **Hunter $30,000 (r3)** · **Strider $35,000 (r4)** · **Ifrit $65,000 (r5)** · **Marid APC $90,000 (r6)**.
- **Helicopters:** MH-9 Hummingbird $25,000 (r2) · Hellcat $50,000 (r3) · Orca $55,000 (r4) · **Blackfish (Infantry) $200,000 (r6)**, plus other light helis $60,000–$70,000 (r4–r6).
- **Boats:** requires the **Cop Boating License ($800)** first; small boats $300–$2,000 (r2), assault boat $8,000 (r3), larger boat $85,000 (r5).

When someone impounds/seizes a vehicle it goes to that owner's garage; you can't buy it back cheaply from theirs.

### Sirens, lights & dispatch

While **driving a cop vehicle**:
- **F key** — toggle siren (Ctrl+F = Yelp, Shift+F = Phaser wail; armored trucks use a deeper siren). Turning the siren on also switches your emergency lights on.
- **Tab key** — opens the **Light Console** with buttons: Emergency Lights, MaxTac Lights, **Request Superior**, **Request Backup**.
- **Request Backup / Request Superior** send a dispatch message + a map marker to all officers (Superior only pings equal-or-higher ranks). Each has a **60-second cooldown**.
- You can also toggle emergency lights via the scroll wheel.

### Stopping & restraining suspects

1. **Down or make a suspect surrender** (a surrendered player has hands up).
2. **Restrain** — scroll "Restrain" or use your restrain action key (5-second spam guard). **Cops do NOT need a zip-tie** (civilian bounty hunters do). Target must be within 5 m and nearly stationary.
3. Aim at the restrained suspect and scroll to open the **interaction menu**, giving you: **Unrestrain, Check Licenses, Search Player, Escort Player, Ticket Player, Send to Jail, Put in Vehicle**.
   - **Escort** / **Put in Vehicle** — drag them and load them into a car.
   - **Check Licenses** — see their licenses.
   - **Search Player** — reveals their contraband value, weapons, magazines, illegal clothing, licenses, drug/alcohol level, and any crime "tells" (drug residue, stolen-cash marks). Their contraband value is broadcast to all cops.
4. **Disarm** (scroll, on a **downed/dead** body within 2 m) strips all their weapons into a nearby cop vehicle or the HQ.
5. **Remove Firing Pin** (scroll, on a restrained suspect) disables their guns; they must spend **60 seconds** using "Fix Firing Pin" to repair once unrestrained.

### Tickets

Aim at a restrained suspect within 3.5 m → scroll **Give Ticket** (or the interaction menu button). Enter an amount:
- **Minimum $1, maximum $1,500,000.**
- The suspect gets a pop-up to pay or refuse. If they refuse (close it), it's announced to you and them.
- When they pay, the money is **split evenly among all active on-duty police** (you'll see "Police payout: +$X"), and paying clears their wanted status.
- For any ticket over **$250** you earn cop XP (ticket × 0.0012) and **prestige** (ticket ÷ 25).

### Charging crimes & the wanted list

At **Patrol Officer (rank 2)+**, aim at a restrained suspect within 5 m → scroll **Charge with Crime**. Pick from the charge list; you can apply up to **5 counts at once** for stackable charges. Each charge adds a set **bounty** (the fine/warrant value) and **jail time**. A sample:

| Charge | Bounty | Jail |
|---|---|---|
| Unauthorized Parking | $300 | 30 s |
| Speeding | $100 | 30 s |
| Driving Without A License | $50 | 30 s |
| Robbery | $5,000 | 120 s |
| Bank Robbery / Fed Reserve Robbery | $10,000 | 240 s |
| Murder / Execution | $7,500 | 240 s |
| Kidnapping | $12,500 | 180 s |
| Possession of Cocaine / Meth / Heroin | $7,500 / $10,000 / $5,000 | 60 s |
| Escaping Jail | $10,000 | 300 s |
| Prison Break | $10,000 | 2,400 s |

(Charges 1–110 exist; possession charges scale with the drug.) A suspect's total bounty is the sum of their charges; their total jail time is the sum of charge times **capped at 25 minutes served**.

### Arresting & jail

To jail a **restrained, wanted** suspect (bounty must be > $0):
1. Stand within 3.5 m **inside a police HQ zone**, near the **jail release point (< 30 m)**, or within **20 m of a deployed Mobile HQ**. (In the last 4 minutes of a restart you get a temporary "Send to Jail (Anywhere)".)
2. Scroll **Send to Jail** and confirm. Skiptracer/interrogation spots run a short timed arrest; normal HQ arrests are instant.
3. The bounty is paid out and **split among all active police**, you earn **prestige (bounty ÷ 50)** and **+170 side XP** per arrest. Big-bounty arrests award achievements (over $5k / $75k / $250k).

**Arrest vs. kill:** arresting a wanted criminal pays the **full** bounty. Merely killing them still pays, but reduced (as low as 60% of the bounty, improving to 100% with the right prestige talents) — so cuffs beat bullets for your wallet.

**Combat loggers:** if a wanted player disconnects mid-arrest they drop a **fugitive bodybag**. Scroll **"Send Combat Logger to Jail"** on the bag to jail them anyway.

**What jail is like for the prisoner** (useful to know as a cop): they're stripped, teleported to prison, and must serve their time (up to **25 minutes**), **pay their full bail** (= their bounty), or break out. Turning yourself in to prison is only allowed if your bounty is **≤ $100,000**; above that you must pay the warrant off in full. Prison plates/talents shave time.

### Seizing contraband & vehicles

- **Seize illegal items into Evidence** (at HQ) or **into a Vehicle** (in the field, within 20 m of a car/air) — takes a restrained suspect's illegal virtual items. Requires rank 1+ (HQ) or rank 2+ (field).
- **Search Trunk / Seize Trunk** — inspect and confiscate illegal cargo from any vehicle, tent, or cargo net.
- **Impound Vehicle** (scroll) — sends a civilian vehicle to its owner's garage and pays **$100 (ground) / $200 (air/boat)** to you for cleaning the streets. You can't impound a police vehicle that still has gear in it.
- **Seize Vehicle** (red scroll) — for armed/illegal vehicles (armed Offroads, Ifrit, Strider, etc.). Destroys it and pays **25% of the vehicle's value split among police**. Seizing a Hunter requires **Corporal (rank 3)+**.
- **Search / Seize House Storage** — **Lieutenant (rank 5)+** can search and confiscate a house's stored contraband (within 8 m).
- **Seize Objects** — pick up dropped weapon-holders/loot.

### Bounty tracking (Warrant officers)

If you're in a **Warrant** slot (or a Captain/Chief with the "all" role):
- Use the **fugitive-tracking NPC** ("Track Fugitive") or the scroll **"Track Fugitive 1"** near the bounty marker.
- **Track APB Target** highlights the current All-Points-Bulletin suspect. Arresting the APB target is worth a bonus (and a lucrative quest).
- Your **tracking radius** (how tight the marker is) starts at ~1,400 m and shrinks with tracking talents down to ~200 m — the more talented you are, the more precisely you pin the fugitive.

### MaxTac

MaxTac is a separate whitelist on top of your cop whitelist. When the server flags MaxTac as needed (during federal events):
1. Scroll **"Report for MaxTac Duty"** at the gear/vehicle NPC (limited slots — first come, first served).
2. You're auto-kitted with a **DMR-03 marksman loadout**, plate carrier, smokes and med kits, and teleported to the active event HQ (Pyrgos, Air, Highway or Dump).
3. You get the **MaxTac Armory** and **MaxTac Garage** — Ifrit / Hunter / Strider **$15,000**, Prowler **$6,000**, Qilin **$5,000**, Orca **$20,000**, Van **$6,000** — with **unlimited pulls** while active.
4. When the event ends you're warned at 10 / 5 / 1 minutes, then reverted to patrol gear. You can scroll **"Deactivate MaxTac"** to revert early.

### Mobile HQ (field command post)

**Sergeant (rank 4)+** with the Mobile HQ prestige talent:
- **Buy Mobile HQ ($40,000)** at the vehicle NPC — spawns a MaxSec covered truck.
- Drive it into position, stop, and scroll **"Setup Mobile HQ"**. Talents unlock higher tiers (a ground motor pool, then air + ground).
- Once set up it acts as a forward base: it enables **Send to Jail / seize into evidence** within 20 m, and spawns gear/vehicle NPCs (and a helipad at the higher tier). Scroll **"Pack Up Mobile HQ"** on the base to tear it down.
- **Restrictions:** must be **1,500 m from any federal event**, **1,000 m from gang/cartel areas**, and **30 m from any building**. There's a **30-minute cooldown** between HQ setups (shared MaxSec-wide).

### Security cameras & MaxSec UAV

- Carry a **UAV Terminal** (from the equipment shop, rank 4+) to unlock **"Show Security Cameras"** on the scroll wheel. You then get camera options for key hotspots — **Jewelry Store (desk/gates), Federal Reserve gates, Prison, Bank (vault/doors), and the town squares** (Athira, Kavala, Sofia, Pyrgos) — each usable only when you're within range of that location. Press **4** to close a camera.
- **Deploy MaxSec UAV** — **Captain (rank 6)+** who owns a UAV item can scroll to launch a recon drone; open your UAV terminal (**\ key**) and connect.

### Street Crime Division / undercover

Undercover slots are a **pure whitelist** (set by staff), not a rank — any whitelisted SCD officer takes a `cop_scd` slot at any rank. As an SCD officer you spawn in **civilian-looking gear**, your **rank/identity is hidden**, and your armory unlocks a much wider selection of "illegal-looking" weapons (Katiba, AK-12, MX variants, DMRs, etc.) so you can blend in. Scroll **"Show / Hide Rank Identity"** to reveal or conceal that you're a cop. (Captains/Chiefs also get this undercover option.)

### Revoking civilian licenses

Aim at a restrained civilian and scroll:
- **Revoke Drivers License** / **Revoke Bounty Hunter License** — **Corporal (rank 3)+ (verify: rank 2+ from the gate)**. (Base revoke needs rank 2+.)
- **Revoke BH License (Server Cycle)** — **Corporal (rank 3)+**; blocks them from re-buying the bounty hunter license until the next restart. Use only for genuine abuse.

### Radar & traffic enforcement

- **Radar gun:** press your **radar action key** (User Action 8) to toggle a live speed readout on the vehicle you're aiming at. You need a **P07 in hand**, or a radar talent to use it on foot without the pistol, or the in-vehicle radar talent to run it while seated.
- **Speed cameras** are automatic and civilian-facing: driving a car past a camera at **7+ km/h over the limit** auto-fines the driver **$5**, plays a camera flash, and (if they have no driver's license) adds a "Driving Without A License" charge. The sighting is broadcast to all on-duty cops so you can go intercept.

### Prestige & talents

**Prestige** is the MaxSec progression currency. You earn it from paid tickets (ticket ÷ 25), arrests (bounty ÷ 50), and contraband/vehicle seizures — and higher ranks earn a bigger cut of their squad's payouts. You spend prestige on **talents** that unlock better weapons in your armory, extra gear (flashbangs, better NVGs), abilities (in-vehicle radar, Mobile HQ, tighter fugitive tracking), and quality-of-life perks. Keep arresting and ticketing to climb.

---

## Medic / Trauma Team

Trauma Team (TT) is LooneyBin's medic faction: a whitelisted "green" team whose job is reviving downed players, patching people up, driving ambulances/medical aircraft, and fighting building fires — and who, at higher ranks, become a heavily-armed military branch with their own weapon and vehicle fleet.

### Joining the TT and your rank

- You join the TT by choosing a **medic (independent) slot** at the lobby/spawn screen. If you're on the TT whitelist you spawn at your real rank; if you're **not** whitelisted you still get in as a **TT EMT Volunteer** (an unarmed, basic-kit trial medic) instead of being kicked.
- Ranks, lowest to highest: **EMT Volunteer → TT Private → TT Corporal → TT Sergeant → TT Warrant Officer → TT Lieutenant → TT Commander → TT Marshal**. Your rank is shown before your name everywhere (kill feed, revive menus, when you down someone).
- Rank is stored on your account and is granted/raised by TT command / server staff (there is no in-game "buy a promotion" button). *(verify the exact application/promotion process for your server.)*
- **Volunteers** are deliberately limited: basic medical kit only, **no working weapons** (any shot you fire is voided), no TT Prestige menu, and the lowest gear/vehicle access (roughly Private-level vehicles). Everything below assumes at least Private unless noted.
- TT members and cops draw a **government paycheck of $10,000 every 5 minutes**. TT members start each life with about **$6,500** in the bank.

### Where you operate

TT hospitals / HQs sit in the major towns — **Kavala, Pyrgos, Athira, Sofia**, the central **Air HQ**, and a base near the **garbage dump (Feres side)**, plus the main military hospital in central Altis. At any TT HQ, walk up to the medic NPCs and use the scroll wheel. The actions you'll see (TT members):

- **TT Equipment Shop** — weapons, ammo, tools, NVGs, smokes, the UAV terminal (rank-gated, see below).
- **TT Item Shop** — medical consumables (defibs, blood bags, adrenaline, FAKs, supply-drop flare, fuel cans, road cones).
- **Loadout System** and **TT Uniform Shop (Trauma Team Locker)** — save/load kits, buy TT uniforms/backpacks, and the Revive Drone kit.
- **Redeploy Me!** — teleport to any TT spawn point (shows how many players are near each; blocked while you're in combat).
- **Medical Assistance** — a doctor fully heals you for **$50** over ~7 seconds (stay within 5 m). Available to everyone, and it also clears the "unprofessionally revived" weakness.
- **Respond to Active Fire** — appears only when a building is currently burning (firefighting, see below).
- Vehicle NPCs at each HQ sell **ground, air, and boat** medical vehicles (see the vehicle tables below).

You also carry a **GPS** at all times and get a live map of every downed player and every on-duty medic (see "Map & requests").

### Reviving downed players (your main job)

When a player is knocked out they get a death screen with a **"Request TT"** button and a 2-minute cooldown between requests. Pressing it flags them as revivable and pings every online medic (and medic-cops).

**As a medic:**
1. You get a notification "*<name> is requesting a Trauma Team*" and a message telling you how far away they are. **Press 8 to accept**, or open the **TT Dispatch** respond menu to see all pending patients (distance, direction, nearest town, whether they're still revivable, who else is responding, and how long ago they called). Accepting marks you en-route and draws a red hospital icon over the body.
2. Get within ~3.5 m, look at the body, scroll and pick **"Use Defibrillator"** (you must be carrying a **Defibrillator** item). A progress bar plays; stay close and don't deploy your weapon or it cancels.
3. You can only revive players who **requested a medic / hit Allow**, or who are **in your group**. There's a short ~3-second cooldown between revive attempts.

**Revive speed (medic):** roughly **15 seconds**, dropping to about **10 seconds** with the *Life Saver V* talent.

**Revive payout (medic):** you're paid **$2,500** for reviving another TT member, or **$3,500** for reviving a civilian or cop. You also gain **+50 side XP and +250 TT Prestige** per revive. If other TT medics are close by they're counted as partners and the cash reward is **split evenly** among you.

**Cooldowns / caps:**
- A player you revive is immediately revivable again by other TT (TT revives set no re-down lockout on the patient).
- A recently-revived patient normally can't be revived again for a cooldown window unless you're **Lieutenant (rank 5)+**.
- Your defibrillator has a short internal recharge, but TT medics are **not** hit by the long civilian "defibs recharging" lockout — you can work back-to-back.

**The downed player pays, not you:** whoever gets revived has **$2,500** taken from their bank (only **$300** for brand-new players). If they can't afford it their bank drops to $0.

### Blood transfusions, First Aid, and Adrenaline

These heal living-but-hurt players. Scroll actions appear when you carry the item and are near a valid target:

- **Blood Transfusion** ("Give Blood Transfusion" / "Blood Transfusion Self") — consumes a **Blood Bag**, sets the target to full health. Takes ~20 seconds base; the **Life Saver** talents cut this hard (≈16 s → 10 s → 5 s). Works while prone. Gives **+5 side XP / +25 TT Prestige**.
- **First Aid Kit** ("Treat Yourself" / "Treat Player") — consumes a **First Aid Kit**, heals a chunk of damage (down to 0.25) and fixes broken legs. Fast (~7 seconds). Gives **+5 side XP / +15 TT Prestige**.
- **Adrenaline Shot** ("Give Adrenaline Shot") — consumes an **Adrenaline Shot**, used on a **downed but not dead** player to perk them up. Quick (~2 seconds). Gives **+15 side XP**, and **+25 TT Prestige** when used on someone else.
- The *Life Saver I* prestige perk gives a **chance not to consume** the FAK/blood bag/adrenaline on use.

### TT Item Shop prices

Buy these at any TT HQ (also the Hospital shop for civilians). Buy prices:

- **First Aid Kit** — $75
- **Blood Bag** — $159
- **Defibrillator** — $961
- **Adrenaline Shot** — $1,425
- **Medical Supply Drop** (flare) — $5,100
- **Full Fuel Can** — $75 · **Nitrous Injector** — $1,602 · **RedGull** — $160

TT medics **spawn with First Aid Kits only** — buy your defibs, blood bags and adrenaline before heading out.

### TT weapons (TT Equipment Shop)

Weapon access unlocks by rank (a "Volunteer" counts as rank 0 and gets none of the primaries). Highlights by rank:

- **Private (1):** SMGs and pistols — SMG-05 $3,500, PDW2000 $3,000, Vermin/SMG-01 & Sting/SMG-02 $4,000, P07 $1,000.
- **Corporal (2):** assault rifles — MX $3,500, MXC $3,000, Promet/MSBS $3,500, Katiba $4,000, SDAR $1,500, plus smoke access.
- **Sergeant (3):** marksman/heavier — DMR-06 $4,250, SPAR-02 $5,000, MXM $4,500, MX 3GL $5,000, Hunter shotgun $6,000, heavy pistol $2,500.
- **Warrant Officer (4):** Flare Gun $1,000.
- **Lieutenant (5):** snipers — DMR-01 $3,250, DMR-03 $4,250, EBR $5,200, MX SW $5,000, **UAV Terminal $1,000**.
- **Commander (6):** AK-12 $4,500, Mk200 LMG $9,000, LMG-03 (SPMG) $30,000.
- **Marshal (7):** Zafir LMG $50,000, RPK-12 $20,000, DMR-04 $6,000.

**Special medic sidearm (prestige-locked):**
- **Medic P07 (Heal/Taser)** — $20,000, needs the *Combat Medic* prestige perk. A green P07 that fires (most medic weapons void their shots). In **taser mode** it stuns/downs; in **heal mode** each hit **heals the target +10% HP** and deals no damage.
- **P07 Suppressor** — $2,000, needs the next prestige perk.
- The *I AM A SURGEON!* prestige perk lets you **toggle the P07 between Healing Rounds and Takedown Rounds**.

### TT vehicles (ground / air / sea)

Bought from the vehicle NPCs at TT HQs; access is rank-gated. Buy prices (independent):

**Ground (med_car):**
- Rank 1: Transport Van $2,000 · Offroad $2,500 · Medevac Van $5,000 · Transport Truck $8,000
- Rank 2: Prowler (unarmed) $12,000 · HEMTT Medical $15,000
- Rank 3: Armed Offroad $25,000 · Hunter $30,000 · Strider $35,000 · **HEMTT Fuel (the "TT tempest fuel" firefighting refuel truck)** $15,000
- Rank 6: Ifrit $35,000
- Rank 7: AMV-7 Marshall (armed APC) $150,000

**Air (med_air):**
- Rank 3: Mohawk $60,000 · Cessna $50,000 · Orca $55,000 · Taru (medevac) $70,000
- Rank 4: Taru $75,000 · Xi'an (infantry) $75,000
- Rank 5: UAV $40,000 · Gryphon CAS jet $150,000
- Rank 6: Ghost Hawk $200,000 · Huron $180,000
- Rank 7: Blackfish $300,000

**Sea (med_ship):**
- Rank 1: Speedboat $2,000 · Assault Boat $3,000
- Rank 3: Rescue Boat $3,000 · Transport Boat $5,000

Some marquee vehicles are unlocked by **TT Prestige perks instead of (or on top of) rank** — see below. Buying vehicles above a stock ambulance also requires the matching **Transportation** talent (helicopters need *Transportation IV*, protective/armored vehicles need *Transportation V*).

**Sirens & lights:** in the driver's seat of any medic vehicle, scroll for **Emergency Lights (ON/OFF)**, which also runs the siren — a loud wail heard out to **650 m**. The medevac van/ambulance is the classic siren truck.

### The Revive Drone (Sergeant+)

A remote medical drone that revives and treats people from the air.

- **Requirements:** TT **Sergeant (rank 3)+**, the *"Do drones dream of electric sheep?"* prestige perk, the **Medic Revive Drone** backpack ($20,000, from the Trauma Team Locker), and a **UAV Terminal** to fly it (sold in the TT Equipment Shop at Lieutenant/rank 5+).
- Deploy the drone, connect via the terminal, and fly it over a patient. While piloting through the drone, press **R** for the treatment menu (**Revive / Blood Bag / First Aid / Adrenaline**) and **F** for medic lights + siren. Targets must be within ~10 m below the drone.
- **Battery:** 5 revive charges + 10 support charges (blood/FAK/adrenaline, any mix). A drone revive pays the pilot **$3,500** (+50 XP). Run both to zero and the drone goes **out of battery** for a **2-hour recharge** (an on-screen timer counts down; it recharges even while packed away). The battery survives relogs and restarts.
- If a non-Sergeant grabs the drone kit off the ground, it self-destructs.

### Medical Supply Drop (Sergeant+)

- Buy a **Medical Supply Drop** flare ($5,100) and use it from your inventory. TT **Sergeant (rank 3)+** only.
- It parachutes a crate onto your position stocked with **15 First Aid Kits + 2 Medikits**, tagged to you. The crate despawns after ~30 minutes.
- **Cooldown: one supply drop per 15 minutes, server-wide**, and you can only have **one active crate** at a time.

### Firefighting (Fire & Rescue)

When an arsonist torches a building, TT dispatch sounds and the burning house is marked on the map. Any TT member can respond — firefighting is open to all of Trauma Team.

1. At a TT HQ, scroll **"Respond to Active Fire"** and confirm. You're redeployed to the nearest HQ and issued full firefighter kit: **red construction coveralls, a regulator/gas mask, an SCBA (oxygen) backpack, plate carrier, pilot helmet, smokes/chemlights**, plus a **fire hose and fire axe** on your back. The target building is marked for you.
2. **Fill your hose:** your hose starts at **0 / 100 gallons**. Drive/spawn a **HEMTT Fuel ("TT tempest fuel")** truck and use the fill action (stand still). Fill rate ~**+1 gallon per half-second** (doubled with the *Rapid Refill* perk).
3. **Put out the fire:** aim the hose at the burning building (within a 65° cone) and turn it on. You must **stand still** — moving more than 25 m auto-shuts the hose. It drains **2 gallons per tick** (halved with *High-Pressure Hose*) and adds **+1% progress per ~2 seconds** (doubled with *Fire Marshal*). Reach **100%** to complete your contribution.
4. **Wear the gear.** Anyone inside the fire zone (about a 13 m radius) **without** the red coveralls + regulator mask takes burning damage (~0.04 per half-second) and catches fire. This includes civilians — stay clear of burning buildings.

**Payout:** every firefighter who reaches 100% is paid **$25,000** (**$31,250** with the *Hazard Pay* perk), plus **+150 side XP and +150 TT Prestige**. If nobody extinguishes it, the house **burns to ruins after 8 minutes**. Up to 5 fires can be active at once.

### TT Prestige perks

Open the **"TT Prestige"** phone app (available at **Sergeant/rank 3+**) and spend TT Prestige points (earned from revives, blood bags, FAKs, adrenaline, firefighting, house repairs). Four branches:

- **Armor:** Field Medic (Tier-2 vests, 5k) → Deer with Kevlar (Tier-3, 5k) → Mommy's special boy (skate helmets, 5k) → Luke Cage (Tier-4/EOD vests, 10k) → Mild Concussion (pilot/crew helmets, 10k).
- **Air:** Flight Medic (Hummingbird, 10k) → Call the waahmbulance! (Hellcat, 20k) → Death Flight (Orca, 20k) → *Do drones dream of electric sheep?* (Revive Drone, 20k) → Trauma Team Executive Package (Xi'an medical VTOL, 50k).
- **Ground:** Baby Driver (Hatchback Sport, 5k) → My Summer Car (Qilin, 10k) → Why does a medic need a tank? (Hunter, 20k) → …II (Strider, 30k).
- **Weapons:** Combat Medic (P07 sidearm, 50k) → Shhh let me heal you (P07 suppressor, 50k) → I AM A SURGEON! (P07 heal/takedown toggle, 50k).

### TT standard talents

Open the **Talents** phone app and spend points in the independent tree:

- **Self Protection I–II** — unlock ballistic vests, then helmets.
- **Transportation I–V** — free toolkit-less vehicle repairs; unlock ambulances/SUVs, heavy vehicles, helicopters, then armored/protective vehicles.
- **Life Saver I–VI** — chance to not consume medical gear; faster blood bags (20% → 50% → 75%); revives cut to ~10 seconds; and finally, players you revive get only a **1-minute** re-down cooldown.
- **Personal Gain I–III** — bigger revive reward and improved paychecks.
- **Fire & Rescue: Fire Marshal** (hose twice as fast) → **High-Pressure Hose** (half the water) / **Rapid Refill** (refill twice as fast) / **Hazard Pay** (+25% firefighting pay).

### For civilians and cops

- **Getting revived:** when downed, hit **"Request TT"** on the death screen to call medics, then wait. Any medic who reaches you revives you for a **$2,500** bank fee ($300 for new players). You can also be revived by a **civilian with a defibrillator** or, in a group, a squadmate — but an **amateur (civilian) revive leaves you weakened** (reduced health, forced to walk, drained stamina for ~40 seconds) and blocks blood bags on you for ~10 minutes.
- **Civilians can buy** a Defibrillator ($961), Blood Bags and FAKs at the Hospital NPC and self-revive/heal others. Civilians and cops who revive someone are also paid the revive fee.
- **Medical dispensaries** (separate "Medicinal Dispensary" NPCs in several towns) sell cannabis/medicinal products, not emergency gear.
- **Stay away from burning buildings** unless you're a suited-up firefighter — the fire zone will cook you.

### Tips & gotchas

- You need a **Defibrillator** in your inventory to revive at all, and **Blood Bags / Adrenaline / FAKs** for the other actions — stock up at the TT Item Shop before every patrol.
- A patient must be **flagged revivable (they requested a medic / hit Allow) or in your group** — you can't revive random bodies that never called.
- Don't have your weapon deployed (bipod down) and don't walk off mid-action — the revive/transfusion/FAK bar cancels.
- Higher rank = better guns *and* vehicles, but the flashy aircraft/armor also need the matching **TT Prestige perk** and **Transportation** talent.
- Firefighting is the fastest single payout in the branch ($25k+ per fire) but demands the full fire suit and a fuel truck for water.

---

## Bounty Hunting

Bounty hunting is the civilian path to hunting down wanted criminals for cash: you buy a licence, get assigned a fugitive, track them across the map with a search circle, stun and restrain them, and drag them to jail for a payout equal to their wanted value.

### What It Is, In One Line

As a licensed bounty hunter you request bounties on wanted players, track them by map circle, restrain them with non-lethal weapons, and jail them for money and Honor -- effectively a freelance version of MaxSec's warrant squad.

### Getting Started: The Bounty Hunter Licence

You become a bounty hunter in two steps, and you must be a **civilian** (cops and Trauma Team cannot).

1. **Unlock the Vigilante talent first.** Open your phone, go to the **Talents** app, and spend a talent point on **Vigilante** (the entry bounty-hunter talent). Its description: "Able to utilize Bounty Hunter abilities. Max bounty $50,000." You cannot buy the licence without it.
2. **Buy the licence at a Skiptracer NPC.** Scroll-wheel the Skiptracer (a plain-clothes man in a red-and-white polo shirt) and choose **"Bounty Hunter Training ($12,500)"**. This costs **$12,500** and grants the Bounty Hunter Licence.

**Skiptracer NPC locations (3 across Altis):**
- Map grid position `[17787, 14657]` -- north-east of Pyrgos (verify exact town)
- Map grid position `[5529, 15015]` -- western Altis (verify exact town)
- Map grid position `[25290, 21795]` -- near Sofia in the north-east (verify exact town)

**Important -- the licence replaces your Rebel Licence.** The Bounty Hunter Licence and the Rebel Licence are mutually exclusive. Buying the bounty licence removes your rebel licence (and buying the rebel licence later removes your bounty licence and your bounty-hunter status). The training action even labels itself "(replace rebel)". Choose your path.

### Requesting a Bounty

Once licensed, you can get assigned a fugitive from either:
- The **Bounty** phone app ("Request a new bounty"), or
- The **"Request Bounty"** scroll-wheel action on a Skiptracer NPC.

Requesting picks a random eligible wanted player and drops a red search circle on your map. Rules that govern who you can be assigned:
- The target must be a **civilian** who is **online, alive, and not already arrested**.
- Their wanted value must be **at least $25,000** and **no higher than your personal maximum** (see talent table below).
- They cannot be **you**, a **member of your group or gang**, or a player in **passive/invisible mode**.
- They must be at least **750 metres** from the police spawn (no camping the jail).
- Higher-wanted players are **more likely** to be chosen (selection is weighted by wanted amount).
- You have a **5-second cooldown** between bounty requests.

**Your maximum bounty value and how many you can hold at once are set by your talents:**

| Talent | Max single bounty | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Vigilante (base) | $50,000 | 2 active bounties |
| Bounty Hunter 1 | $100,000 | 2 active bounties |
| Bounty Hunter 2 | $150,000 | + see group members' tracked targets on your map |
| Motivated | (uses your BH-tier cap) | Raises active bounties to **4** at once |
| Master Bounty Hunter | Unlimited (effectively up to $5,000,000) | Unlocks Skiptracer + Honor system |

Base players (no Motivated talent) can hold **2** bounties at a time; with **Motivated** you can hold **4**. If you try to request past your cap you get "You already have a maximum of X bounties."

### Tracking Your Fugitive

When a bounty is assigned you get:
- A **red diagonal-grid circle** on your map labelled "Fugitive [name]", centred on a random point near the target.
- A task marker ("Bounty Target") and a system-chat line telling you the target, their wanted value, and rough distance/location.

The **circle radius is your search accuracy** -- the smaller it is, the tighter you've pinned them:
- **300 m** -- base (no Honor upgrades)
- **250 m** -- with Novice Bail Agent (Honor tree)
- **210 m** -- with Improved Bail Agent
- **175 m** -- with Master Bail Agent

**Re-tracking (updating their location):** walk **inside the circle** and use the scroll-wheel action **"Track Fugitive 1/2/3/4"** (one per active bounty) to re-centre the circle on their current position. You **cannot track from inside a vehicle** -- you must be on foot.

Re-track cooldown (how often you can refresh a target):
- **180 seconds** -- base
- **150 seconds** -- with Basic Snitches (Honor tree)
- **120 seconds** -- with Improved Snitches
- With **Master of Information** you can actively track **2 separate targets within any 2-minute window**.

Extra tracking perks:
- **Bounty Hunter 2** talent shows your group-mates your tracked targets (orange circles) so you can hunt as a team.
- **Master Crusader** (Honor tree) draws an in-game waypoint and a guide line to the centre of your most recently tracked circle.

A bounty automatically drops off your list if the target **dies, respawns, goes invisible/passive, or gets arrested** -- you'll be notified and can request a new one.

### Capturing and Jailing

1. **Stun and restrain the target.** Use a non-lethal (stun/rubber-bullet) weapon to down them, then restrain them. You may only jail a target **you personally restrained** as a bounty hunter -- you cannot jail someone a cop restrained.
2. **Bring them to a jail point.** Drag/escort the restrained fugitive to the **prison release area** or to a **Skiptracer/Interrogation facility**.
3. **Scroll-wheel "Send to Jail"** on the restrained target. You must be **within 3.5 m** of them, and **within 19 m of the prison point** OR **within 15 m of a Skiptracer/Interrogation facility**. The target must be your assigned bounty.
4. **Hold the arrest.** A progress bar runs for **about 20 seconds** (about 18 seconds if you've completed the "Vigilante Flash" quest). During a standard arrest you must stay within **10 m** of your start point and keep the target within **5 m**. **Press 8 to cancel.** The arrest fails if you move too far, enter a vehicle, get downed/killed, or the target breaks free.

At a **Skiptracer or Interrogation facility** the arrest is far more forgiving: you can stand up to **100 m** away and the target can be up to **30 m** away during the arrest.

On a successful jail, the fugitive is sent to prison, their wanted status is cleared, and you are paid.

### Rewards and Payouts

Bounty payouts go to your **bank account**, not on-hand cash:
- **Base payout = the fugitive's full wanted value**, capped at **$9,999,999**.
- If your target is the server's current **All-Points Bulletin (APB)** target, the payout is **x1.5**.
- Arresting at a **Skiptracer facility** (Master Crusader Honor talent) adds a **+20% bonus**, capped at **$25,000**.
- You earn **Honor** = the bounty value divided by **50** per arrest (spend it in the Honor tree).
- You gain **+170 bounty-hunter experience** per arrest toward your standard talent tree.

**Sharing with your group:** if a group member is within **100 m** when you jail the target, you're asked "Would you like to share the reward?" Choosing to share splits the bounty **evenly** between you and each nearby group-mate. If you take the Honor **Fair Share** line, you also automatically receive a cut of your group-mates' arrests: **5%** (Fair Share), **10%** (Taste of the Action), or **15%** (Claim Your Prize).

### The Standard Bounty-Hunter Talent Tree

Spend talent points in the phone **Talents** app:
- **Vigilante** -- unlocks bounty hunting; max bounty $50,000.
- **Bounty Hunter 1** -- max bounty $100,000.
- **Bounty Hunter 2** -- max bounty $150,000; see group tracked targets.
- **Motivated** -- up to 4 active bounties.
- **Watch Dog** -- get reports when your bounty uses an ATM.
- **Informants** -- rare chance informants spot your bounty in populated areas.
- **Feud** -- access to higher-calibre stunning weapons.
- **Master Bounty Hunter** -- unlimited max bounty; no wanted charge for restraining a group member's bounty; access to buy and pull the civilian **Hunter** armoured vehicle; unlocks the **Skiptracer** facility, Skiptracer shops, and the **Honor** talent system.

### The Honor Tree (Skiptracer Specialties)

Once you have **Master Bounty Hunter**, the phone **Honor** app opens the bounty-hunter prestige tree, bought with the Honor you earn from arrests. It splits into two paths:

**Crusader (lawful hunter):**
- **Bail Agent line** -- shrinks your search circle to 250 m / 210 m / 175 m.
- **Snitches line** -- faster location updates (2.5 min / 2 min).
- **Master of Information** -- track 2 targets per 2-minute window.
- **Fair Share line** -- 5% / 10% / 15% cut of group-mates' arrests.
- **Master Crusader** -- auto waypoint to your tracked circle; access to the **Skiptracer jail facility** (the +20% arrest bonus). Access to the illegal MX-series stun rifle/carbine.

**Predator (outlaw hunter):**
- **Slim Jim / Hotwire line** -- 15% / 30% faster vehicle lockpicking; cheaper vehicle claims.
- **Pickpocket line** -- rob money off a restrained bounty; chance to take high-value items; **Disarming** removes their holstered secondary weapon and grenades.
- **Eluder line** -- 10% / 20% / 30% chance that crimes you commit against your bounty target don't register (so hunting doesn't make you wanted).
- **Apex Predator** -- at the **Interrogation facility** you can strip **all** gear from a captured player, and you can pickpocket/disarm **anyone**, not just your bounty. Access to the illegal MX-series stun weapons.

### Pickpocketing Your Bounty

With the Predator pickpocket talents, once your target is restrained you can rob them:
- **Pickpocket** -- take **money** off your restrained bounty.
- **Improved Pickpocket** -- chance to pull **high-value items** from their inventory.
- **Disarming** -- strips their **holstered secondary weapon and grenades**.
- **Apex Predator** -- lets you use pickpocket/disarm against **any** restrained player, not only your assigned bounty.

### Combat-Log Fugitives (the "Fugitive Bodybag")

If a wanted player rage-quits while **downed or restrained** (i.e. escaping an arrest), the server drops a **"Fugitive Bodybag"** at their logout spot:
- All cops and licensed bounty hunters get a **map ping** (orange dot, "Fugitive Bodybag") that lasts about **60 seconds**, plus a message with distance and direction if you're within 50 m.
- Walk up and **scroll-wheel the bodybag -> "Send Fugitive to Jail"** (you must be within **6 m**).
- This jails the offline fugitive **on their next login** (it persists through a server restart), and pays you the **full bounty plus a combat-log bonus of 50% of the bounty (minimum $10,000)**.
- Only **one** person can claim each bag. Unclaimed bags **despawn after 30 minutes**.

### Bounty-Hunter Shops and Gear

At the Skiptracer NPC (and the Bounty Hunter shop) you can access, while licensed:
- **Bounty Hunter Shop** -- non-lethal / stun weapons for hunting.
- **Bounty Hunter Item Shop** -- bounty-hunter virtual items.
- **Refill Magazines** and an **ATM** on the spot.
- With **Master Bounty Hunter**: the **Loadout System**, **Skiptracer Clothing Shop**, **Skiptracer Weapon Shop**, and **Skiptracer Item Shop**.

If you've completed the "The Punisher" Honor quest, you also gain a scroll-wheel toggle to **Load Lethal / Non-Lethal Rounds for the P07 pistol** while holding one -- switch between downing and lethal shots on the fly.

### The Bounty-Hunter Badge

While you have active bounties you get scroll-wheel actions **"Show Badge" / "Hide Badge"**. Showing your badge puts a **police-style badge icon over your head** in other players' name-tags, marking you as an active bounty hunter; hiding it conceals your status so you can approach targets discreetly.

### Dropping a Bounty

If a target is unreachable, open your **wanted/bounty list**, select the fugitive, and click **Drop**:
- You can drop a bounty once every **5 minutes** (reduced to **4 minutes** if you've completed the "The Dropper" quest).
- A dropped target is excluded from your future bounty requests for about **45 minutes**, so you won't just get re-assigned the same person.

### Quest Perks (Lifetime Quests)

Several lifetime quests upgrade your bounty hunting:
- **The Dropper** (drop 300 bounties) -- drop cooldown 5 min -> 4 min, plus 15,000 Honor.
- **The Punisher** (earn 100,000 Honor) -- ability to load lethal rounds in the P07, plus 5,000 Honor.
- **Vigilante Flash** (arrest 100 bounty targets) -- jail your bounty ~10% faster, plus 5,000 Honor.
- **Crusader** / **Master Blacksmith** -- additional Honor and gear rewards.

There are also achievements: **Bounty Hunter** (50 arrests worth over $5,000) and **Watchdog** (200 such arrests).

### Rules of Conduct and Gotchas

- **You are still a civilian.** Stunning another player with rubber bullets is still RDM unless you **verbally warn them first** and give them a chance to comply. Do not open fire unprovoked.
- **Stand down when MaxSec is present.** Your job is to bring in criminals **when police are not around** -- do not interfere with active police operations.
- **Licence revocation:** MaxSec can revoke your Bounty Hunter Licence. If revoked, you **cannot re-buy it until the next server restart**.
- **Rebel vs. bounty is a hard choice** -- you can only hold one of the two licences at a time.
- **You can't track from a vehicle** -- get out and re-track on foot.
- **Your targets can fight back and profit from it.** Wanted players have their own rewards for killing bounty hunters who have their bounty active, so expect resistance -- approach carefully.
- Minimum bounty value to be assigned a target is **$25,000**; targets under that won't appear.

---

## Casino & Gambling

LooneyBin has four separate ways to gamble your money away: the **Casino** phone app (seven games), the **Lottery** at gas stations, the **LoonyBets** sportsbook in your phone browser, and a **self-exclusion** switch for when it all gets to be too much. Every wager and payout in all of these runs through your **bank balance** (ATM cash), never your on-hand cash — so you need money in the bank to play, and winnings land in the bank.

> **The house always wins.** Every casino game is tuned with a built-in house edge (roughly 5%–12% depending on the game). Over time you *will* lose money — gamble for fun, not for income.

### Where & how to open the Casino

The Casino is a **phone app**, not a building — there's no NPC or map marker to drive to. Open your **phone**, tap the **Casino** icon (the tooltip says "Play blackjack" but it opens the full game menu), and pick from the game-select lobby:

- **Blackjack**
- **Coin Flip**
- **Wheel Spin**
- **Slots**
- **Roulette**
- **Hi-Lo**
- **Crash (aviator)**

**Who can play:** civilians, police (MaxSec), and Trauma Team can all use the casino. You **cannot** gamble while you are:
- Jailed / in a cell
- Restrained (hands tied)
- Dead, downed, or knocked out
- In combat (combat timer active)

If you've self-excluded yourself (see below), the Casino app won't appear on your phone at all.

**Bets are from your bank.** Before every play the bet is pulled from your bank balance; if you don't have the funds you'll get "Not enough bank funds for that bet." Wins are credited straight back to your bank.

**Chip sizes / bet limits (all casino games except the Wheel):** the five chips are **$10,000 / $50,000 / $100,000 / $250,000 / $500,000**. Minimum bet is **$10,000**, maximum bet is **$500,000**.

---

### Blackjack

Classic 21 against the dealer, played on your phone.

**How to play:**
1. Open Casino → **Blackjack**.
2. Pick a chip to set your bet ($10k–$500k), then press **DEAL**.
3. You get two cards, the dealer shows one. Use **HIT** (take a card), **STAND** (keep your total), **DOUBLE**, or **SPLIT**.
4. Beat the dealer's hand without going over 21.

**Rules & payouts (exact):**
- A normal win pays **even money** (you get your bet back plus an equal amount — 2x total return).
- A **natural blackjack** (Ace + 10-value on your first two cards) pays **3:2** (your bet back plus 1.5x your bet).
- A **push** (tie) returns your bet.
- The **dealer stands on 17**, including soft 17.
- **Double:** only on your first two cards — you add a second equal bet, take exactly one more card, then auto-stand. (Bank is debited again when you double.)
- **Split:** if your two cards are equal value, you can split into two hands (adds another bet equal to the base). You can split up to **4 hands** total. **Split aces get only one card each** and auto-stand.
- If the dealer has a blackjack, everyone who isn't also holding a blackjack loses immediately (a blackjack vs. dealer blackjack pushes).

**Gotcha — leaving mid-hand forfeits your bet.** The **< LOBBY** button (top-left) is right where you'd reach to close the window. If a hand is in progress it asks you to **tap it twice** to confirm; leaving forfeits the stake you already put in.

---

### Coin Flip

Fastest game in the house. Pick a side, flip, done.

**How to play:**
1. Open Casino → **Coin Flip**.
2. Pick a chip for your bet.
3. Click **HEADS** or **TAILS** to choose your side (gold = selected).
4. Press **FLIP**.

**Payout:** a correct call **doubles your bet** (2x return, net profit = your bet). The coin has a slight tilt against you: you win **46%** of the time, so about **8% house edge**. Use **FLIP AGAIN** to replay.

---

### Wheel Spin (daily prize wheel)

This one is **not** a normal bet game — it's a fixed-cost prize wheel with a free daily spin.

**Cost:**
- **One FREE spin every 24 hours.**
- After your free spin is used, each additional spin costs **$50,000** (from your bank).
- The SPIN button shows "SPIN – FREE DAILY" or "SPIN – $50,000" so you always know which you're getting.

**How to play:** Open Casino → **Wheel Spin**, wait for the button to finish "checking..." (it verifies your free-spin status), then press **SPIN**. Use **SPIN AGAIN** to go again (it re-checks whether your free spin is still available).

**Prize pool (weighted — chances out of 1000):**

| Prize | Chance | What you get |
|-------|--------|--------------|
| Cash | 70% | A random cash prize between **$1,000 and $200,000** (skewed low — average around $50,000), paid to bank |
| Drug Bust | 22.5% | Triggers a **server-wide event**, not a personal payout (see below) |
| Ifrit | 4% | An **Ifrit** (armed MRAP) delivered to your garage |
| Qilin | 2.5% | A **Qilin** (armed LSV) delivered to your garage |
| Blackfish | 1% | A **Blackfish VTOL** delivered to your garage |

- **Drug Bust:** instead of paying you, this fires a server-wide event — **all illegal drug sell prices go up +12% for 5 minutes**. It's announced to everyone, so sell your stash fast. It does **not** stack; if a bust is already running you'll just be told it's already active.
- **Vehicle jackpots** go straight to your personal garage (you'll need to pull them from a garage). 
- **Daily vehicle cap:** you can win at most **3 vehicle jackpots per day**. After that, any further vehicle result **downgrades to a cash prize** for the rest of the day.

---

### Slots

Three-reel slot machine. Match three symbols to win.

**How to play:** Open Casino → **Slots**, pick a chip, press **SPIN**. **SPIN AGAIN** to replay.

**Payouts** — you only win on **three of a kind**, and the payout is a multiple of your bet (the return, so subtract 1x for net profit):

| Three of a kind | Return multiple |
|-----------------|-----------------|
| Cherry | **8x** |
| Lemon | **12x** |
| Bell | **22x** |
| Bar | **45x** |
| Triple Seven (777) | **150x** |

- Overall return-to-player is about **88%** (~12% house edge).
- All five chip sizes work on slots (up to the $500k max bet).
- **Gotcha — the 777 jackpot is heavily nerfed.** A natural triple-seven only actually pays out about **1 in 100** times it would otherwise land; the rest of the time the last reel rerolls to a near-miss. The 150x is real but you will very rarely hit it.

---

### Roulette

American double-zero roulette — **38 pockets**: 0, 00, and 1 through 36. Flat **5.26% house edge** on every bet.

**How to play:**
1. Open Casino → **Roulette**.
2. Pick a chip for your bet amount.
3. Choose a bet: click an outside bet (RED, BLACK, ODD, EVEN, 1-18/LOW, 19-36/HIGH, a dozen, or the green 0 / 00), **or type a number 0–36** into the number box for a straight bet. Typing a number replaces any outside bet you had selected.
4. Press **SPIN**.

**Payouts:**

| Bet | Payout | Notes |
|-----|--------|-------|
| **Straight** (single number, 0–36 or 00) | **36x** return (35:1) | Win only if the ball lands exactly on your number |
| Red / Black / Odd / Even / Low (1–18) / High (19–36) | **2x** (1:1) | — |
| Dozen (1–12, 13–24, 25–36) | **3x** (2:1) | — |

- **Every outside bet loses on both 0 and 00.** That's the house edge. Only a straight bet on 0 or 00 can win those pockets.

---

### Hi-Lo

Guess whether the next card is higher or lower. Build a streak, then cash out before you bust.

**How to play:**
1. Open Casino → **Hi-Lo**, pick a chip, press **DEAL**.
2. A card is shown. Press **HIGHER** or **LOWER** to guess the next card.
3. Each correct guess grows your pot multiplier. Keep guessing to build it, or press **CASH OUT** to bank your winnings.

**Rules & payouts (exact):**
- Each correct guess multiplies your pot. The riskier the call, the bigger the jump; every correct guess grows the pot by **at least 1.05x**.
- **A tie counts as a loss** — if the next card is the *same rank*, you bust. (So calling "higher" on an Ace or "lower" on a 2 is an automatic loss.)
- A wrong guess or a tie ends the run with **nothing**.
- The pot multiplier is **capped at 30x** — the max you can win from one run is **$15,000,000** (on the $500k max bet). Further correct guesses past 30x won't grow it.
- **Cash out anytime** for bet × current multiplier.
- **Gotcha — the early cash-out penalty:** if you cash out **before making any guess** (multiplier still 1.0x), you only get back **50% of your bet**. Make at least one correct guess to remove the penalty. The screen warns you about this.
- **Gotcha — leaving mid-run forfeits your stake.** Pressing **< LOBBY** during a live run asks you to tap twice to confirm; leaving forfeits the bet.

---

### Crash (aviator)

A rising multiplier that can crash at any moment. Cash out before it crashes.

**How to play:**
1. Open Casino → **CRASH (aviator)**, pick a chip, press **DEAL**.
2. A multiplier starts climbing from 1.00x and accelerates upward.
3. Press **CASH OUT** at any time to bank **your bet × the current multiplier**.
4. If the multiplier **crashes** before you cash out, you **lose your bet**.

**Numbers (exact):**
- Roughly **7% of rounds instant-bust** at 1.00x (you lose immediately).
- The multiplier is **capped at 20x**.
- Pace: it reaches about **2x in ~5 seconds** and **10x in ~17 seconds**, so higher targets take real nerve.
- House edge is about **7%**.

---

### Casino self-exclusion ("Casino Ban")

If gambling is becoming a problem, you can permanently block yourself from the casino.

- Open **phone → Settings** and find the **Casino Ban** row.
- Confirm on the "Ban me / LET IT RIDE" prompt.
- This **permanently** removes the Casino app from your phone and blocks you from all casino games.
- **It is permanent — only an admin can undo it.** Use it deliberately.
- (Note: this only covers the Casino app. The Lottery and LoonyBets sportsbook are separate.)

---

### Lottery

A cheap, long-odds jackpot draw run at fuel stops.

**Where:** Walk up to a **gas station attendant** or a **civilian vehicle shop** NPC and use the scroll-wheel actions:
- **"Buy Lottery Ticket ($2,000)"**
- **"Get Lottery Jackpot"** (just displays the current jackpot — free)

**How it works (exact):**
- Each ticket costs **$2,000** from your bank.
- Every ticket is an instant **1-in-1,000** chance to win the entire jackpot — there's no scheduled draw, the roll happens the moment you buy.
- The jackpot **starts at $25,000** and grows by **$1,000 for every losing ticket** anyone buys. When someone wins, it **resets to $25,000**.
- Winnings are paid to your bank with a "Lottery Won!" message.
- **Gotcha — no rapid-buying.** You can't spam tickets; buy them one at a time (there's a short cooldown, "You cannot rapidly buy lottery tickets!"). You also need at least $2,000 in the bank.

**Tip:** check the current jackpot with "Get Lottery Jackpot" before buying — the longer nobody's won, the bigger the pot.

---

### LoonyBets sportsbook

Parimutuel betting on live **heist outcomes** — will the robbers pull it off?

**Where:** Open **phone → Browser → loonybets.com** (also listed in the LoonyNet directory as "LOONYBETS").

**When markets are open:** There are **no standing markets**. A betting window only opens **when a heist alarm actually sounds**. The whole server gets a notification ("THE BOOK IS OPEN") when one starts. Markets exist for:

| Heist | Betting window |
|-------|----------------|
| Bank of Altis | 30 minutes |
| Jewelry Emporium | 25 minutes |
| Federal Reserve | 35 minutes |
| Evidence Lockup | 35 minutes |
| Altis Shipping convoy | 50 minutes |
| Loony Bin breach | 45 minutes |

**How to bet:**
1. Go to loonybets.com in your phone browser while a market is open.
2. Each open market asks a yes/no question (e.g. "Federal Reserve: do the robbers get away with the gold?").
3. Tap **YES** (robbers succeed) or **NO** (they fail).
4. Enter your stake in the popup, then place the bet.

**Rules & payouts (exact):**
- Stake is **$5,000 minimum, $50,000 maximum**, paid from your bank.
- **One bet per player per market** — you can't double up on the same heist.
- It's **parimutuel**: winners get their **stake back plus a share of the losing pool**, split proportionally to how much they staked. The house takes a **10% rake** off the losing pool.
- The odds shown next to YES/NO ("pays 2.40x", etc.) are **live estimates** and shift as more people bet — the final payout depends on the pools at settlement, not when you bet.
- A market settles when its heist resolves (robbers succeed → YES pays, or they're stopped → NO pays). If a window closes with no clear outcome, it **settles NO**.
- If you're **offline** when a bet you won settles, the winnings are added straight to your bank for next login.
- Winners and losers both get a settle notification, and the result is posted to the in-game news.

**Tip:** because it's parimutuel, betting the **less popular** side pays more if it wins — you're splitting a bigger losing pool among fewer winners.

---

## Talents & Achievements & Titles

Your character has three separate progression layers, all opened from apps on your in-game **Phone**: **Talents** (permanent perks you spend points on), **Prestige/Specialities** (deeper perk trees you buy with a special currency), and cosmetic **Achievements** and **Titles** that decorate your name.

---

### Where to find everything (Phone apps)

| App | Who sees it | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| **Talents** | Everyone | Your main talent tree (Civilian / MaxSec / TT). Spend talent points. |
