| **Perks** | Civilians in a gang | Your gang's shared perk tree. |
| **Infamy** | Civilians with a **Rebel License** | Rebel speciality tree (spend Infamy). |
| **Honor** | Civilians with a **Bounty License** + the **Master Bounty Hunter** talent | Skiptracer / bounty-hunter speciality tree (spend Honor). |
| **Prestige** | MaxSec, Corporal and above | Cop speciality tree (spend Prestige). |
| **TT Prestige** | Trauma Team, AEMT and above | TT speciality tree (spend TT Prestige). |
| **Achieve** | Everyone | View achievements and equip an achievement name-prefix. |
| **Titles** | Everyone | Browse stat-milestone titles, equip one, and pick its color. |

---

### Standard Talents

**What it is:** a tree of permanent, always-on perks for your current life (Civilian, MaxSec cop, or Trauma Team). Open the **Talents** app, pick a talent from the tree, read its info panel, and press **SELECT TALENT** to learn it. You need a free talent point available, and if a talent has a prerequisite you must own that first.

**How many points you get (your free cap):**
- **Civilians:** 20 points.
- **Trauma Team:** 16 points.
- **MaxSec Cops:** 20 points, **plus 2 more for every rank above the 2nd** (so a Corporal has 22, a Sergeant 24, a Lieutenant 26, a Captain 28, and the top rank 30).

On the current server you are handed your full point allotment right away, so there is no XP grind to unlock your points. *(This is current beta behavior and may change; the tree still shows an XP/points readout at the top.)*

**Unlearning / respec:** you can unlearn any talent for free (the confirmation says $0). You **cannot** unlearn a talent that still has a learned talent branching off it — remove the child talent first. There is no longer a "forget everything" respec at the DMV; unlearn talents one at a time in the app.

#### Buying extra talent points

Once your free points are all spent, tap **BUY TALENT POINT** in the Talents app to purchase more.
- **1st extra point: $1,000,000.** Each one after costs **$500,000 more** than the last (2nd = $1,500,000, 3rd = $2,000,000, and so on).
- Paid from your **bank**, not on-hand cash.
- Purchased points are permanent and saved to your account.
- Buying is blocked once you already have enough points to learn **every** talent in your tree (68 civilian talents, 36 MaxSec talents, 20 TT talents), so you can never waste money on useless points.

#### Civilian talent tree

**Bounty Hunter line** (needs a Bounty License to actually hunt):
- **Vigilante** — unlocks Bounty Hunter abilities and the phone **Bounty** app; max bounty $50,000.
- **Bounty Hunter 1 / 2** — raise max bounty to $100,000, then $150,000 and let you see group-mates' tracked targets on the map.
- **Motivated** — hold up to 4 active bounties at once.
- **Watch Dog** — alerts when your bounty uses an ATM.
- **Informants** — small chance informants spot bounties in towns.
- **Feud** — access to higher-caliber stun weapons.
- **Master Bounty Hunter** — buy/pull the civilian Hunter, no charge for restraining a group-mate's bounty, **unlimited** max bounty, and unlocks Skiptracer + the **Honor** talent system. *You must drop your Rebel Training (rebel license) before you can take this.*

**Vehicle & licensing:**
- **Silver Tongue** — all new vehicles cost 10% less.
- **Commercial License** — buy/pull large cargo trucks.
- **Ranking Rebel** — buy/pull heavy para-military vehicles.
- **Commercial Pilot** — buy/pull large helicopters. → **Airborne** (air vehicles stocked with parachutes; safety chute on 100m+ slingloads) and **Advanced Tracking** (air vehicles come with a tracker).
- **Weapon Technician** — use vehicle ammo reloads.

**Delivery line:**
- **Delivery Man** — +10% on all delivery-mission payouts. → **Delivery Expert** (+15% on Small Parcel jobs, no vehicle needed) → **Experienced Trucker** (delivery NPCs sell you repair/survival goods at a markup) and **Porch Pirate Protection** (half your DP-mission money auto-deposits to bank, the rest as cash).
- **Precious Cargo** — +20% on Ground Shipment jobs (needs a container-capable vehicle).
- **Delivery Pilot** — +25% on Air Shipment jobs (needs a heavy helicopter).

**Drug runner:** **Drug Hawker** (reduced chance police are alerted to Drug Runner activity) → **Drug Baron** (further reduced).

**Robbery line:**
- **Intimidating** — up to 20% more money robbing stores. → branches: **Heister** (faster bolt-cutters, can fully open gates with a chance to avoid the alarm), **Hacker** (delays store alarms up to 2 min), **Mugger** (20% chance personal robberies aren't reported) → **Professional** (+10% bank payout when you start the robbery), **Systems Engineer** (much faster on evidence vaults), **Safe-Cracker** (use demo charges on bank safes), **Clandestine** (reduced chance bank cameras spot you while masked).

**Mining line:** **Major Miner** (better chance of valuable finds) → **Brawny** (faster mining/metal-detecting) → **Ore Specialist** (more metal ores), **Gem Specialist** (more gems), **Oil Runner** (faster oil, more rubber).

**Vehicle theft:** **Twocker** (better lockpick success) → **Grand Auto Theft** (pick large ground vehicles) → **Airbork** (pick helicopters).

**Survival & utility:**
- **Hunger Games** — food/drink drain reduced 75%.
- **Excavator** — faster shipwreck excavating.
- **Piscator** (medium fishing net) → **Trawler** (large net).
- **Mechanic** (slightly faster repairs) → **Grease Monkey** (1-in-4 chance a repair kit isn't used up).
- **Hawk Eyes** — spawn with NVGs and Binoculars.

**Motorist path:**
- **Road Warrior** — vehicles you pull come with a Toolkit and 3 First Aid Kits in the trunk; unlocks the path.
- **Autopilot Certified** — the GPS Autopilot Module installs **free** (a $30,000 value) on any vehicle you own, at the garage or dealer.
- **Chauffeur** — autopilot gets +30% cornering grip, +12% speed in every mode, and stops crawling through tight hairpins.
- **Networker** — GPS destination search reveals live gang turfs, active cartels, and running events.
- **Pit Boss** — reinforced frame; collision/ram damage reduced 40%.

**Homesteader path:**
- **Homesteader** — property tax on all your homes reduced 40%; unlocks the path.
- **First-Time Buyer** — buy any house or shed for 20% less.
- **Landlord** — all storage upgrades (virtual + physical) cost 35% less.
- **Locksmith** — house keys cost 50% less to cut.
- **Open House** — reach house/shed storage from **25m** away instead of 10m.

**Blue Collar path:**
- **Blue Collar** — +24 max carry weight on foot; unlocks the path.
- **Assembly Line** — every processing job runs 25% faster.
- **Bumper Crop** — every field harvest yields +1 unit → **Rockhound** (mining uranium and fungi pulls +1 per swing).
- **Mule Team** — run at full speed carrying gold bars (no forced walk) and +12 more weight.

**Merchant path:**
- **Broker** — +10% on everything you sell at any market; unlocks the path.
- **Plug** — drug sales at Wong's Triad and turf dealers pay +25% on top of Broker.
- **Wholesaler** — legal-goods sales (ore, oil, produce, etc.) pay +20% on top of Broker.

**Outlaw path:**
- **Chop Artist** — chop shops pay +20% per stolen vehicle; unlocks the path.
- **Hijacker** — convoy robbery payouts +25%.
- **Master Thief** — both chop-shop and convoy jobs pay +15% on top of everything else.

#### MaxSec (Cop) talent tree

Available to police only. Highlights, grouped by line (some entries note the minimum rank in-game):
- **Investigation:** Watch Dog → Informants → Snitches → **Surveillance** (AR-2 Darter drone, Sgt) or **On the Beat** (alerts on dealer/runner deals).
- **Traffic:** Traffic Cop (radar always usable in a police vehicle) → Highway Patrol (any weapon acts as radar) → Traffic Control (place traffic objects).
- **Show of Force:** unlocks the MK20 SMG → MXC/SDAR → MX → MXM/MK.1, plus **Prudent** (tactical vest) and **Stunning** (flashbangs).
- **Mechanic** (repair without tools) → **Grease Monkey** (faster repairs).
- **Nourished** → **Hunger Games** (food/drink drain −25%, then −50%).
- **High Alert** → **Sentry** → **Heedful** (police vehicles get alarms and trackers).
- **First Aid → Level B → Level C** (blood bag heals to 80/90/100) → **Combat Medic** (defib revives).
- **Vehicles:** Country Cop → Patrol Officer → Advanced Driver, plus flight line Flight Training → Advanced Pilot → Flight Transport.
- **Profiteer 1/2/3** — receive 75% → 85% → 100% of an arrested criminal's warrant value, plus a growing bonus on drugs seized from vehicles.
- **Reserve Guard** — instantly unlock Federal Reserve front gates.
- **Night Eyes** — spawn with NVGs on death.

#### Trauma Team talent tree

Available to TT only:
- **Self Protection I/II** — buy ballistic vests (EMT+), then helmets (Paramedic+).
- **Transportation I–V** — fast tool-less repairs, then ambulances/SUVs, heavy vehicles, helicopters, and protective vehicles.
- **Life Saver I–VI** — chance to not consume medical gear, progressively faster blood bags (20/50/75%), faster revives (down to 10 seconds), and a 1-minute revive cooldown for people you revive.
- **Personal Gain I–III** — +20% revive reward, then paychecks of $1,000, then $1,500 every 5 minutes (requires a soft-log to kick in).
- **Fire & Rescue path:** **Fire Marshal** (hose knocks flames down 2× faster) → **High-Pressure Hose** (put out fires with half the water) → **Rapid Refill** (refill at the TT tempest 2× faster), plus **Hazard Pay** (all firefighting payouts +25%).

---

### Gang Perks (gang talent tree)

**What it is:** a shared perk tree for your whole gang, opened from the **Perks** phone app (civilians in a gang only). Perks benefit every member.

- **Points:** up to **9**, earned as your gang levels up. Thresholds are 36, 97, 190, 320, 490, 690, 920, 1180, and 1500 gang XP for points 1 through 9. Gang XP comes from gang-war kills and capturing cartels (+5 XP each).
- **Who can edit:** only the gang **leader or officer** (ranks 1 and 2). Lower ranks can view the tree but can't spend points.
- **Perks:** market discount line (**Gang Groupon** −10% at rebel markets → **Hagglers** −5% clothing/gear → **Offer He Can't Refuse** −5% weapons), a sell-price line (**Fence** +5% at Wong's/dealers → **Kingpin** +2% dealers → **Bootlegger** +2% Wong's), **Flyboys** (spawn air vehicles at owned turf), and a gathering line (**Drug Pushers** faster coke/heroin/cannabis → **Distributors** faster ephedra/henbane).

---

### Prestige & Speciality trees

Beyond standard talents, four deeper trees let you spend a special earned currency on high-end perks. Each speciality has a fixed currency cost and may require a prerequisite. Learning is instant; you don't spend talent points, you spend the currency.

#### Infamy (Rebel) — "Infamy" app

**Requires a Rebel License.** You can hold up to **21** infamy specialities. Unlearning one **refunds** its infamy but **costs $5,000**. Infamy is earned through rebel and gang-war combat and some quests.

Costs (Infamy):
- **Combat/lockpick line:** Specialist (1,000) → I got this (2,000) → Break it open (5,000) → Skilled fingers (10,000) → Lucky fingers (15,000); side branch Plate presser (2,000) → More wings (5,000) → Can't see shit (10,000).
- **Medic line:** Medic (1,000) → Second use (2,000) → Golden blood (5,000) → Quick patch up I (10,000) → II (15,000); Life saver (2,000) → Every second counts (5,000) → A good heart (10,000) → Get the f up! (15,000).
- **Drug Lord line:** Drug Lord (1,000) → Snoop Dogg (2,000) → Poppy (5,000) → Cokehead (10,000) → Junky (20,000); Invisible (2,000) → Pablo Escobar (5,000) → On crack (10,000) → Dollar bill (20,000).
- **Arms Dealer line:** Arms Dealer (1,000) → Keep spraying (2,000) → Takes ages (5,000) → AutoScout (10,000) → Thrift shop (20,000).

#### Honor / Skiptracer (Bounty Hunter) — "Honor" app

**Requires a Bounty License and the Master Bounty Hunter talent.** You can hold up to **15** honor specialities. Unlearning **refunds** honor but **costs $5,000**. Honor is earned from bounty arrests (about **1 honor per $50** of the target's bounty; group-mates share a smaller cut) and some quests. The two master specialities (Master Crusader, Apex Predator) require all their prerequisite branches first.

Costs (Honor):
- **Crusader tree:** Crusader (500) → Novice/Improved/Master Bail Agent (2,000 / 10,000 / 25,000); Basic/Improved Snitches → Master of Information (2,000 / 10,000 / 25,000); Fair Share → Taste of the Action → Claim Your Prize (2,000 / 10,000 / 25,000, paying 5/10/15% of a group-mate's arrest); **Master Crusader** (50,000) — auto waypoint to the last tracked target + Skiptracer jail access.
- **Predator tree:** Predator (500) → Slim Jim (2,000) → Hotwire apprentice (10,000) → Lockpick and a Dream (25,000); Pickpocket (2,000) → Improved Pickpocket (10,000) → Disarming (25,000); Novice/Improved Eluder → Master of Elusion (2,000 / 10,000 / 25,000); **Apex Predator** (50,000) — strip all gear at the interrogation facility, pickpocket/disarm anyone.

#### MaxSec Prestige — "Prestige" app

**MaxSec only (Corporal and above to open).** Spend **Prestige**, earned from arrests (roughly **bounty ÷ 50 × 0.4** prestige per arrest, shared with nearby cops). Cop prestige specialities **cannot** be unlearned, so choose carefully. Many entries have a rank gate (SGT+, LT+, CPT ONLY) shown in-game.

Costs (Prestige):
- **Warrant Squad (tracking radius):** Novice Man Tracker (1,000) → Improved (5,000) → Corporal Master (10,000) → Sergeant (20,000) → Sergeant Master (30,000) → Lieutenant Manhunter (40,000) → Master Manhunter (50,000), tightening the fugitive circle from 1 km down to 200 m. **Update speed:** Basic (5,000) → Improved (20,000) → Cultivated (35,000) → Master of Information (65,000), from 8-minute down to 2-minute updates.
- **Combat Medicine:** I/V Beginner (10,000) → Technician (15,000) → Expert (25,000) → Master (40,000); Triage Technician (25,000) → Expert (35,000) → Master (45,000) → Master Medic (65,000) → Master Combat Medic (100,000).
- **Undercover:** Unmarked Vehicles (1,000) → Improved (15,000) → Advanced (30,000) → Vehicle Master (65,000); Basic Air (10,000) → Improved Air (35,000) → Advanced Air (65,000).
- **Mobile HQ** (40,000) → Vehicle garage technician (50,000) → Air garage technician (75,000).
- **Leadership:** Tacticool (5,000) → Allied Armory / spawn with an MX (20,000) → Invested / Carrier Light (30,000) → Armory Anarchy / spawn with an MXM (20,000).

#### TT Prestige — "TT Prestige" app

**Trauma Team, AEMT and above only.** Spend **TT Prestige**, earned from TT actions — revives, blood bags, FAKs, putting out fires, clearing wreckage, and repairing houses. Cannot be unlearned.

Costs (TT Prestige):
- **Armor:** Field Medic (5,000) → Deer with Kevlar (5,000) → Mommy's special boy (5,000) → Luke Cage (10,000) → Mild Concussion (10,000).
- **Air:** Flight Medic / Hummingbird (10,000) → Hellcat (20,000) → Orca (20,000) → medical revive drone (20,000) → Xi'an medical VTOL (50,000).
- **Ground:** Baby Driver / Hatchback Sport (5,000) → Qilin (10,000) → Hunter (20,000) → Strider (30,000).
- **Weapons:** Combat Medic / P07 sidearm (50,000) → P07 suppressor (50,000) → "I AM A SURGEON!" (50,000) — toggle your P07 between Healing Rounds (+10 HP/shot) and Takedown Rounds.

---

### Achievements

**What it is:** milestones that unlock automatically the moment you meet their condition (you get an "Achievement Unlocked" popup). Open the **Achieve** app to see them all — unlocked ones are in normal color, locked ones are red. Some achievements come with a **reward unlock**, and some let you wear a colored **name prefix**.

**How to equip a prefix:** in the Achieve app, select an unlocked achievement that has a title and press the button to apply it (e.g. "Warlord Steve"). Pick **"Remove title"** at the top of the list to clear it. Each prefix has its own fixed color.

**Money achievements** (bank balance; all sides):
- **Prestigious** ($500,000), **Upper Class** ($1,000,000), **Prominent** ($3,000,000, reward: **Jury Corruption**), **Tycoon** ($6,000,000, reward: **Global Discounts**), **Wolf Pack** ($8,000,000, reward: **Wolf Dew Sport Hatchback**), **Eminent** ($10,000,000, reward: **Purchasable Jet**).

**Gang-war kills** (civilian):
- **Thug** (25), **Enforcer** (100), **Warlord** (250, reward: **Free Vehicle Reloads**), **Death Harbringer** (1,250), **Doom Slayer** (2,500).

**War rating** (civilian): **Oppressor** (200), **The Punisher** (750), **Reaper of Death** (5,000 in current season).

**Arrests:**
- MaxSec: **Blue Coat** (50), **The Heat** (200), **Hardened** (400), **Veteran** (800).
- Bounty hunter: **Bounty Hunter** (50), **Watchdog** (200).
- Also awarded live on the arrest itself: **Rookie Blue** ($5,000 target), **Baconator** ($75,000), **Hardened Cop** ($250,000).

**APB evasion** (civilian): **Evaded** (1), **Elusive** (5), **The Invisible** (10).

**Quest / event achievements** (civilian): **Gang Wars Champion** (reigning champ), **Obliterator**, **Diabolical**, **Spinner**, **Menace** (each from completing a specific quest).

**Hidden/legacy achievements** exist too (e.g. **Seasoned** → US 6-Color Desert Camo, **Old School**, **2k19 Promotion Player**) and won't appear in your list unless already earned.

---

### Titles

**What it is:** a second, separate name decoration earned from **lifetime stat milestones** (kills, sales, playtime, etc.), independent of achievements. Open the **Titles** app — it fetches your stats, then shows every title for your side; unlocked ones are normal, locked ones red.

**How to equip:** select an unlocked title and press the button to wear it, or **"Remove Title"** to clear it. Unlike achievement prefixes, you choose the **color** yourself.

**Coloring your title:** use the title color menu to pick from Red, Orange, Yellow, Chartreuse Green, Green, Spring Green, Cyan, Azure, Blue, Violet, Magenta, Rose, Black, or White. Your choice is saved and applied to whatever title you wear.

**Titles are stat-gated** and the app shows the exact requirement for each. A sampling of the many titles and their thresholds:
- **Cop-kills:** Freedom Fighter (250), BluFor Butcher (500), Pig Farmer (1,000), Pack Watch (1,200), BluFor Exterminator (1,500), 12 Annihilator (3,000), Robopig Tamer (3,500); plus headshot titles MaxSec Mald Maniac (300) and Anti-Robocop Deadshot (750).
- **War kills / war rating:** Darth Vader (1,000), Hell Demon (1,500), Horsemen of the Apocalypse (4,000); rating titles Ghost Rider (1,000) up to Angel Of Death (25,000).
- **Drugs & processing:** The Cook / Meth Head / Heisenberg (500 / 1,000 / 5,000 meth), Walter White (20,000 Heisenberg Meth), Colombian Entrepreneur → Pablo Escobar (1,000 → 7,500 coke bricks), J. Robert Oppenheimer (5,000 uranium).
- **Booze:** Homebrewer / Distiller / Bootlegger (250 / 1,000 / 4,000 scotch), Blue Label Scotch (7,500), Appalachian Moonshiner → Popcorn Sutton (2,000 → 4,500 moonshine).
- **Prison / crime:** Convict / Enemy of the State / Caged (300 / 600 / 900 minutes served), Institutionalized (500 plates pressed), Alcatraz Warden (25 prison breaks).
- **Cop & medic playtime/earnings:** Rookie (50h), True Officer (200h), No Life (500h), Career Cop (1,000h); Good Samaritan (50h medic), Surgeon (100h medic); plus cop/medic earnings ladders up to $5m / $4m.
- **Casino & lottery:** High Roller / Big Baller (win $300k / $600k), Jackpot! (win $3,000,000 lottery), Ace of Spades (500 blackjacks), Gambling Addiction (lose 500 blackjacks).
- **Events & feats:** Gun Game Champion, Fortnite Royaler (battle royale), Graveyard Keeper, Turf Rat (35 turfs), Cartel Sweater (75 cartels), Fortified/Donda (25/50 gang-fortress caps), Fishers Champion (Discord fishing bot winner).

There are 150+ titles in total — cop, civilian, and TT each see their own set, and the in-game **Titles** app is the source of truth for exact requirements.

---

### How your name is decorated (tips)

- **Achievement prefixes** wrap around your name (e.g. "Warlord Steve", "The Prominent Steve") in a fixed color, and are always visible to others.
- **Titles** show as a separately colored tag using the color you picked.
- Other players only see your **title** if they have the **"Show Titles"** option enabled in their own Settings app — so if someone can't see your title, that's a setting on their end.
- You can wear an achievement prefix and a title at the same time; they're independent systems.
- Gotcha: if you lose the stat/achievement backing a name (e.g. a season war-rating reset drops you below a threshold), the game will automatically un-equip that name for you.

---

## Ben Bones & Looner (social)

*The island's social media: post to the **Looner** microblog, boost other people's loons, and text the paranoid conspiracy-radio host **Ben Bones** for AI-powered "advice." All of it lives on your phone, all of it is free.*

Everything here runs through your phone. Open it with the **Y key** (default). You cannot open your phone while your hands are restrained (handcuffed). No license, item, talent, rank, or money is ever required for any of the social features below.

There are three things Ben Bones touches: the **Looner** microblog, the **Ben Bones Official** text contact, and the **DataBattle News** website (his "newspaper"). Each is covered below.

---

### Looner (the microblog)

Looner is a live, server-wide microblog — think a tiny in-character version of Twitter/X. Everyone's posts show up in one shared feed, and every player sees the same feed update in real time.

**Where to find it**
1. Press **Y** to open your phone.
2. Open the **Browser** app (icon labeled "Browse the LoonyNet"). This is available to everyone.
3. You land on the **LoonyNet** home page. Get to Looner any of these ways:
   - Tap the **Looner** entry in the on-screen site directory (it's the first one, tagged *"what are you doing?"*).
   - Type `looner.com` into the address bar at the top and press **GO**.
   - Pick **Looner** from the page menu (the dropdown just under the address bar).

When you're on Looner, a **compose bar** appears pinned at the bottom of the screen with a gold **LOON IT** button.

**Posting a loon**
1. Tap the text field at the bottom and type your message.
2. A live character counter sits just above the box. You get **180 characters**. The number counts down as you type and turns **red** once you're inside the last 20 characters (past 160 typed). The box hard-stops you at 180.
3. Tap **LOON IT** (or it shows "Posting...").
4. Your post appears in the feed for everyone almost instantly, stamped with your in-game name, a fresh timestamp, and a post number (e.g. `#42`). New posts start at **0 boosts**.

Your name is shown exactly as your character name (trimmed to 28 characters). If for some reason you have no name, you post as **"Anonymous Loon."** Quote marks, backslashes, and angle-brackets are stripped from posts, and line breaks are flattened to spaces.

**Boosting (likes)**
- Under every post is a heart with a count and the word **boost**. Tap it to boost that loon.
- You can boost any post **once** — try again and you'll get *"Looner: you already liked that loon."*
- There's a short **2-second** cooldown between boosts.
- Alternative: instead of typing a message, type `like <number>` in the compose bar (for example `like 42`) and hit LOON IT to boost post #42.

**Reading the feed**
- The feed shows up to the **50 most recent posts**, oldest at the top, newest at the bottom.
- Each post shows: a profile picture, the poster's name, a **gold verified check** (if verified), how long ago it was posted (**now / 12m / 3h / 2d**), and the post number.
- If a post is a reply, it shows a small **`re: #N`** line pointing at the post it's answering.
- The page auto-refreshes in place whenever anyone posts or boosts while you're looking at it. To force a refresh, re-select **Looner** from the page menu.
- The tip line at the top reminds you: *"tap the heart under a loon to boost it."* When nobody has posted, the page reads *"Dead air. Be the first — type below and LOON IT."*

**Verified badges**
The gold check next to a name means that account is verified. Only three kinds of poster are verified: the **server owner**, the **current Governor**, and **Ben Bones**. Everyone else posts unverified. (The verified status is decided the moment you post.)

**Ben Bones ratios your posts**
Ben Bones has a bot on Looner that *dunks on players*. **Roughly 40% of the time you make a post**, Ben Bones immediately fires back a standalone verified rant that @-mentions you — accusing you of being a lizard, a globalist asset, a chemtrail, etc. His dunk lands with a huge boost count out of the gate (**200 to ~1,800 boosts** — the classic "ratio"), so it will usually sit above your original post. It's comedy; don't take it personally.

**Ben Bones' own posts**
When his broadcast is online, Ben Bones also posts fresh conspiracy rants on his own — reacting to things that are actually happening on the island (Fed Reserve alarms, blackouts, weapon airdrops, and so on) — roughly **every 25 minutes**, and he'll reply-dunk a recent player post roughly **every 12 minutes**. His posts show up verified with big boost counts (**150 to ~1,950**). If the island's been quiet, he'll just invent a paranoid conspiracy of the day. *(These auto-posts depend on his broadcast daemon being switched on; the built-in 40% ratio above always happens regardless.)*

**Looner limits at a glance**
| Thing | Value |
|---|---|
| Max post length | 180 characters |
| Post cooldown | 1 post per **20 seconds** (*"slow down — one post every 20 seconds"*) |
| Boost cooldown | 1 per **2 seconds** |
| Boosts per post | **1 per player, ever** |
| Feed size shown | up to **50 posts** |
| Ben Bones ratio chance | **~40%** per player post |

**Gotchas & tips**
- Everything you post is public and server-wide, tied to your character name. There's no anonymous posting and no deleting.
- Players can only **post** and **boost** — there's no button to reply to a specific person's loon. Threaded `re:` replies only come from the Ben Bones bot. If you want to "reply" to someone, just type `@TheirName ...` as a normal post.
- Posting is a good way to bait a funny Ben Bones ratio — but expect it to out-boost you.
- A "trending / most-boosted" sort exists behind the scenes but is **not currently reachable from the phone** — the feed you see is always newest-first.

---

### Texting Ben Bones Official (DM the AI)

You can privately text Ben Bones and get an in-character reply written by an AI. He's genuinely lore-aware and will actually try to help you — just filtered through full bunker-conspiracy paranoia.

**How to text him**
1. Press **Y** to open your phone.
2. Open the **Message** app (*"Send text messages to other players"*).
3. In the contact list, find **Ben Bones Official** — it's pinned at the list and shown in **gold**.
4. Select it, type your message (up to **200 characters**), and send.
5. You'll see: *"You texted Ben Bones Official. He's in the bunker — give him a minute to reply."*
6. His reply arrives a short while later as an **incoming text from "Ben Bones Official"** — a styled gold notification that also drops into your phone's message history so you can re-read it.

**What he knows / how he answers**
Ben Bones understands the island: cartels, the Federal Reserve / bank / jewelry / evidence / shipping / Loony Bin heists, chemtrails, server restarts (which he calls "The Great Reset"), gangs and turf, blackouts, rebel weapon airdrops, LoonyCoin mining, the casino, Red Gull, Super Male Vitality, Summon Vehicle, and the other LoonyNet sites. Ask him where to sell something, how a heist works, or just chat — he'll answer, always through the conspiracy lens. Replies are short (**1–3 sentences, up to ~280 characters**). He remembers the last few things you two said, so you can hold a short back-and-forth.

**DM limits & timing**
| Thing | Value |
|---|---|
| Max message length | **200 characters** |
| Text cooldown | 1 text per **15 seconds** (*"Ben Bones is on the other line (bunker signal is bad). Give it a moment."*) |
| Reply delivery | his answer is pushed to you within about **8 seconds** of it being written, as long as you're still online |
| Reply window | if you go offline, or more than **1 hour** passes, that reply is dropped |

**Gotchas & tips**
- Don't put any formatting/markup in your text — messages containing things like `<t`, `<img`, `<a`, or `<br/>` are rejected.
- Give him a moment — the reply isn't instant. If nothing comes back at all, his broadcast may be down (*"SIGNAL JAMMED, patriot. The globalists cut my line. Text me again."* is his fallback line). His live replies depend on the AI daemon being switched on.
- Quote marks and backslashes are stripped from what you send.
- His texts are saved in your **Message History** (button in the phone messaging screen), so you can go back and re-read the ranting wisdom.

---

### DataBattle News (Ben Bones' newspaper)

Ben Bones is also the voice behind **DataBattle**, the island's "last free press" — a browsable conspiracy news site. It's read-only (there's nothing to buy or click-to-earn), but it's part of the Ben Bones experience and it reacts to the live server.

**Where:** Phone → **Browser** app → tap **DataBattle News** on the LoonyNet home, or type `databattle.com`.

**What's there:**
- A rotating rack of headline articles in the left/page menu — *The Great Reset, CLANKR Is Watching, Stimmy Checks, Red Gull Exposed, Tiny Island Reset (the Malden move), Chemtrails Over Malden, Lizard Police, The Bug Invasion, Hospital Harvest, Follow The Tax,* and more. Each is a full Ben Bones rant tying a real game system (restarts, the anti-cheat, the Governor's tax, cartel payouts, etc.) to a paranoid theory.
- A **BREAKING** ticker at the top of the front page that pulls in live island events, so the "news" changes with what's actually going on.
- A running "SPONSORED" gag for **Super Male Vitality** (*"40% off with code WAKEUP"*) and a daily **ALERT LEVEL: CRIMSON** banner.

**Tip:** DataBattle, Looner, and Ben Bones' texts all share the same character. If you want the flavor of what he's ranting about on Looner or in your DMs, the DataBattle articles are the "source material." (The Browser also hosts other, non-Ben-Bones LoonyNet sites — LoonyBets betting, Loonstreets leaderboards — but those are separate systems with their own guides.)

---

## Governor & Politics

The Governor is an elected civilian who rules Altis for a 7-day term: they set the island's sales tax, decide whether weed and SPAR-16 rifles are legal, collect tax into a Government treasury they can spend, pardon a wanted criminal, and (when they enable the death penalty) send high-bounty prisoners to the electric chair. Any civilian can run for the seat, and every player gets one vote per election.

### Where everything happens

- **Governor Building** — marked on your map with a white flag icon labelled "Governor Building," in central Altis (Pyrgos area). This is the seat of power: the sitting Governor runs the law menu, the treasury bank, and the door locks from a laptop here.
- **"Run for Office" NPC** — stands at the Governor Building only. Scroll-wheel on them to file your candidacy.
- **"Vote for Governor" NPCs** — stand at the Governor Building and at voting booths in several major towns (Pyrgos, Kavala, and Sofia among others). Scroll-wheel on any of them to cast your vote. (verify: exact town list)
- **Wanted List** — opened from your player inventory (Y-menu); the "Pardon" button appears here for the Governor.

### Running for Governor (candidacy)

Any civilian can campaign. Walk up to the **"Run for Office"** NPC at the Governor Building and scroll-wheel **Run for Office**.

Requirements and steps:
1. You must **not** already be the sitting Governor and **not** already be on the ballot for the current election.
2. Write a **campaign policy** — a short manifesto voters will read. It must be **at least 15 characters and no more than 150 characters**.
3. Enter a **campaign investment**. The minimum is **$75,000**, and you must have that much in your **bank** to pay it. You can invest more if you want (it just shows a bigger number to voters).
4. Accept the warning prompt, then submit. Your investment is deducted from your bank immediately.
5. The server announces your candidacy and policy to everyone, and your name appears on every voting booth's ballot.

**Important:** the campaign investment is a one-way cost — it is **never refunded and never paid back out**, even if you lose or the term ends. It is purely a barrier to entry and a bragging number on the ballot. You can only file **one candidacy per election**, and there is a few-second cooldown between attempts.

### Voting for Governor

Scroll-wheel **Vote for Governor** on any voting NPC. Select a candidate from the list — each row shows the candidate's **name, vote count, investment, and policy** — then confirm with **Yes**.

- You get **one vote per election**, full stop. Once you've voted you cannot change it or vote again until the next election.
- There is a few-second spam cooldown between attempts.
- If a candidate you're looking at has already been dropped from the ballot, your vote won't go through — pick someone still listed.

### How terms and elections resolve

- A Governor's **term lasts 7 days**. At the end of the term the **candidate with the most votes** is seated for the next 7-day term.
- **If nobody ran that term, the sitting Governor keeps the seat** for another 7 days automatically.
- The very first Governor of a cycle is normally **seated by the server owner** to kick things off. However, if the seat is **empty** and players start filing candidacies, an **open election runs for 7 days from the first candidacy** and then automatically seats the top-voted player as the first Governor — so a vacant seat can fill itself even without an owner appointment.
- Votes and candidacies **persist through server restarts**, so a campaign in progress isn't wiped by a reboot.

**Fair-play rule (staff-enforced):** you — or any member of your gang — **cannot hold office more than once in any 30-day period.** Trying to dodge this (e.g. leaving your gang to re-run) is treated as exploiting and can get you punished. This feature is meant to rotate through the whole server, not be cornered by one group.

### Governor powers: setting the laws

As the sitting Governor, go to the Governor Building, aim at the **laptop** there (stand within ~5 m and within 15 m of the building), and scroll-wheel **Governor Laws**. This opens a law panel with three controls:

- **Tax Rate slider (0–15%)** — sets the island-wide sales tax surcharge (see "How taxes hit you" below). Your slice of this tax funds your treasury.
- **Weed Legal (ON/OFF)** — when ON, cannabis, marijuana, processed marijuana, and all weed paraphernalia (bongs, rigs, vapes, joints, papers, pre-rolls) become **legal**: police can no longer seize them from you, your vehicle, or your house, and the marijuana fields/dispensary turn **green** on the map. When OFF, all of that is illegal contraband again and shows **red**.
- **SPAR-16s Legal (ON/OFF)** — when ON, the **SPAR-16 rifle** is legal to buy and carry (sold at Billy Joe's Firearms; see below). When OFF, it's flagged **(ILLEGAL)** and counts as contraband.

Submitting changes broadcasts the new laws to the whole server (with a loudspeaker announcement at the Governor Building) and plays a "Government Laws Changed" notification listing the new tax rate and legality of weed and SPAR-16s.

**Law-change cooldown:** once you submit a law change you must wait **35 minutes** before you can change the laws again. The panel tells you how many minutes remain if you're too early.

> **Heads-up on the death penalty:** in the current build, the **SPAR-16s Legal toggle also acts as the death-penalty switch** — turning SPAR-16s legal is what arms the electric chair (see "The electric chair" below). If you don't want executions happening, leave that toggle OFF. (verify: this coupling is by-design vs. a quirk)

### The treasury (Governor Bank)

Your sales-tax income is collected into a **"Government" treasury** — a shared government bank account. Every taxed purchase on the island deposits **your flat tax slice** (only your 0–15% portion, not the wealth surcharge) into this treasury. Each single deposit is capped at **$5,000,000**, and executions add **$5,000** each.

To spend it, aim at the office laptop and scroll-wheel **Governor Bank**. The panel shows the treasury balance and your personal cash. To take money out:

- **Minimum withdrawal: $500.** You can't pull less.
- **Maximum withdrawal: $9,999,999** in a single transaction.
- You can't withdraw more than the treasury currently holds.
- Withdrawals have a short **anti-exploit processing delay** and a few-second cooldown between them — wait for "Processing Txn..." to finish before withdrawing again.

There's also an **ATM** action on the same laptop for your personal banking.

### Pardons

The Governor can wipe a wanted player's entire bounty and warrants. Open your **Wanted List** (from your inventory), select the wanted player, and click **Pardon** (then confirm).

- You must be a **civilian** Governor (a cop-side player can't use the Governor pardon).
- The target must be **online**. They receive a **Yes/No prompt** and must **accept** the pardon.
- If they accept: their wanted level is cleared, any bounty-hunter contracts on them are cancelled, and it's announced server-wide. You then **cannot pardon anyone else until the next server restart** — one successful pardon per world session.
- If they **decline**, no pardon happens and you're free to try again on someone else.
- You **cannot pardon yourself** ("Corruption only goes so far!").

### The electric chair (death penalty)

If you enable the death penalty (the SPAR-16/death-penalty law toggle), prisoners being released from jail can be **executed by electric chair** instead of walking free. This triggers automatically on release when **all** of these are true:

- The prisoner's **bounty is at least $100,000**.
- The Governor's **death-penalty law is currently ON**.
- There are **at least 5 minutes left** before the next scheduled server restart.

When it fires, the convict is strapped into the electric chair at the prison, put in a prison uniform, and — after about a 30-second warning and a dramatic execution sequence — **killed and then respawned**. Each execution deposits **$5,000** into the Government treasury. There is nothing the prisoner can do to stop it; it replaces their normal release.

### Governor Building door locks

As Governor you can seal your office. Aim at the **Governor Building** itself (within ~15 m) and scroll-wheel **Toggle Governor Building Locks** to lock or unlock all its doors (with a lock/unlock sound). Use it again to reverse.

### How taxes hit you (every player)

Whenever you buy from a shop — vehicles, weapons, clothing, houses, loadouts, and general/virtual/black-market shops — a **sales tax is added on top of the base price**. That rate is the sum of two parts:

1. **The Governor's tax:** 0–15%, whatever the sitting Governor set (0% if the seat is empty). This part funds the treasury.
2. **A wealth surcharge** based on **your bank balance** (a money sink — this part is destroyed, not banked by anyone):

| Your bank balance | Wealth surcharge |
|---|---|
| Up to $750,000 | +0% |
| Over $750,000 | +2% |
| Over $1,250,000 | +4% |
| Over $2,500,000 | +8% |
| Over $5,000,000 | +10% |
| Over $7,500,000 | +12% |
| Over $10,000,000 | +15% |
| Over $20,000,000 | +20% |

You can see your current combined rate any time in the **Money menu**, shown as **"Governor Tax Rate: X% (+Y%)"** — X is the Governor's tax, Y is your personal wealth surcharge. So a rich player under a high-tax Governor can pay up to ~35% extra on purchases. Keeping less money in the bank lowers the wealth portion.

### Tips & gotchas

- **The office actions only appear if you ARE the Governor** and you're standing at the Governor Building aiming at the laptop. If "Governor Laws"/"Governor Bank" don't show up, you're either not the seated Governor, too far from the marker, or not looking at the laptop.
- **Your $75k+ campaign investment is gone for good** whether you win or lose — treat it as the entry fee, not a deposit.
- **You only get one vote and one candidacy per election** — spend them wisely; they don't reset until the next term.
- **Law changes are on a 35-minute clock** — plan your tax/legality tweaks so you're not locked out mid-crisis.
- **Governor pardon is once per restart and needs the target to accept** — coordinate with the person you're pardoning so they say Yes.
- **Legalizing weed** instantly makes all weed products safe from police seizure server-wide (great for weed gangs); turning it off makes carrying them a crime again.
- **The SPAR-16 rifle** is sold at **Billy Joe's Firearms** for **$7,500** (needs a **Firearms License**) and shows a red "(ILLEGAL)" tag whenever the Governor has banned it — and remember that same toggle currently arms the electric chair.
- **Don't game the 30-day office rule via gang-hopping** — staff watch for it and it's a bannable exploit.

## Quests & Seasonal Events

LooneyBin has a full quest system (short "restart" challenges and permanent "lifetime" grinds) plus limited-time holiday events that drop money, loot and gifts. Everything below is claimed passively as you play — you almost never have to hand anything in.

### Opening the Quest Menu

Open your phone (default **Y** key) and tap the **Quests** app. It is available to everyone (civ, cop, TT). The menu has a dropdown filter with two lists:

- **Restart Cycle Quests** – short quests that reset every server restart. You may only have **one active at a time**.
- **Lifetime Quests** – long-haul goals that track permanently across all your sessions. These are **always active** — you never accept them.

**Reading the list (colour code):**
- **Green** = already completed.
- **Red** = your currently active restart quest.
- **Cyan/blue** = a lifetime quest.

Click a quest to see its objective, reward and your live progress (e.g. "Completed: 3" or "No Progress") on the right. Double-click a quest to **bookmark** it so the list re-opens on it. When a quest finishes you get an on-screen banner, a chime, and the reward is handed over automatically.

**Accepting / cancelling a restart quest:** with a quest selected and no quest active, the button reads **Accept Quest** — click it and confirm. While a quest is active the button becomes **Cancel Active Quest**; cancelling wipes that quest's progress but lets you pick a different one. Lifetime quests show **Already Active** (you can't accept or cancel them).

### Restart Cycle Quests

These reset at every server restart. Accept one, complete the objective, and it auto-completes for a cash/item reward plus **+75 faction XP** and a completion chime.

- **Civilians** are offered a **rotating random selection (up to 8)** of the civilian restart quests each restart, plus the Completionist quest. (The exact rotation is picked server-side — *verify count*.)
- **Police and Trauma Team** see their full restart-quest list.
- Cash, New Drachma and Honor rewards are paid straight to your account. **Item rewards spawn in a personal Quest Chest** on the ground next to you (see "Quest Chests" below).

Lifetime quests are the endgame grind. They **track automatically and permanently** — you don't accept them, progress never resets between sessions, and each one can only be completed once. ### Quest Chests (how item rewards are delivered)

When a quest rewards **items** (weapons, drugs, gear), they don't drop straight into your inventory — a **personal loot chest spawns on the ground next to you**:

- It's marked on your map with an **orange dot labelled "Quest Chest"** and a waypoint line is drawn to it.
- **Only you can see and loot it** — nobody can steal it.
- It **persists if you disconnect** and re-appears next login, and stays until you empty it completely. Cash, New Drachma and Honor rewards, by contrast, are credited instantly.
- Tip: the pop-up urges you to "act fast," but the chest won't vanish on a timer — just make sure you clear it before the next server restart.

### Anti-cheese cooldowns (important gotcha)

Many quests (especially lifetime ones and repeatable cop/TT tasks) have a hidden **per-quest cooldown**. If you repeat the same qualifying action too quickly, you'll see *"Your previous action did not count towards quest completion due to being too recent to your last action."* Space your actions out — spamming won't farm progress faster. (Typical cop/medic restart quests have 60–300 second cooldowns; a few lifetime quests have cooldowns as long as 300 seconds.)

### Seasonal Events

Holiday events are **switched on by staff for the season** and only run while active. When one is live you'll get an announcement.

#### Easter Egg Hunt

When the Easter event is running, **bunnies spawn around Athira**. Walk up to one and use the scroll-wheel action **"Pickup easter bunny."**

- **Requirements:** you must be a **civilian**, within ~2 m of the bunny, and carrying a **weight of 10 or less** (drop your gear/inventory first or you'll be "too heavy").
- **Reward:** a random cash prize of **$5,000–$10,000** (in $1,000 steps), paid straight to you.
- **First come, first served:** each bunny can only be claimed by one player, then it disappears.

#### Christmas Tree Presents

While the winter event is active you can claim **one free present per day** from the Christmas tree. Walk up to the present box by the tree and use the scroll action **"Open present"** (you must be within ~3 m). Confirm the pop-up to open it.

- **One per day** — after opening, it resets and you can grab the next one **tomorrow**. There's also a 15-second anti-spam lock right after opening.
- If your inventory is full the loot **drops at your feet**, so make room first.
- **The reward depends on the calendar day (UTC)** — it's an advent-style calendar running Dec 19–31:

| Day | Reward |
|---|---|
| Dec 19 | $20,000 |
| Dec 20 | 1 gold nugget |
| Dec 21 | 1 Speed Bomb |
| Dec 22 | 2 OGL harness vests |
| Dec 23 | 4 Mini Grenades |
| Dec 24 | 5 flawless diamonds |
| Dec 25 | 1 Mk200 LMG (Arco optic + flashlight) + 3× 200-round boxes |
| Dec 26 | 1 Tractor |
| Dec 27 | 4 Christmas Gifts |
| Dec 28 | $15,000 |
| Dec 29 | 20 coke bricks |
| Dec 30 | 3 Zafir LMGs (Arco optic + flashlight) + 5× 150-round boxes |
| Dec 31 | 1 Armed Offroad |

(The exact tree location on the map isn't specified in the code — *verify* — but you'll be reminded "go to the Christmas tree to open your gift" when a present is waiting.)

#### Gift Items (Admin, Christmas & Halloween Gifts)

**Gifts are usable virtual items** that pop one random reward when opened. You receive them from staff, event rewards, the login bonus and certain quests (e.g. the Christmas tree's Day 27 hands out Christmas Gifts, and several lifetime quests reward Admin Gifts). To open one, **use it from your inventory** — you must be a **civilian with weight 10 or less**.

- **Admin Gift** – the "starter" tier. Possible drops: apple, beer, diamonds (flawless/cut), cocaine, coke bricks, marijuana, doubloons, dog/goat/man flesh, meth, blue meth, rubber, Red Gull, döner kebab, a gold bar, a gold nugget, a treasure map, treasure, aged whiskey, or cash of **$5,000 / $7,500 / $10,000**.
- **Christmas Gift** – richer pool: same item spread plus larger stacks, and cash up to **$40,000** (in $5,000 steps from $5,000). Occasionally gives worthless "coal" (a dud).
- **Halloween Gift** – same idea as the Christmas gift; cash up to **$40,000**, occasionally a worthless "candy" dud. (Where Halloween gifts are handed out — *verify*, typically staff/Halloween events.)

Gotcha: opening any gift can give **illegal items** (drugs, organs, weapons), so don't open them in front of a cop. If you roll the "coal"/"candy" dud you get nothing — better luck next time.

### Daily Login Bonus (related daily reward)

Not a quest, but the closest cousin: log in every day for a **consecutive-day streak reward**. When one is waiting you'll be told *"You have a login bonus available… Type !loginbonus to retrieve it."* Type **`!loginbonus`** in chat and confirm to open the crate.

- **Requirements:** you must be a **civilian, outside any vehicle, not in prison**, with a bonus available.
- **One claim per day**, and the reward escalates the longer your unbroken streak runs (Day 1 starts at **$1,000**; later days give drugs, weapons, gold, New Drachma, even vehicles, up to a top tier around day 100 of three Zafir LMGs). **Missing a day resets your streak.** If a vehicle reward can't fit (garage full) you're paid its sell value in cash instead.

## Mining Rigs / LoonyCoin

Passive crypto-mining side hustle: collect eight PC hardware parts that drop at federal heists, build a **Mining Rig**, plug it into a house you own, and it quietly mines **LoonyCoin** around the clock — even while you're logged off. Come back, collect the coins, and cash them out at the Blackmarket.

There is **no license, no talent, no rank, and no purchase cost** to get into mining. The only thing you must own is a **house** (or shed) to install the rig in. The parts are free — you just have to fight for them.

### The short version (start here)

1. A federal heist starts → a **hardware case** drops at the scene → scroll **"Grab Component"** to loot one random PC part.
2. Repeat until you have **all 8 parts**, then scroll **"Assemble Mining Rig"** to build the rig.
3. Walk up to a **house you own** and scroll **"Install Mining Rig"**.
4. It mines **2 LoonyCoin per hour**, 24/7. Come back and scroll **"Collect LoonyCoin"**.
5. Take your LoonyCoin to the **Blackmarket** and sell it for **$6,000 each**.

### Step 1 — Collecting the 8 crypto components

Whenever a **federal event begins**, a courier drops a metal hardware case at the crime scene, marked with **blue smoke**. Everyone on the server gets the announcement:

> "A courier dropped a hardware case at the scene of the crime — looks like PC parts."

The events that spawn a component crate are:

- **Federal Reserve robbery**
- **Bank robbery**
- **Evidence Lockup raid**
- **Loony Bin breach**
- **Jewelry Emporium robbery**

Prison Breaks and Prisoner Transport convoys do **not** drop a crate.

To loot it, get within about **5 metres** of the case, look at it, and scroll **"Grab Component"**. You receive **one random part** and the case vanishes.

**Key facts about the drops:**

- Each crate gives exactly **one component**, and it's **random** — you may get duplicates before you complete the set.
- **One grab per crate** — first person to scroll it gets the part, so it's a race.
- The drop is **announced server-wide to everyone, including cops**, and the case appears right at an active heist. Expect a firefight over it.
- The case **despawns after 10 minutes (600 seconds)** if nobody grabs it.
- Because parts only drop at federal events, completing a full set of 8 takes multiple heists.

**The 8 parts you need** (in-game item names):

| # | Item name |
|---|-----------|
| 1 | Ryzen 5950X (CPU) |
| 2 | RTX 3090 Ti (GPU) |
| 3 | 3000W PSU |
| 4 | 10TB M.2 SSD (storage) |
| 5 | ASUS Dark Hero Mobo (motherboard) |
| 6 | 64GB DDR4 Ram |
| 7 | NZXT Water Cooler |
| 8 | Console Frame (case) |

Each part weighs **3** inventory space, except the **Console Frame**, which weighs **5**. (Note: the "Gaming LoonyBin Console" item is **not** one of the eight and is not needed for the rig.)

### Step 2 — Assembling the Mining Rig

Once you have **all eight components in your virtual inventory at the same time**, a self scroll-wheel action **"Assemble Mining Rig"** appears (you don't need to be near anything).

- It consumes **one of each** of the 8 parts and gives you **one "LoonyBin Mining Rig."**
- It's all-or-nothing — if you're missing even one part, it won't build (it'll tell you how many parts are missing).
- The assembled rig weighs **15** inventory space.

### Step 3 — Installing the rig in your house

Stand within about **6 metres** of a **house or shed you own**, look at it, and scroll **"Install Mining Rig."**

Requirements to install:

- You must be the **house owner** — a house key is **not** enough to install (keys only let you *collect*, see below).
- The house must **not already have a rig** — **one rig per house**.
- The building must be a properly registered/owned house.

Installing **consumes the rig**. From that moment it mines LoonyCoin **24/7, around the clock, whether you're online or not.** Your rig and its progress are **saved across server restarts**, so a reboot won't wipe it.

> The install message notes it's "loud enough that a nosy cop might notice." As far as the code shows, this is flavour text — there is no actual cop tracker or detection tied to an installed rig.

There is no in-game action to **remove or move** a rig once it's installed.

### Step 4 — Mining rate, cap, and collecting

- **Rate:** **2 LoonyCoin per hour.**
- **Storage cap:** **96 LoonyCoin.** That's **48 hours** of mining. Once the rig hits 96 stored coins it stops accruing more, so **collect at least every 2 days** or you're leaving money on the table.
- Mining runs continuously in real time — offline time counts.

To collect, walk up to the house with the rig (within about **6 metres**), look at it, and scroll **"Collect LoonyCoin."**

- You may collect if you're the **owner OR you hold a key** to that house. (So co-owners/keyholders can empty the rig, but only the owner could install it.)
- You get the accrued coins (rounded down), and the timer resets.
- If **less than 1 coin** has built up (i.e. under ~30 minutes since the last collection), you'll get: "The rig is still warming up — nothing to collect yet."
- Each LoonyCoin weighs **1** inventory space, so a full 96-coin haul is **96 weight** to carry to market.

### Step 5 — Selling LoonyCoin

LoonyCoin is **sell-only at the Blackmarket** for **$6,000 each**. You cannot buy it anywhere.

- A **full 96-coin haul sells for $576,000** (96 × $6,000).
- The price is **flat** — LoonyCoin is not part of the dynamic drug market, so it doesn't fluctuate. (A server-wide "bust bonus" event could raise all Blackmarket sells by 12%, but that event is not currently active.)

**Where the Blackmarket is:** look for the shady Blackmarket dealer — a lone civilian NPC. There are **two** on the island, one in the **west** (around map coordinates 5390, 17900) and one in **central Altis** (around 20740, 15680). (verify exact towns)

### Gotchas & tips

- **Duplicates are normal.** Parts drop randomly, so you'll pile up spares of some parts while waiting on others. You only need one of each of the 8 to build.
- **Getting the parts is the hard part, not the mining.** Component crates only spawn at federal heists, they're announced to the whole server, and they sit in the middle of an active robbery. Grabbing one is a PvP moment.
- **One grab per case, 10-minute despawn** — don't dawdle, and don't expect a second chance from the same crate.
- **Collect within 48 hours.** The rig caps at 96 coins; anything past 48 hours of idle mining is wasted.
- **Ownership vs. keys:** only the **owner** installs a rig; **anyone with a key** to that house can collect from it. Choose who holds keys accordingly.
- **One rig per house.** If you're pumping out multiple rigs, you'll need multiple owned houses to run them.
- **Rigs survive restarts** — they're persisted, and the server restores every installed rig shortly after boot, so your passive income keeps ticking through reboots.
- **Full economics:** at 2 coins/hr × $6,000, an installed rig earns roughly **$12,000/hour** of passive income, up to the 96-coin ($576,000) ceiling per collection cycle.

## World Events (Blackout, Admin Events, Stimmy)

World Events are server-wide happenings that an admin (or the economy) triggers on top of normal Altis Life. They come in three flavors:

- **Opt-in competitive events** you actively *join* — **Gun Game**, **Battle Royale**, and the **Graveyard Shift** (Halloween). You press a key to enter, get a preset loadout, and fight for a cash prize.
- **Ambient zone events** that apply to *everyone* who happens to be in the affected area — **The Purge**, **Bug Invasion**, **Blackout**, **Chemtrails**, and **Lizard Police**. No sign-up; you're in it if you're there.
- **Stimmy Checks** — an economy stimulus toggle that quietly multiplies certain cartel payouts (explained at the bottom).

Only **one** admin event can run at a time, and admin events won't start while a seasonal event is running (or vice-versa).

### How events are announced

When an event fires you'll usually see all of these at once:

- A **yellow server broadcast** across the top of your screen (e.g. "ADMIN EVENT: GUN GAME at Molos! Civilians -- press your event key to join!").
- A headline on the **databattle.com news page** in your phone's browser — the in-world conspiracy anchor **Ben Bones** spins every event into a story.
- A verified **@BenBones post on Looner** (the in-game social feed) for the bigger events.
- A **map marker** for any event that has a physical zone (Purge red zone, Bug Invasion brown circle, etc.).

If you tab in late (join mid-event), zoned events still apply to you the moment the server sees you inside them.

### Joining an admin event (the "9" key)

For the three opt-in events (**Gun Game**, **Battle Royale**, **Graveyard Shift**), you join by pressing the **9 key** on the number row while the join window is open.

Requirements to join:
- You must be a **civilian** (cops and medics can't join these three).
- You must **not** be downed, restrained, or under arrest.
- You can't join once the join window has **closed** ("in progress").

What happens when you join:
- Your **real loadout, clothing skins, and position are saved**, then you're teleported into the arena with an event loadout.
- When the event ends you're **returned to your exact pre-event spot with your original gear** and skins.
- If you're **dead when the event ends**, you get a **45-second warning to respawn** — respawn within that window to keep your saved position and loadout.

No license, item, talent, or fee is required — joining is free.

### Gun Game

**What it is:** A free-for-all where every kill upgrades your weapon; the first player to get a kill with all 15 weapons wins.

- **Where:** An admin-chosen location (any of ~17 spots — Molos, Galati, the Factory, Power Plant, Kavala Castle, the racetrack, etc.). The broadcast names it.
- **How to enter:** Press **9** during the **3-minute** join window.
- **Loadout:** You start with a preset weapon and are auto-refilled between kills. Your **defib, bloodbag, cocaine, and Red Gull are removed** for the event.
- **How to win:** Get **one kill with each of the 15 weapons**. Every kill bumps you to the next tier and announces "Advanced to the next tier! X/15." The ladder runs from heavy machine guns, through assault and marksman rifles and an SMG, down to pistols, ending on a **heavy revolver** — landing the **15th-tier kill** wins.
- **Prize:** **$250,000** paid straight to your **bank (ATM)**.
- **Rules/limits:**
  - Joins close **3 minutes** after start. If nobody wins, the event auto-ends after a **30-minute** cap with no winner.
  - **Leaving the play area (more than 300 m from the center) kills you** — a "You stepped out of the play area!" warning fires.
  - It's night-aware: you're auto-given NVGs if it's dark.
- **Gotcha:** Dying resets your on-hand pocket cash as usual, but your **saved gear and bank are safe**; you respawn back into the arena on your current tier.

### Battle Royale

**What it is:** Last-player-standing in a shrinking circle, complete with a HALO parachute drop.

- **Where:** An admin-chosen location.
- **How to enter:** Press **9** during the **3-minute** join window.
- **Loadout:** A black MX rifle with an Arco optic and spare mags, a parachute, plus **2 First Aid Kits**. Your defib/bloodbag/cocaine/Red Gull are removed.
- **The drop:** When joins close you're launched to **~600 m altitude** and free-fall; your chute **auto-deploys around 100 m**. Steer toward the center.
- **How to win:** **Be the last one alive.** The winner gets **$250,000** to their **bank (ATM)**.
- **The zone:** A blue "Battle Royale Zone" circle **starts at 400 m radius** and, once joins close, **shrinks to 35 m over about 18 minutes**. **Outside the circle you take steady bleed damage** (with a red screen and blood effects) until you get back in.
- **Rules/limits:**
  - Joins close **3 minutes** after start.
  - If **fewer than 2** fighters joined, the match is **cancelled**.
  - Hard cap of roughly **27 minutes** of fighting after joins close; if it times out there's no single winner.
- **Tip:** You have a GPS in this loadout — use it plus the shrinking marker to always know where the safe circle is.

### Graveyard Shift (Halloween event)

**What it is:** A seasonal Halloween dig — unearth graves, gather bones, and burn them at campfires for loot, racing to find the bones of Death himself.

- **Where:** The **Halloween Graveyard** (central Altis, near the coordinates the event marker shows). You must stay inside the **~300 m** event area or you're removed.
- **How to enter:** Press **9** during the event. This is an **open, come-and-go dig** — there's no join-lock phase.
- **Loadout:** A farmer outfit and a **shovel** attached to your hand. Campfires, a church, and grave sites are spawned around you.
- **Step by step:**
  1. Walk up to a **grave (crater) site** and use the scroll action **"Unearth Remains"** (green). Stay within 7 m or the dig fails. About **1 in 6 graves is empty** — move to another.
  2. On a successful dig, skeletal remains appear. Use **"Gather Skeletal Remains"** (purple) to pick them up. You can only carry **one body's bones at a time**.
  3. Take the bones to a **lit campfire** and use **"Burn Skeletal Remains"** (red). Stay within 7 m while it burns.
- **Rewards:**
  - **Every ordinary burn** grants **one random valuable item** — e.g. beer, cut/flawless diamonds, cocaine, marijuana, doubloons, dog/goat product, meth, rubber, Red Gull, döner kebab, a speed bomb, man-flesh, a treasure map, treasure, aged whiskey, or **$1,500 / $2,500 cash**.
  - **Winning the event** (see below) drops a jackpot: roughly **3–12 coke bricks, 4–12 blue meth, 1 Halloween gift, $5,000–$15,000 cash, 1 speed bomb, and 4–8 aged scotch**.
- **How to win:** Either **burn 16+ bodies**, or — with a **1-in-80 chance each time you gather bones** — find **"Death himself's" bones**, which auto-qualify you. Burning the winning bones triggers the jackpot loot and ends the event with you as winner.
- **Limits:** The round lasts **20 minutes**. Leaving the graveyard area removes you.
- **Note:** This is a **seasonal/Halloween** event that admins run occasionally, not an always-on system.

### The Purge

**What it is:** A lawless red zone where all crime — up to and including murder — is temporarily legal.

- **Where:** A **town square** the admin picks — **Kavala, Athira, Pyrgos, or Sofia**. A pulsing **red "THE PURGE" circle** and a warning dot appear on your map.
- **How to take part:** **Nothing to join** — the zone applies to everyone inside it automatically. Watch/listen for the government announcement and sirens at the start.
- **Zone:** **450 m radius**, lasts **20 minutes**.
- **What changes inside the zone:**
  - **All crimes are legal** — committing them inside the red zone **adds no wanted charges**.
  - **RDM (random deathmatch) rules do NOT apply** inside the zone — you can kill freely.
  - You get an on-screen warning when you **enter** ("YOU'VE ENTERED THE PURGE ZONE") and when you **leave** ("normal laws apply again").
- **Gotcha:** The instant you step **outside** the red circle, normal law and RDM rules snap back on — don't chase a target past the boundary and get yourself wanted.

### Bug Invasion

**What it is:** Ben Bones' "government bugs disguised as rabbits" swarm a town — shoot them for cash.

- **Where:** A **town square** (Kavala, Athira, Pyrgos, or Sofia). A **brown "BUG INVASION" circle (~300 m)** marks it on the map.
- **How to take part:** **No sign-up** — just show up and start shooting the rabbits inside the zone.
- **Reward:** **$3,000 per bug**, paid to your **bank (ATM)**. If the shot's attribution is unclear, the nearest shooter within 60 m gets credited.
- **Numbers:** Exactly **100 bugs** spawn (staggered in, no refills). If one player somehow cleared them all, that's a theoretical **$300,000** — realistically it's a scramble split among everyone shooting.
- **Duration:** Runs about **10 minutes** or until **every bug is dead**, whichever comes first, then the survivors are cleaned up.
- **Tip:** They **scatter under fire**, so they're harder to hit than they look — bring an automatic weapon and get there fast before others thin the swarm.

### Blackout

**What it is:** "The Governor missed the power bill" — a city goes dark.

- **Where:** A **town square** (Kavala, Athira, Pyrgos, or Sofia). Every **streetlamp and power line within 900 m** of the square switches **off**.
- **How to take part:** Nothing to do — it's pure atmosphere and a crime enabler. There's **no reward and no damage**.
- **Duration:** **10 minutes**, then the grid "recovers" and the lights come back automatically.
- **Tip:** It's explicitly "crime o'clock" — the darkness is cover for robberies, drug runs, and getaways. Bring **NVGs or a flashlight**; everyone else is blind too.

### Chemtrails

**What it is:** A spray-plane "chemtrail" cloud settles over a town; breathing it does something random.

- **Where:** A **town square** (Kavala, Athira, Pyrgos, or Sofia), marked with a **green "CHEMTRAILS" circle (~350 m)**. A jet does three low passes first — "LOOK UP" broadcast.
- **How to take part:** Just be inside the green zone. Roughly every **20 seconds** you're inside, you roll a random effect:
  - **~40%:** a **"Super Male Vitality" buff** — you move about **12% faster** for **60 seconds**.
  - **~35%:** a **paranoia haze** — a chromatic-aberration screen distortion for ~15 seconds.
  - **~25%:** nothing (…or does it?).
- **Duration:** **5 minutes**. Cosmetic/flavor only — no cash, no lasting harm.

### Lizard Police

**What it is:** "The badges are shedding" — every cop turns reptilian.

- **Where:** Server-wide (all police).
- **What players see:** For **8 minutes**, on-duty cops get a **green reptile tint** and a "LIZARD MODE" toast; civilians get the Ben Bones broadcast and news headline.
- **How to take part:** Nothing to do — it's purely cosmetic lore/chaos. No mechanics change, no reward.

### Stimmy Checks

**What it is:** An economy-stimulus mode that multiplies cartel payouts to re-float the economy when the server is low-pop. **You cannot trigger it — it's an owner-only toggle** — but its effect is player-facing, so it's worth knowing:

- **When Stimmy is ON**, the **gang that owns a cartel earns 10× the normal cut** into its **gang bank** for the **Arms, Drug, and Wongs** cartels. (The **Weed/other** cartels are unaffected.)
- **Your personal tax cut is unchanged** — Stimmy only inflates the **gang-bank** credit, not the cash that lands in your pocket.
- So if you're in a gang holding the Arms, Drug, or Wongs cartel and the bank suddenly climbs **10× faster than usual**, Stimmy Checks are on. It's a deliberate economy boost, not a bug.

*(verify: exact real-world day/time an admin schedules any of these events — the code has no fixed schedule; they're launched manually. The Graveyard Shift's on-screen text still reads "LoonyBin 2020 Halloween," so treat it as a legacy seasonal event.)*
