## Using drugs — highs, overdose & addiction

Cooking, growing and selling narcotics is one thing — actually *taking* them is a whole separate mechanic most players never touch. Every hard drug you can sell, you can also consume for a real gameplay buff. The catch: drugs build a hidden **intoxication meter** that can make you **overdose and die**, and repeated use builds a **per-drug addiction** that gives you the shakes until you cough up cash for **rehab**. This section covers using drugs (and booze) for effect — not making or selling them.

### What it is (the three hidden meters)

There are three separate things running in the background. None of them are shown on your normal HUD, so you have to track them in your head:

- **Intoxication ("drug level")** — a 0–100% meter that climbs every time you take a hard drug and slowly drains over time. This is the one that can **kill you** via overdose. Alcohol does **not** feed this meter.
- **Addiction** — five separate addiction bars, one group per drug family. Each hit nudges that drug's bar up. Let it get high and you go into **withdrawal** (screen shakes, "you need a fix"). Addiction is what **rehab** clears, and it **persists through relogs**.
- **BAC (alcohol level)** — a separate booze meter with its own blurry-screen effect. Doesn't overdose you, but wrecks your aim and shows up when a cop searches you.

### Where & how to take them

Everything is used the **same way**: open your inventory (the **Y menu / phone inventory**), click the item, and hit **Use**. No NPC, no shop, no scroll wheel on the ground — it all happens from your item list.

- **Hard drugs** (cocaine, heroin, meth, crank, LSD, PCP) are used **instantly** from the inventory — one click, effect applies, one unit consumed.
- **Weed is different** — you can't smoke raw flower. You have to turn it into a smokeable first (roll a joint, pack a bong, load a dab rig, or use a vape). See the cannabis section.
- **Booze** (beer, moonshine, scotch, rum) is also used straight from the inventory.

Requirements to *consume*: just having the item in your inventory. There's **no license, talent, or rank gate** on using drugs — the licenses/gear gate the *making* and *selling*, not the swallowing. (Prices for buying the items live in the making/selling section.)

### Cannabis (weed) — the aim buff

Weed is the safest, most useful drug to actually use in a fight because it **reduces your weapon sway** and **kills pain effects** — with almost zero overdose risk.

You can't smoke raw `marijuana`/`marijuanam` — try to Use it and you'll be told to **roll a joint or pack a bowl** first. Your options:

- **Roll a joint** — Use **RAW Papers** (`canna_papers`), pick the flower to roll with. Smoking a pre-rolled joint takes **12 seconds**.
- **Vape** (`canna_vape`) — **8 seconds**. Warning: the vape is basically a **gag/downside** — it slows your animations for ~5s ("who put nicotine in my dab pen?!") and gives you **no sway buff**. Skip it if you want the actual benefit.
- **Glass Bong** (`canna_bong`) + flower — **12 seconds**.
- **Glass Dab Rig** (`canna_rig`) + **Golden Wax** (`canna_dab`) — **12 seconds**, and the strongest.

**What smoking does:**
- **Removes pain** (clears the injured screen effect).
- **Reduces weapon sway by 12%** (aim tightens up) for a set time:
  - **Joint → 1 minute**
  - **Bong → 3 minutes**
  - **Dab rig → 6 minutes** (also briefly slows you and blurs the screen on the head-rush)
- Intoxication meter only ticks up **+0.5%** per smoke — you would need to smoke well over a hundred times in a row to reach overdose territory, so weed practically **won't OD you**.

Gotchas: the sway buff **won't apply** if you have **revive fatigue**, or if a weed sway-buff is **already active** (wait for it to wear off — re-smoking early is wasted). Bong/rig can "fall out of your hands and break" if the progress bar is interrupted, wasting the flower.

### Cocaine, Meth & Crank — the speed buffs

These three are the "go fast" drugs. Each gives the same core buff — **+15% movement/animation speed** — but they hammer your intoxication and addiction meters at very different rates.

- **Cocaine** (`cocainep`) — "You feel unstoppable!" **+15% speed for 6 minutes.** Intoxication **+15%**, addiction **+10%**.
- **Meth** (`meth`) — "You feel incredible!" **+15% speed for ~4.5 minutes**, plus a color-inversion/chromatic screen effect for ~4 min. Intoxication **+20%**, addiction **+30%** (addicts you *fast*).
- **Crank** (`crankp`) — "You feel incredible!" **+15% speed for ~4.5 minutes**, same trippy screen effect. Intoxication **+40%** and addiction **+50%** per hit — **this is the most dangerous drug to use.** Two or three cranks back-to-back and you're in overdose range and heavily addicted.

Important: these speed buffs are **absolute, not stacking.** Cocaine, meth, crank and Red Gull all set your speed to the **same +15%** — taking a second one doesn't make you faster, and whichever one **expires last resets you to normal speed**, potentially cancelling another buff that was still supposed to be running. Don't bother chaining them for more speed; you only get the shared +15%.

### Heroin & LSD — the trips

These are mostly **visual/roleplay trips** with **no combat buff** — and they still build addiction and overdose risk, so there's little practical reason to use them in a fight.

- **Heroin** (`heroinp`) — "You feel terrific!" A warm reddish color-wash + chromatic effect for **~4 minutes**. Intoxication **+10%**, addiction **+10%**. No speed or aim benefit (verify — no stat buff is applied in code).
- **LSD** (`lsd_0` / `lsd_1`) — Full psychedelic freak-out: heavy screen distortion, **butterflies**, and it **plays trippy music** for about **~1 minute**. Intoxication only **+2%**, but addiction **+15%** — and LSD shares the **same addiction bar as cocaine**, so mixing coke and acid stacks that one bar quickly.

### PCP

**PCP** (`pcp`) — "You can now run farther." It maxes out and disables your fatigue so you can sprint indefinitely for about **5–6 minutes** (the message says 6 min; the timer runs ~5 min). Notably PCP does **not** add to your intoxication meter and does **not** build addiction — it's outside the overdose/addiction system entirely. **Heads up:** stamina is already removed server-wide (everyone has effectively infinite sprint), so PCP's "run farther" benefit is **largely redundant now** — it's mostly a leftover.

### Alcohol — beer, moonshine, scotch & rum

Booze is its **own** system — separate meter, **no** intoxication/overdose risk, **no** addiction. Drinkable items: **beer, moonshine, scotch (`scotch_1/2/3`), rum (`rum_0/1/2/3`)**.

Each drink:
- Raises your **BAC**: **beer +10%**, everything else **+15%**.
- Restores **+10 thirst**.
- Blurs your screen (radial blur + chromatic aberration) that gets **worse the drunker you are**, wearing off over **~3 minutes** (BAC drops ~10% each cycle).
- **Massively increases your weapon sway** the drunker you get — this is a **downside for shooting**. Being drunk is the opposite of smoking weed: weed tightens your aim, booze ruins it.

So alcohol is a roleplay / thirst item, not a combat buff — and a cop who searches you can read your BAC (see below).

### The intoxication meter & overdosing

Every hard drug adds to your hidden intoxication meter (weed +0.5%, LSD +2%, heroin +10%, cocaine +15%, meth +20%, **crank +40%**). It drains slowly on its own over time.

**Overdose only becomes possible once your intoxication is above 90%.** From that point, **every additional hard-drug hit** rolls the dice:
- **~40% chance** to trigger an **overdose episode** — "You have overdosed!" Your **controls lock up (you can't move or act)** and you spasm on the ground for a random **up to ~40 seconds**.
- At the end of the episode, there's a **~30% chance it kills you** outright — "You have died of a drug overdose," instant death. Otherwise you survive (a little nod) and your intoxication drops sharply.

So the danger isn't one drug — it's **stacking hits while already near the top of the meter.** Crank is the fast track: a couple of cranks in a row can push you past 90% and into kill-roll range. Weed and LSD barely move the meter, so they're very unlikely to OD you on their own. **Alcohol and PCP can't overdose you at all** (they don't touch this meter).

If you're deliberately buffing before a fight, take your speed hit *early* and let the meter drain before re-dosing — don't slam a second crank when you're already lit up.

### Addiction & withdrawal

Every hard drug also fills one of **five addiction bars** by drug family:

| Drug | Addiction bar | Per-hit addiction | Intoxication per hit |
|------|--------------|-------------------|----------------------|
| Cannabis | 1 (weed) | +3% | +0.5% |
| Cocaine | 2 (shared w/ LSD) | +10% | +15% |
| LSD | 2 (shared w/ cocaine) | +15% | +2% |
| Heroin | 3 | +10% | +10% |
| Meth | 4 | +30% | +20% |
| Crank | 5 | +50% | +40% |
| PCP | none | — | — |
| Alcohol | none | — | — |

Once a bar gets high **and it's been ~10 minutes since your last hit of that drug**, you start getting cravings, escalating with the bar:
- Above ~40%: *"You are craving a high."*
- Above ~60%: *"Getting high occupies all of your thoughts."*
- Above ~90%: **full withdrawal** — *"You feel shakey and anxious! You need a fix!"* and your **screen/camera starts shaking** repeatedly, which trashes your aim and driving.

**Taking another hit of that drug immediately clears the withdrawal shakes** (that's the "fix"). Addiction **persists across relogs** (it's saved to your profile, separately for civ / cop / TT). It also eases slowly on its own over time, but the **only reliable cure is rehab**.

### Rehab — kicking the habit

To fully wipe your addiction and withdrawal, go to a **Clinic or Hospital** and use the **"Check in to Rehab"** scroll action on the medic NPC.

- You must **actually have an addiction** or it'll tell you *"You do not have a drug addiction!"*
- **Cost is based on your worst addiction bar** (paid by **debit/bank card**):
  - **Trivial** (barely addicted): **$10 flat**
  - **Minor** (≥10%): ~**$1,000–3,000**
  - **Moderate** (≥30%): ~**$3,000–5,000**
  - **Substantial** (≥50%): ~**$5,000–7,000**
  - **Major** (≥70%): ~**$7,000–9,000**
  - **Extreme** (≥90%): ~**$9,000–10,000**
  - (Roughly **$100 per 1% of addiction**.)
- You get a brief **blackout** (a few seconds up to ~10s the more addicted you are), then wake up **fully clean** — all addiction bars, intoxication and withdrawal reset to zero. *"You've kicked the addiction and feel healthy again!"*

Because meth and crank addict you so fast (and rehab is priced by your worst bar), a heavy crank habit is genuinely expensive to clean up — budget for it.

### Cops can tell

When a cop **searches** you, your intoxication and BAC are shown right on their search readout, so you can't hide being lit:

- **Drug level** shows as: *"Seems to have a buzz"* → *"moderately intoxicated"* → *"very paranoid and jittery"* → *"stoned out of his mind"* → *"tripping massive balls."*
- **Alcohol** shows as a fake **BAC** line: *"BAC > 0.03… unable to concentrate"* all the way up to *"BAC > 0.25; Blacked Out intoxicated."*

Separately, if you've handled drugs recently your **cash carries "traces of narcotics"** (the drug-runner flag) and a searching cop can **seize all your on-hand cash** — but that's the drug-*dealing* trace, not the being-high effect. Bank your money before you go near a cop.

### Gotchas & tips

- **Weed is the only real combat buff** among the drugs (tighter aim + no pain) and it's the safest — near-zero OD risk. The dab rig gives the longest buff (6 min).
- **The vape is a trap** — no sway buff, just a slow-down gag. Roll a joint or pack a bong instead.
- **Speed buffs don't stack.** Cocaine/meth/crank/Red Gull all give the same +15%; a second one is wasted and the *last to expire* drops you back to normal, possibly ending another buff early.
- **Crank is the overdose king** — +40% intoxication per hit. Don't double up when you're already high or you're rolling the death dice.
- **Overdose freezes your controls for up to ~40s** before the kill roll — a brutal time to get caught in a firefight. Don't dose mid-combat if your meter's already high.
- **Addiction follows you through relogs** — you can't relog off the shakes. Only another hit or rehab clears it.
- **Meth/crank addict you far faster than they intoxicate you** — you'll be hooked long before you're at OD risk. Factor the rehab bill into your profit.
- **Alcohol ruins your aim** the drunker you get — don't drink before a gunfight; do smoke weed.
- **Heroin, LSD and PCP have no fighting benefit** — use them for roleplay/visuals, knowing heroin and LSD still cost you addiction and OD risk (PCP doesn't).

---

## Rehab (curing drug addiction)

### What it is
Every time you use a recreational drug you build up an **addiction**. Once your addiction gets high enough you start suffering **withdrawal** — screen shakes, "You feel shakey and anxious! You need a fix!" spam, cravings — and you risk a fatal **overdose** if you keep using. **Rehab** is the instant cure: you pay a medic to clean you up, and it wipes *all* of your addictions back to zero, kills the withdrawal shakes, and removes the overdose risk.

You don't have to use rehab — addiction also fades slowly on its own if you just stop using (see Gotchas). Rehab is the "pay money, be cured right now" button.

Your addiction level is **saved to your character** and survives relogs and server restarts, so it doesn't reset just because you disconnect.

### Where
Talk to the **medic / paramedic NPC standing inside any hospital or TT clinic**. It's the same NPC that offers "Medical Assistance" and the ATM.

- Scroll on the medic and pick **"Check in to Rehab."**
- It's available to **everyone** — civilians, cops, and Trauma Team (medics).
- The scroll option sits at the **bottom of the scroll list** (lowest priority), so scroll down past ATM / Medical Assistance / shop options to find it.

Hospitals/clinics are placed in the usual town medical buildings across the map (Kavala, Pyrgos, Sofia, Athira, etc. — check any hospital icon on your map) (verify exact town list).

### Requirements
- **You must actually have an addiction.** If you're clean, the medic just says *"You do not have a drug addiction!"* and nothing happens.
- **Money** — see the price table below. No license, item, talent, or rank is required to use rehab itself.
- Payment is pulled **from cash on hand first.** If you don't have enough cash, it will pull from your **bank account — but only if you're carrying a Debit Card item.** If you have neither enough cash nor a debit card, the payment fails and you stay addicted.

### How much it costs
The price is based on your **single worst addiction** (whichever drug you're most hooked on), on a 0–1 scale. The formula is roughly **your worst addiction × $10,000.**

| Severity shown | Your worst addiction | You pay |
|---|---|---|
| Extreme | 0.90 – 1.00 | ~$9,000 – $10,000 |
| Major | 0.70 – 0.89 | ~$7,000 – $8,900 |
| Substantial | 0.50 – 0.69 | ~$5,000 – $6,900 |
| Moderate | 0.30 – 0.49 | ~$3,000 – $4,900 |
| Minor | 0.10 – 0.29 | ~$1,000 – $2,900 |
| Trivial | below 0.10 | **$10 flat** |

- **Maximum possible cost: $10,000** (fully maxed-out addiction).
- The exact dollar amount is shown to you in the confirmation popup *before* you pay, so you're never charged blind.

### Step-by-step
1. Go to any **hospital or clinic** and look at the **medic/paramedic NPC**.
2. Scroll and select **"Check in to Rehab."**
3. A popup tells you your addiction severity (e.g. *"You have a major drug addiction!"*) and the **exact price**. Click **Yes** to proceed, **No** to back out for free.
4. The cost is deducted (cash first, then bank via debit card). You'll get a chat line confirming *"You paid $X from cash on hand"* (or from your bank).
5. Your **screen blacks out** for a few seconds — *"Time passes slowly..."* — while you dry out. You're checked into the facility behind the scenes and returned to the exact spot you were standing.
6. You get the message **"You've kicked the addiction and feel healthy again! See you soon."** All your addictions are now **0**, withdrawal is gone, and cravings stop.

### Timers
- The blackout is short: a **3-second fade to black**, then a stay that scales with how addicted you were — up to about **10 extra seconds** at maximum addiction (much shorter if you were only lightly hooked), then a ~1-second fade back in.
- **Worst case (maxed addiction) is roughly ~14 seconds** start to finish; a minor addiction clears in just a few seconds.
- You **cannot cancel or move during the blackout** — once you've paid, you're locked in until it finishes. Do it somewhere safe.

### Gotchas
- **It clears EVERYTHING for one price.** You're charged based on your single worst addiction, but rehab wipes *all* drugs at once. So if you're extreme on meth and minor on weed, you pay the meth price and both are cured. Batch your bad habits into one visit.
- **Harder drugs get expensive fast.** Addiction per use: cannabis is the mildest, then cocaine / heroin, then LSD, and **meth and especially crank spike your addiction the fastest** — a couple of crank hits can jump you into "major/extreme" territory and a $7,000–$10,000 rehab bill.
- **You can quit for free if you're patient.** Addiction slowly decays on its own when you stop using (a small amount every few minutes). If you can tough out the withdrawal shakes and cravings and just don't touch drugs, it fades to zero eventually at no cost. Rehab is the pay-to-skip-the-suffering option.
- **Overdose is the real danger rehab removes.** If your addiction is very high (above ~0.9) and you use *another* drug, there's roughly a **40% chance to overdose** — up to ~20+ seconds of uncontrollable spasms with your controls disabled, and about a **30% chance it kills you outright.** Clearing your addiction in rehab removes this risk entirely (verify exact odds).
- **Withdrawal is a combat liability.** At high addiction you get periodic **camera shake** and nag messages every few seconds — miserable in a firefight. Rehab stops it instantly.
- **Bring money or a debit card.** No cash and no debit card = the transaction silently fails and you're still addicted. If you plan to pay from the bank, make sure you're carrying a **Debit Card** item.
- **Trivial addictions are basically free** ($10), so if you're only very lightly hooked there's no reason not to just clear it.
